<!-- FORMAT:usage-context-v1 -->

<!-- WORD:W001 -->
# adapt

**词源**：经法语来自拉丁语 adaptare，原意是“调整到合适”：ad- 表示朝向，aptare 表示使合适。可以用“使自己或事物适合新条件”串起适应、改制与改编；adaptation 是相应的名词形式。

<!-- USE:U00101 -->
## adapt /əˈdæpt/ v.
`改变自身的想法或行为，使自己适应新的环境或条件。通常用 adapt to something；强调调整自己时也可用 adapt oneself to something。这里的 to 是介词，后接名词或动名词；所适应的对象明确时，adapt 也可以单独使用。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00101r USE:U00101 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"国家公园如何调整游客管理","sourceUrls":["https://www.usgs.gov/publications/adapting-visitor-use-management-under-a-changing-climate-across-us-national-park"],"displayContext":"USGS研究摘要，2025年7月；63名工作人员、31个公园单位，摘要缩编，未读期刊全文。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00101r1 USE:U00101 -->
A 2025 study of 31 U.S. national park units found staff taking action to adapt to climate-related changes in how visitors use the parks.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00101r2 -->
Responses included revised programmes and better communication to support visitor safety.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00101r -->
一项2025年、涉及美国31个国家公园单位的研究发现，工作人员正在采取行动，适应气候相关的游客游览变化。他们的应对措施包括调整活动安排、加强沟通以保障游客安全。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00101r -->
<!-- USE:U00102 -->
## adapt /əˈdæpt/ v.
`调整、修改某物，使它适合另一对象、用途或情况。adapt something to something 强调与条件相适应，adapt something for a purpose 强调新用途；可调整设备、场所、办法或文本，包括修改文本本身。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00102a USE:U00102 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00102a1 USE:U00102 -->
Shelves were built to adapt the library for use as an office.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00102a -->
人们搭建了架子，把图书室改成适合办公的地方。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00102a -->
<!-- USE:U00103 -->
## adapt /əˈdæpt/ v.
`把作品改写或编排成适合另一种媒介的形式。adapt a novel for the screen 表示把小说改编成电影；be adapted from a play 表示由剧本改编而来，也可涉及广播、电视等形式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00103r USE:U00103 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"《欢乐之家》的改编与人物抉择","sourceUrls":["https://www.bfi.org.uk/bfi-film-releases/house-mirth"],"displayContext":"BFI影片简介，作品英国2000年；片介缩编，未观看影片，不据网页未注明年份的排期推断重映年份。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00103r1 USE:U00103 -->
Terence Davies' The House of Mirth, adapted from Edith Wharton's novel, follows a young woman seeking a good marriage.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00103r2 -->
Her honour and love for another prevent her from accepting a wealthy banker's advances.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00103r -->
特伦斯·戴维斯的《欢乐之家》改编自伊迪丝·华顿的小说，讲述一位希望缔结美好婚姻的年轻女性。她的荣誉感及对另一人的爱，使她无法接受一位富有银行家的追求。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00103r -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00103a USE:U00103 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[],"displayContext":"固定教材原例，展示主动改编与for目的结构。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00103a1 USE:U00103 -->
The scriptwriter helped him to adapt his novel for the screen.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00103a -->
编剧帮助他把小说改编成电影。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00103a -->
<!-- USE:U00104 -->
## adapted /əˈdæptɪd/ adj.
`适合某一环境或用途的，常用 be well adapted to/for。既可描述人工改造后的适配状态，也可描述生物适应环境的特征。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00104a USE:U00104 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00104a1 USE:U00104 -->
The camel's feet, well adapted for dry sand, are useless on mud.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00104a -->
骆驼的脚很适合干沙地，到了泥地上却派不上用场。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00104a -->
<!-- USE:U00105 -->
## adaptable /əˈdæptəbəl/ adj.
`能够调整自身以适应新环境或条件的，可形容人、动物等的适应能力。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00105a USE:U00105 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00105a1 USE:U00105 -->
The company needs a more adaptable and skilled workforce.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00105a -->
这家公司需要一支适应能力更强、技能更熟练的员工队伍。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00105a -->
<!-- USE:U00106 -->
## adaptable /əˈdæptəbəl/ adj.
`能够被修改、改制或改编以适合另一用途的；强调对象可被调整，而非它自己改变行为。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00106r USE:U00106 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"选择能够调整的保存方案","sourceUrls":["https://www.archives.govt.nz/manage-information/how-to-manage-your-information/digital/best-practice-guidance-on-digital-storage-and-preservation"],"displayContext":"Archives New Zealand数字保存指南，Flexibility条件缩编；行政与技术范围限原指南。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00106r1 USE:U00106 -->
Look for an adaptable storage solution that can respond to changing preservation requirements.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00106r2 -->
Its components should be easy to integrate with other systems and applications.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00106r -->
应选择能够随保存要求变化而改配的存储方案。它的组件应当容易与其他系统和应用程序整合。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00106r -->
<!-- USE:U00107 -->
## adaptability /əˌdæptəˈbɪləti/ n.
`适应变化或适合改用的能力，通常不可数。既可指人的适应能力，也可指羊毛等材料适应不同用途的能力，常接 of 说明具有这种能力的对象。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00107a USE:U00107 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00107a1 USE:U00107 -->
The adaptability of wool is one of its great attractions.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00107a -->
羊毛能适应多种用途，是它的一大吸引力。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00107a -->
<!-- USE:U00108 -->
## adaptably /əˈdæptəbli/ adv.
`以能够适应变化的方式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00108a USE:U00108 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00108a1 USE:U00108 -->
She responded adaptably when the timetable changed.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00108a -->
时间表变动时，她灵活调整了应对方式。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00108a -->
<!-- USE:U00109 -->
## adaptation /ˌædæpˈteɪʃən/ n.
`为适应新用途或条件而改变行为或事物的过程；表示这一过程时通常不可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00109r USE:U00109 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"调整工作为何会遇到障碍","sourceUrls":["https://www.usgs.gov/publications/adapting-visitor-use-management-under-a-changing-climate-across-us-national-park"],"displayContext":"USGS研究摘要，2025年7月；所述31个公园单位访谈结果，非所有公园的普遍断言。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00109r1 -->
Park staff were already changing visitor management as the climate changed.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00109r2 USE:U00109 -->
But climate adaptation could be held back by funding and staffing limits, changing priorities, and uncertainty about future conditions.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00109r -->
随着气候变化，公园工作人员已在调整游客管理。但资金与人员限制、优先事项的变化以及未来状况的不确定性，都可能妨碍气候适应工作。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00109r -->
<!-- USE:U00110 -->
## adaptation /ˌædæpˈteɪʃən/ n.
`经过改编或改制而形成的作品或事物，可数；a screen adaptation of a novel 指小说的电影改编作品。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00110a USE:U00110 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00110a1 USE:U00110 -->
We watched Branagh's screen adaptation of Shakespeare's Henry the Fifth.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00110a -->
我们观看了布拉纳改编自莎士比亚《亨利五世》的电影。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00110a -->
<!-- USE:U00111 -->
## adaptation /ˌædæpˈteɪʃən/ n.
`生物学中，生物或物种逐渐变得适合其环境的过程。讨论长期适应时，应与个体有意识地调整行为区分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00111r USE:U00111 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"适应产生抗性之后，换一种预防思路","sourceUrls":["https://openstax.org/books/microbiology/pages/9-1-how-microbes-grow"],"displayContext":"OpenStax《Microbiology》§9.1引用2023年研究的说明，缩编与中译，CC BY-NC-SA4.0；未独立复验该研究。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00111r1 USE:U00111 -->
Rapid microbial adaptation can produce resistant strains.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00111r2 -->
To prevent biofilms in space, researchers tried making surfaces slippery rather than killing the organisms, so that the microbes could not attach.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00111r -->
微生物的快速适应可能产生抗性菌株。为了防止太空环境中的生物膜，研究者尝试把表面变得润滑，使微生物无法附着，而不是将它们杀死。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00111r -->
<!-- USE:U00112 -->
## adaptation /ˌædæpˈteɪʃən/ n.
`生物学中，感觉系统针对持续刺激或环境变化作出的适应性调节，例如视觉对明暗变化的适应。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00112r USE:U00112 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"比较两只眼的明暗适应","sourceUrls":["https://www.cns.nyu.edu/~david/courses/perception/demos/demo-adaptation.html"],"displayContext":"纽约大学视觉课程演示，原步骤缩编；原文建议遮眼五至十分钟，本段取其最低时长。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00112r1 -->
In a reasonably bright room, cover one eye tightly for at least five minutes.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00112r2 USE:U00112 -->
Then dim the room, uncover the eye, and open your eyes one at a time to compare their sensitivity: this demonstrates dark adaptation.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00112r -->
在较明亮的房间里，严密遮住一只眼至少五分钟。然后调暗房间，移开遮挡，轮流睁开两只眼比较敏感度；这演示的就是暗适应。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00112r -->
<!-- USE:U00113 -->
## adaptive /əˈdæptɪv/ adj.
`有能力或倾向随情况作出适应性调整的，属于正式用语，可形容行为、规则、系统等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00113a USE:U00113 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00113a1 USE:U00113 -->
Societies need to develop highly adaptive behavioural rules for survival.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00113a -->
社会需要发展出适应能力很强的行为规则，才能生存。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00113a -->

<!-- WORD:W002 -->
# advocate

**词源**：来自拉丁语 advocatus，原是“被叫到身边来的人”，指证人、顾问等；ad- 表示到身边，vocare 表示呼唤。由替人发言、提供支持，容易联想到辩护与公开倡导；英语动词后来由名词形成。

<!-- USE:U00201 -->
## advocate /ˈædvəkeɪt/ v.
`公开主张、推荐或以论据支持某项行动、政策或事业，属于正式用语。直接接所主张的内容，如 advocate a policy、advocate doing something；词尾读 /keɪt/，与名词读音不同。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00201a USE:U00201 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00201a1 USE:U00201 -->
Mr Williams is a conservative who advocates fewer government controls on business.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00201a -->
威廉斯先生是一位保守派，主张减少政府对企业的管制。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00201a -->
<!-- USE:U00202 -->
## advocate /ˈædvəkeɪt/ v.
`为某人或某一立场进行申辩、提出支持理由，直接接所维护的对象。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00202a USE:U00202 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00202a1 USE:U00202 -->
The representative advocated the defendant's position before the panel.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00202a -->
这位代表在审议小组面前为被告的立场作了辩护。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00202a -->
<!-- USE:U00203 -->
## advocate /ˈædvəkət/ n.
`公开支持某项政策、行动或事业的人，可数，属于正式用语，常接 of。词尾弱读 /kət/，与动词读音不同。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00203a USE:U00203 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00203a1 USE:U00203 -->
He was a strong advocate of free market policies and a multi-party system.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00203a -->
他曾是自由市场政策和多党制的坚定倡导者。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00203a -->
<!-- USE:U00204 -->
## advocate /ˈædvəkət/ n.
`替别人发声、维护某一群体利益的权益代表，常用 an advocate for somebody。这一用法尤其见于美式英语，所指的人不必是律师。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00204a USE:U00204 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00204a1 USE:U00204 -->
Advocates for the charity spoke on behalf of the families.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00204a -->
该慈善机构的权益代表替这些家庭发声。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00204a -->
<!-- USE:U00205 -->
## advocate /ˈædvəkət/ n.
`法律上，在法庭替人陈述、辩护的专业人士；在苏格兰也特指出庭律师，具体执业称谓依司法辖区而定。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00205a USE:U00205 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00205a1 USE:U00205 -->
She instructed an advocate to represent her in the Scottish court.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00205a -->
她委托了一名出庭律师，在苏格兰法院代表她。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00205a -->
<!-- USE:U00206 -->
## advocateship /ˈædvəkətʃɪp/ n.
`担任辩护人或律师的身份、职务。由名词 advocate 加 -ship 构成，重在所处身份。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00206a USE:U00206 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00206a1 USE:U00206 -->
The document records his admission to advocateship.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00206a -->
这份文件记载了他取得律师身份一事。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00206a -->
<!-- USE:U00207 -->
## advocacy /ˈædvəkəsi/ n.
`为某项事业、政策或行动公开提出支持意见、进行论证的行为，属于正式用语。常接 of；说某人的某项倡导时可用所有格及单数形式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00207a USE:U00207 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00207a1 USE:U00207 -->
I support your advocacy of free trade.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00207a -->
我支持你对自由贸易的倡导。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00207a -->
<!-- USE:U00208 -->
## advocacy /ˈædvəkəsi/ n.
`律师替当事人陈述、论辩的工作、职能或方式，属于正式用语，通常不可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00208a USE:U00208 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00208a1 USE:U00208 -->
Sir Peter would also like to see the current adversarial system of advocacy examined by the royal commission.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00208a -->
彼得爵士也希望皇家委员会审查现行的对抗式法庭辩护制度。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00208a -->
<!-- USE:U00209 -->
## advocacy /ˈædvəkəsi/ n.
`在美式英语的 advocacy group、advocacy organization 等组合中作定语，说明组织以影响政府或其他机构决策为目标；advocacy 本身仍是名词。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00209a USE:U00209 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00209a1 USE:U00209 -->
An advocacy group asked the council to improve wheelchair access.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00209a -->
一个倡议组织要求市议会改善轮椅通行条件。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00209a -->
<!-- USE:U00210 -->
## devil's advocate /ˌdevəlz ˈædvəkət/ n.
`为检验论点或推动讨论而故意提出反面意见的人，常说 play devil's advocate；本人未必赞成所提出的反面立场。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00210a USE:U00210 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00210a1 USE:U00210 -->
Let me play devil's advocate and ask what would happen if demand fell.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00210a -->
让我暂时站在反方，问一问需求下降会怎样。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00210a -->
<!-- USE:U00211 -->
## devil's advocate /ˌdevəlz ˈædvəkət/ n.
`天主教历史上，在列福、列圣程序中受命对候选资格提出质疑的职务，须与当代讨论中故意站在反方的比喻义区分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00211a USE:U00211 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00211a1 USE:U00211 -->
In the historical canonization procedure, the devil's advocate examined objections to the candidate's case.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00211a -->
在历史上的列圣程序中，列圣审查员审查对候选人资格提出的异议。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00211a -->

<!-- WORD:W003 -->
# allocate

**词源**：来自中世纪拉丁语 allocare，含“把某物放到指定位置”的意思：al- 是 ad- 在 l 前的形式，locare 表示放置。由“指定归处”理解分配给某人或划作某一用途。

<!-- USE:U00301 -->
## allocate /ˈæləkeɪt/ v.
`把资源或人员分给特定对象、地点或用途。allocate something to somebody/a place 指分给某对象，allocate something for a purpose 指划作某用途；allocate somebody to do something 指派人承担任务，也可用 allocate somebody something。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00301a USE:U00301 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00301a1 USE:U00301 -->
Tickets are limited and will be allocated to those who apply first.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00301a -->
票数有限，将分配给先申请的人。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00301a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00301b USE:U00301 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00301b1 USE:U00301 -->
Our plan is to allocate one member of staff to handle appointments.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00301b -->
我们的计划是指派一名工作人员处理预约。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00301b -->
<!-- USE:U00302 -->
## allocation /ˌæləˈkeɪʃən/ n.
`分配给某人或某一用途的一份资源，可数，尤其指资金份额，可接 for 或 to。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00302a USE:U00302 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00302a1 USE:U00302 -->
There will be a closer review of funding allocations for future conferences.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00302a -->
今后会议的拨款份额将受到更仔细的审查。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00302a -->
<!-- USE:U00303 -->
## allocation /ˌæləˈkeɪʃən/ n.
`决定把资源分给哪些对象或用途的行为、过程；表示过程时通常不可数，常用 allocation of something。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00303a USE:U00303 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00303a1 USE:U00303 -->
Town planning and land allocation had to be coordinated.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00303a -->
城镇规划和土地分配必须相互协调。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00303a -->
<!-- USE:U00304 -->
## allocable /ˈæləkəbəl/ adj.
`可以分配、划拨给某个对象或归入特定用途的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00304a USE:U00304 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00304a1 USE:U00304 -->
Only the uncommitted funds are allocable to the new project.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00304a -->
只有尚未承诺用途的资金可以拨给这个新项目。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00304a -->
<!-- USE:U00305 -->
## allocator /ˈæləkeɪtər/ n.
`负责分配的人或执行分配功能的事物，可数；可指人员，也可指承担资源分配任务的系统部件。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00305a USE:U00305 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00305a1 USE:U00305 -->
The memory allocator assigns space to each running program.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00305a -->
内存分配器为每个正在运行的程序分配空间。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00305a -->

<!-- WORD:W004 -->
# alternative

**词源**：这组词最终联系到拉丁语 alter，表示“两者中的另一个”。alternate 保留“轮流用这一个、再用另一个”的线索；alternative 经中世纪拉丁语发展出“可供另选的”含义。用“另一个”帮助记忆，但须区分交替与替代。

<!-- USE:U00401 -->
## alternative /ɔlˈtɝnətɪv/ n.
`能够代替另一事物而被采用的一种选择，可数，常用 an alternative to something；可供选择的可能性可以有两个或更多。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00401a USE:U00401 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00401a1 USE:U00401 -->
New ways to treat arthritis may provide an alternative to painkillers.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00401a -->
治疗关节炎的新方法可能提供一种替代止痛药的选择。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00401a -->
<!-- USE:U00402 -->
## alternative /ɔlˈtɝnətɪv/ n.
`在若干可能做法之间作选择的自由或余地。have no alternative but to do something 表示别无选择，只能这样做。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00402a USE:U00402 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00402a1 USE:U00402 -->
I had no alternative but to go.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00402a -->
我别无选择，只能前去。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00402a -->
<!-- USE:U00403 -->
## alternative /ɔlˈtɝnətɪv/ adj.
`有别于已有方案、可代替它使用的，通常置于名词前，如 alternative route、alternative method。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00403a USE:U00403 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00403a1 USE:U00403 -->
There were alternative methods of travel available.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00403a -->
当时还有其他可供选择的出行方式。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00403a -->
<!-- USE:U00404 -->
## alternative /ɔlˈtɝnətɪv/ adj.
`两种事物相互排斥、不能同时采用而需要择一的；强调互斥关系，不只是两者不同。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00404a USE:U00404 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00404a1 USE:U00404 -->
The two arrangements are alternative, so accepting one rules out the other.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00404a -->
这两种安排相互排斥，因此接受一种就排除了另一种。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00404a -->
<!-- USE:U00405 -->
## alternative /ɔlˈtɝnətɪv/ adj.
`提供有别于社会惯常做法的生活、文化或实践方式的，常置于名词前，如 alternative lifestyle、alternative theatre。是否属于非主流取决于具体社会与时代。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00405a USE:U00405 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00405a1 USE:U00405 -->
The story follows unconventional parents who embraced the alternative lifestyle of the Sixties.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00405a -->
故事讲述了一对不循常规、接受了六十年代非主流生活方式的父母。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00405a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00405b USE:U00405 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00405b1 USE:U00405 -->
The festival included alternative theatre.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00405b -->
这个节日的活动包括非主流戏剧。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00405b -->
<!-- USE:U00406 -->
## alternative /ɔlˈtɝnətɪv/ adj.
`在 alternative medicine 等组合中，表示用植物药、按摩、针灸等非常规方法替代常规医疗的；这一类别名称本身不证明某种疗法的疗效。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00406r USE:U00406 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"代替，还是配合使用","sourceUrls":["https://www.nccih.nih.gov/health/complementary-alternative-or-integrative-health-whats-in-a-name"],"displayContext":"NCCIH术语说明缩编；只是术语辨别，不表示某疗法已被证明有效。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00406r1 USE:U00406 -->
When a non-mainstream approach is used instead of conventional medicine, NIH calls it alternative.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00406r2 -->
Using it together with conventional medicine makes it complementary instead.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00406r -->
当一种非主流方法被用来代替常规医疗时，美国国立卫生研究院称它为替代疗法。如果它是与常规医疗共同使用，则称为补充疗法。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00406r -->
<!-- USE:U00407 -->
## alternative /ɔlˈtɝnətɪv/ adj.
`在 alternative energy 等组合中，表示使用太阳、风、水等自然来源而非石油、煤或核能的；这里限定特定能源类别，不泛指任意另一种能源。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00407a USE:U00407 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00407a1 USE:U00407 -->
Wind power is one form of alternative energy.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00407a -->
风力发电是替代能源的一种。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00407a -->
<!-- USE:U00408 -->
## alternatively /ɔlˈtɝnətɪvli/ adv.
`用于引出另一项建议或不同选择，相当于“或者，也可以采用另一种做法”。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00408a USE:U00408 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00408a1 -->
Allow about eight hours for the drive from Calais.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00408a2 USE:U00408 -->
Alternatively, you can fly to Brive.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00408a -->
从加来开车要预留大约八小时。或者，你可以乘飞机去布里夫。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00408a -->
<!-- USE:U00409 -->
## alternate /ˈɔltərneɪt/ v.
`两种事物轮流出现，或在两种状态之间反复变化。不及物，常用 alternate with something、alternate between A and B。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00409a USE:U00409 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00409a1 USE:U00409 -->
Her aggressive moods alternated with gentle or more co-operative states.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00409a -->
她的情绪时而咄咄逼人，时而温和或更愿意合作。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00409a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00409b USE:U00409 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00409b1 USE:U00409 -->
The patient alternated between hot and cold fevers.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00409b -->
病人一会儿发热，一会儿发冷。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00409b -->
<!-- USE:U00410 -->
## alternate /ˈɔltərneɪt/ v.
`使两种事物按先后轮流出现。直接接所安排的事物，可用 alternate A and B 或 alternate A with B。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00410a USE:U00410 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00410a1 USE:U00410 -->
Now you just alternate layers of that mixture and eggplant.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00410a -->
现在只需把那种混合物和茄子一层一层交替铺好。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00410a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00410b USE:U00410 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00410b1 USE:U00410 -->
The band alternated romantic love songs with bouncy dance numbers.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00410b -->
乐队交替演奏浪漫情歌和节奏欢快的舞曲。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00410b -->
<!-- USE:U00411 -->
## alternate /ˈɔltərnət/ adj.
`两类事物一个接着另一个交替出现的，也可形容由交替项目组成的序列，如相间的色带；词尾读 /nət/，与动词不同。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00411a USE:U00411 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00411a1 USE:U00411 -->
They were streaked with alternate bands of colour.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00411a -->
它们带有不同颜色相间的条纹。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00411a -->
<!-- USE:U00412 -->
## alternate /ˈɔltərnət/ adj.
`在一连串单位中隔一个出现一次的，通常修饰复数名词。on alternate days 是隔一天一次，alternate years 是隔年。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00412a USE:U00412 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00412a1 USE:U00412 -->
Lesley had agreed to Jim going skiing in alternate years.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00412a -->
莱斯利已经同意吉姆隔年去滑雪。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00412a -->
<!-- USE:U00413 -->
## alternate /ˈɔltərnət/ adj.
`能够替代原方案、事物或惯常方式的，尤其见于北美英语，相当于 alternative，可修饰路线、生活方式等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00413a USE:U00413 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00413a1 USE:U00413 -->
His group was forced to turn back and take an alternate route.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00413a -->
他所在的小组被迫折返，改走另一条路线。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00413a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00413b USE:U00413 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00413b1 USE:U00413 -->
They chose an alternate lifestyle.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00413b -->
他们选择了另一种有别于惯常方式的生活。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00413b -->
<!-- USE:U00414 -->
## alternate /ˈɔltərnət/ n.
`需要时接替别人或代替另一事物的人或物，可数，尤其见于北美英语，如候补陪审员。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00414a USE:U00414 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00414a1 USE:U00414 -->
In most jurisdictions, twelve jurors and two alternates are chosen.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00414a -->
在大多数司法辖区，会选出十二名陪审员和两名候补人员。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00414a -->
<!-- USE:U00415 -->
## alternate /ˈɔltərnət/ adj.
`植物学中，叶等在茎的两侧交替排列、并非成对对生的，即“互生的”。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00415a USE:U00415 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00415a1 USE:U00415 -->
The plant has alternate leaves along its stem.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00415a -->
这种植物的叶片沿着茎互生。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00415a -->
<!-- USE:U00416 -->
## alternately /ˈɔltərnətli/ adv.
`交替地、轮流地，说明两种状态或动作轮番出现；须与引出另一选择的 alternatively 区分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00416a USE:U00416 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00416a1 USE:U00416 -->
He could alternately bully and charm people.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00416a -->
他会一会儿欺负人，一会儿又讨人欢心。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00416a -->
<!-- USE:U00417 -->
## alternation /ˌɔltərˈneɪʃən/ n.
`交替的动作、过程或结果，可数或不可数，常用 alternation of A and B。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00417a USE:U00417 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00417a1 USE:U00417 -->
The alternation of sun and snow continued for the rest of our holiday.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00417a -->
在我们剩下的假期里，晴天和下雪仍然交替出现。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00417a -->

<!-- WORD:W005 -->
# anticipate

**词源**：来自拉丁语 anticipare，含“提前取得、先行处理”的意思；ante 表示在前，后半联系 capere“取得”。“在事情到来前先走一步”可帮助记住预料、预先应对和抢先。

<!-- USE:U00501 -->
## anticipate /ænˈtɪsəpeɪt/ v.
`预先意识到某事可能发生，或预料某种结果，有时也包含做好准备。通常接名词或 that 从句；单纯表示 expect 的用法曾有争议，但现代英语中也广泛使用，不宜一概判错。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00501a USE:U00501 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00501a1 USE:U00501 -->
At the time we couldn't have anticipated the result of our campaigning.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00501a -->
当时，我们不可能预料到这场倡议行动的结果。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00501a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00501b USE:U00501 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00501b1 USE:U00501 -->
I hadn't anticipated that Rob's team would advance that far.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00501b -->
我没有料到罗布的队伍会走得那么远。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00501b -->
<!-- USE:U00502 -->
## anticipate /ænˈtɪsəpeɪt/ v.
`在需求、问题或对方行动出现前，先采取所需措施。对象常是 a need、a question、an opponent's move，强调提前处理，不只是猜到。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00502a USE:U00502 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00502a1 USE:U00502 -->
What Jeff did was to anticipate my next question.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00502a -->
杰夫所做的，是在我提出下一个问题前先作回应。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00502a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00502b USE:U00502 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00502b1 USE:U00502 -->
He anticipated his opponent's moves.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00502b -->
他预先判断并应对了对手的招数。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00502b -->
<!-- USE:U00503 -->
## anticipate /ænˈtɪsəpeɪt/ v.
`比别人更早采取行动、提出想法或说出某事，也可指较早作品先行触及后来的艺术或思想方向。be anticipated by somebody 表示被别人抢先。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00503a USE:U00503 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00503a1 USE:U00503 -->
In the 50s, Rauschenberg anticipated the conceptual art movement of the 80s.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00503a -->
在五十年代，劳申伯格就已先行触及八十年代观念艺术运动所发展的方向。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00503a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00503b USE:U00503 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00503b1 USE:U00503 -->
He was about to announce the discovery but was anticipated by his pupil.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00503b -->
他正要公开这一发现，却被自己的学生抢先了。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00503b -->
<!-- USE:U00504 -->
## anticipate /ænˈtɪsəpeɪt/ v.
`怀着盼望等待将要发生的事情，可用 with pleasure 等说明期待时的感受。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00504a USE:U00504 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00504a1 USE:U00504 -->
I anticipated the interview with pleasure.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00504a -->
我愉快地期待着这次会面。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00504a -->
<!-- USE:U00505 -->
## anticipate /ænˈtɪsəpeɪt/ v.
`使原定将来发生的事件更早发生；这里改变的是事件的发生时间，而非仅仅预料它会发生。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00505a USE:U00505 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00505a1 USE:U00505 -->
The revised schedule anticipated the opening by a week.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00505a -->
修改后的日程把开幕时间提前了一周。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00505a -->
<!-- USE:U00506 -->
## anticipation /ænˌtɪsəˈpeɪʃən/ n.
`知道愉快或令人兴奋的事即将发生时产生的期待感，通常不可数，如 an atmosphere of anticipation。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00506a USE:U00506 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00506a1 USE:U00506 -->
There's been an atmosphere of anticipation around here for a few days now.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00506a -->
这几天，这里一直弥漫着兴奋期待的气氛。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00506a -->
<!-- USE:U00507 -->
## anticipation /ænˌtɪsəˈpeɪʃən/ n.
`in anticipation of 表示因为预料到某事会发生而事先作准备；of 后接所预料的事件，这件事不必令人愉快。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00507a USE:U00507 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00507a1 USE:U00507 -->
Troops in the Philippines have been put on full alert in anticipation of trouble during a planned general strike.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00507a -->
菲律宾军队已进入全面戒备，以应对预定总罢工期间可能出现的骚乱。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00507a -->
<!-- USE:U00508 -->
## anticipation /ænˌtɪsəˈpeɪʃən/ n.
`预料、预先处理或提前想到的行为、过程，含义比专指兴奋期待的用法更广。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00508a USE:U00508 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00508a1 USE:U00508 -->
Accurate anticipation of demand helps a library order enough copies.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00508a -->
准确预判需求，有助于图书馆订购足够的副本。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00508a -->
<!-- USE:U00509 -->
## anticipation /ænˌtɪsəˈpeɪʃən/ n.
`音乐中，下一个和弦中的音在该和弦到来前先出现的处理，通常提前出现的是随后和弦的一部分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00509a USE:U00509 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00509a1 USE:U00509 -->
The melody introduces an anticipation before the next chord sounds.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00509a -->
旋律在下一个和弦响起之前先引入了一个先现音。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00509a -->
<!-- USE:U00510 -->
## anticipative /ænˈtɪsəpətɪv/ adj.
`预先期待、预料或作准备的，体现事情发生前先作反应的特点。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00510a USE:U00510 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00510a1 USE:U00510 -->
Her anticipative excitement grew as the performance approached.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00510a -->
随着演出临近，她期待中的兴奋越来越强烈。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00510a -->
<!-- USE:U00511 -->
## anticipator /ænˈtɪsəpeɪtər/ n.
`预先想到、预料或抢先采取行动的人，可数；具体预见或抢先的对象由上下文说明。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00511a USE:U00511 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00511a1 USE:U00511 -->
An effective anticipator notices a need before it becomes urgent.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00511a -->
善于预先应对的人会在需求变得紧迫之前就注意到它。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00511a -->
<!-- USE:U00512 -->
## anticipatory /ænˈtɪsəpəˌtɔri/ adj.
`由于预料某事很快发生而产生某种感受或采取行动的，属于正式用语，通常置于名词前，可形容表情或预先采取的措施。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00512a USE:U00512 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00512a1 USE:U00512 -->
She gave an anticipatory smile.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00512a -->
她露出了期待的微笑。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00512a -->

<!-- WORD:W006 -->
# approximate

**词源**：来自拉丁语 approximare“靠近”，含表示朝向的前缀和 proximus“最近的”。“接近而未完全到达”可串起约数、粗略描述与近似。

<!-- USE:U00601 -->
## approximate /əˈprɑksəmət/ adj.
`数值、时间或位置接近正确值而非完全精确的，如 an approximate time、an approximate guess；词尾读 /mət/。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00601a USE:U00601 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00601a1 USE:U00601 -->
The approximate cost varies from around £150 to £250.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00601a -->
费用约在150至250英镑之间。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00601a -->
<!-- USE:U00602 -->
## approximate /əˈprɑksəmət/ adj.
`大致反映某事、并不试图精确呈现全部细节的，可形容 idea、description 等；它表示有粗略认识，与完全不知情不同。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00602a USE:U00602 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00602a1 USE:U00602 -->
They did not have even an approximate idea what the Germans really wanted.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00602a -->
他们甚至大致都不知道德国人真正想要什么。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00602a -->
<!-- USE:U00603 -->
## approximate /əˈprɑksəmət/ adj.
`位置上靠近、邻接的；这里谈空间接近，而非事物之间的大致相似。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00603a USE:U00603 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00603a1 USE:U00603 -->
Your approximate neighbour lives just beyond that wall.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00603a -->
你的近邻就住在那堵墙的另一边。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00603a -->
<!-- USE:U00604 -->
## approximate /əˈprɑksəmeɪt/ v.
`使某物接近，或自身接近某一数值、品质或状态，而不完全相同。可直接接宾语，也可用 approximate to something；动词词尾读 /meɪt/。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00604a USE:U00604 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00604a1 USE:U00604 -->
This account approximates to the truth.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00604a -->
这份叙述接近事实。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00604a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00604b USE:U00604 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00604b1 USE:U00604 -->
The sum approximates the amount required.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00604b -->
这笔款项接近所需金额。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00604b -->
<!-- USE:U00605 -->
## approximately /əˈprɑksəmətli/ adv.
`大约、近似地，常限定数量、时间等；读音沿用形容词中的 /mət/。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00605a USE:U00605 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00605a1 USE:U00605 -->
Approximately $150 million is to be spent on improvements.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00605a -->
将投入大约1.5亿美元进行改善。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00605a -->
<!-- USE:U00606 -->
## approximation /əˌprɑksəˈmeɪʃən/ n.
`与某物相似但并不完全相同的事物、描述或表达，可数，常接 of 或 to。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00606a USE:U00606 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00606a1 USE:U00606 -->
That is a fair approximation of the way in which the next boss is being chosen.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00606a -->
这大体反映了下一任负责人正在如何被选出来。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00606a -->
<!-- USE:U00607 -->
## approximation /əˌprɑksəˈmeɪʃən/ n.
`接近正确数值、计算结果或位置的估计值，可数；表示近似结果，须与精确测量值区分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00607r USE:U00607 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"用矩形逐步逼近面积","sourceUrls":["https://openstax.org/books/calculus-volume-1/pages/5-1-approximating-areas"],"displayContext":"OpenStax《Calculus Volume1》§5.1，缩编与中译，CC BY-NC-SA4.0。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00607r1 -->
For a continuous, nonnegative function, first approximate the area under its graph using rectangles.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00607r2 USE:U00607 -->
Smaller and smaller rectangles give closer and closer approximations to the area; taking a limit gives the exact area.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00607r -->
对于连续且非负的函数，先用矩形估算其图像下的面积。矩形越来越小，得到的面积近似值就越来越接近真实面积；取极限则可得到精确面积。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00607r -->

<!-- WORD:W007 -->
# archive

**词源**：经法语、拉丁语追溯到希腊语表示“公共办公建筑”的词；其复数又指存放在其中的公共记录。由“管理记录的地方”联想到“档案”，再到把资料存档的动作。

<!-- USE:U00701 -->
## archive /ˈɑrkaɪv/ n.
`收集保存的文件、记录集合，常含历史信息，尤其可指公共部门或机构的记录。可数，常用复数 archives。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00701r USE:U00701 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"档案中保存着哪些节目","sourceUrls":["https://americanarchive.org/exhibits/first-amendment/protests-60s-70s"],"displayContext":"American Archive of Public Broadcasting展览馆藏说明，三个固定历史时点，事实缩编。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00701r1 USE:U00701 -->
The AAPB archives contain programmes covering the occupation of Alcatraz Island in 1969, the Siege of Wounded Knee in 1973, and the Longest Walk in 1978.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00701r2 -->
These recordings document the Red Power movement.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00701r -->
美国公共广播档案收有一些节目，报道了1969年占领阿尔卡特拉斯岛、1973年包围伤膝镇以及1978年“最长的步行”活动。这些录音影像记录了“红色力量”运动。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00701r -->
<!-- USE:U00702 -->
## archive /ˈɑrkaɪv/ n.
`保管档案、记录的地方，常用复数形式指档案馆或档案室；具体是存放地点还是记录集合，须由语境判断。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00702a USE:U00702 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00702a1 USE:U00702 -->
The archive occupies two rooms beneath the library.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00702a -->
档案室占用了图书馆下方的两个房间。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00702a -->
<!-- USE:U00703 -->
## archive /ˈɑrkaɪv/ adj.
`来自档案、作为历史资料保存的，置于名词前，如 archive film、archive material。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00703a USE:U00703 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00703a1 USE:U00703 -->
The documentary includes pieces of archive film.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00703a -->
这部纪录片中包含一些历史档案影像。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00703a -->
<!-- USE:U00704 -->
## archive /ˈɑrkaɪv/ v.
`把文件或资料放入档案中保存，直接接所归档的内容，如 archive the letters。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00704a USE:U00704 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00704a1 USE:U00704 -->
The staff archived the signed letters after the meeting.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00704a -->
会议结束后，工作人员将签署的信件归档。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00704a -->
<!-- USE:U00705 -->
## archive /ˈɑrkaɪv/ v.
`计算机用语，把数据转移到不常使用的文件或存储介质中保存，强调从日常使用转入较少访问的存档状态。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00705a USE:U00705 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00705a1 USE:U00705 -->
The system archives inactive records to a separate storage tier.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00705a -->
系统把不再活跃使用的记录转存到独立的存储层。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00705a -->
<!-- USE:U00706 -->
## archival /ɑrˈkaɪvəl/ adj.
`属于档案、与档案有关的，通常放在名词前，如 archival material。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00706a USE:U00706 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00706a1 USE:U00706 -->
The introduction discusses his extensive use of archival material.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00706a -->
导言讨论了他对档案材料的广泛使用。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00706a -->
<!-- USE:U00707 -->
## archivist /ˈɑrkɪvɪst/ n.
`以收集、整理、保管历史档案与记录为工作的人，即档案工作人员，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00707a USE:U00707 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00707a1 USE:U00707 -->
An archivist arranged the papers by their original office of creation.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00707a -->
一位档案工作人员按文件最初形成的部门整理了这些文件。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00707a -->

<!-- WORD:W008 -->
# capacity

**词源**：经法语来自拉丁语 capacitas，源于 capax“能够容纳、接纳的”。从“装得下多少”理解容积和承载量，再联系“能做到多少”的能力与产能。

<!-- USE:U00801 -->
## capacity /kəˈpæsəti/ n.
`接受、感受、承受或完成某事的能力，也指智力或某种才能，可数或不可数。常用 capacity for something/doing something、capacity to do something；creative、earning、intellectual、mental 说明能力种类，limited、diminished 说明其限度，develop/boost one's capacity 表示发展或增强能力。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00801a USE:U00801 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00801a1 USE:U00801 -->
Our capacity for giving care, love and attention is limited.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00801a -->
我们给予照护、爱与关注的能力是有限的。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00801a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00801b USE:U00801 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00801b1 USE:U00801 -->
People differ in their capacity for heat, pain, and other physical stresses.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00801b -->
人们承受高温、疼痛及其他身体压力的能力各不相同。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00801b -->
<!-- USE:U00802 -->
## capacity /kəˈpæsəti/ n.
`现有设备与资源能够生产或提供的数量，尤指最大产出，通常不可数。可说 productive、full、maximum、spare capacity，用 boost、expand、increase、reduce 表示产能变化；work at full capacity 是满负荷生产。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00802a USE:U00802 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00802a1 USE:U00802 -->
Bread factories are working at full capacity.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00802a -->
面包工厂正在满负荷生产。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00802a -->
<!-- USE:U00803 -->
## capacity /kəˈpæsəti/ n.
`设备能够承载、提供或完成的规格量，可数，常以具体单位说明，如载重能力、发动机容量或设备功率。具体含义取决于计量对象，不都表示体积。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00803a USE:U00803 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00803a1 USE:U00803 -->
The display describes an aircraft with a bomb-carrying capacity of 454 kg.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00803a -->
展品说明介绍了一架载弹量为454千克的飞机。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00803a -->
<!-- USE:U00804 -->
## capacity /kəˈpæsəti/ n.
`容器或内部空间能够容纳的体积，可数或不可数，常用升、加仑等计量，如 fuel、lung、storage capacity。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00804a USE:U00804 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00804a1 USE:U00804 -->
They inspected the fuel tanks, which had a capacity of 140 litres.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00804a -->
他们检查了那些容量为140升的油箱。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00804a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00804b USE:U00804 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00804b1 USE:U00804 -->
Grease 6 ramekin dishes of 150 ml (5-6 fl oz) capacity.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00804b -->
给六个容量为150毫升（5至6液体盎司）的烤盅抹上油。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00804b -->
<!-- USE:U00805 -->
## capacity /kəˈpæsəti/ n.
`建筑、场地或车辆能够容纳的最大人数或物品数，常用单数。seating capacity 指座位容量，a capacity of 后接具体数量。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00805a USE:U00805 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00805a1 USE:U00805 -->
Each stadium had a seating capacity of about 50,000.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00805a -->
每座体育场约能容纳五万名观众就座。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00805a -->
<!-- USE:U00806 -->
## capacity /kəˈpæsəti/ n.
`在短语 to capacity 中表示达到全部容量或动用全部可用资源。filled to capacity 是装满或满员，work to capacity 是全力、满负荷运作。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00806a USE:U00806 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00806a1 USE:U00806 -->
Toronto hospital maternity wards were filled to capacity.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00806a -->
多伦多医院的产科病房已经住满。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00806a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00806b USE:U00806 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00806b1 USE:U00806 -->
The workshop is working to capacity.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00806b -->
这间工场正在满负荷运作。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00806b -->
<!-- USE:U00807 -->
## capacity /kəˈpæsəti/ adj.
`把可用空间或席位完全占满的，置于名词前，如 a capacity crowd、a capacity audience。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00807a USE:U00807 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00807a1 USE:U00807 -->
A capacity crowd of 76,000 people was at Wembley football stadium for the event.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00807a -->
七万六千名观众挤满了温布利足球场，观看这项活动。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00807a -->
<!-- USE:U00808 -->
## capacity /kəˈpæsəti/ n.
`履行某项工作、职责或代表某组织时所处的身份、职能，主要见于书面语。常用 in one's capacity as somebody，也可说 in a personal/civil capacity。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00808a USE:U00808 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00808a1 USE:U00808 -->
She visited the Philippines in her capacity as a Special Representative of Unicef.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00808a -->
她以联合国儿童基金会特别代表的身份访问了菲律宾。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00808a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00808b USE:U00808 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00808b1 USE:U00808 -->
This article is written in a personal capacity.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00808b -->
本文以个人身份撰写。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00808b -->
<!-- USE:U00809 -->
## capacity /kəˈpæsəti/ n.
`法律上，作出特定行为、承担相应权利义务的资格或能力；具体能力及其判断条件依法律制度和行为类型而定。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00809a USE:U00809 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00809a1 USE:U00809 -->
The court considered her capacity to enter into the contract.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00809a -->
法院审查了她订立该合同的法律行为能力。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00809a -->
<!-- USE:U00810 -->
## capacity /kəˈpæsəti/ n.; capacitance /kəˈpæsɪtəns/ n.
`电学中，电系统储存电荷的能力，或储存电荷量的变化与相应电势差变化的比值。现代技术表达常用 capacitance，符号为 C；线性情形可写 C = Q/V，更一般的微分电容写作 dQ/dV。这里指电荷储存能力，而非电流。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00810r USE:U00810 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"同样电压能储存多少电荷","sourceUrls":["https://openstax.org/books/university-physics-volume-2/pages/8-1-capacitors-and-capacitance"],"displayContext":"OpenStax《University Physics Volume2》§8.1，缩编与中译，CC BY-NC-SA4.0。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00810r1 -->
Capacitors with different shapes and sizes store different amounts of charge at the same voltage.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00810r2 USE:U00810 -->
Capacitance is the amount of charge stored per volt across the plates.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00810r -->
形状和尺寸不同的电容器，在相同电压下储存的电荷量不同。电容表示极板间每单位电压所对应的储存电荷量。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00810r -->
<!-- USE:U00811 -->
## capacitative /kəˈpæsətətɪv/ adj.; capacitive /kəˈpæsətɪv/ adj.
`与电容有关、依靠电容作用的，用于电学；capacitive 是 capacitative 的另一形式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00811a USE:U00811 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00811a1 USE:U00811 -->
A capacitive sensor responds to a change in capacitance.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00811a -->
电容式传感器会对电容的变化作出响应。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00811a -->

<!-- WORD:W009 -->
# coherent

**词源**：来自拉丁语 cohaerere“黏在一起”；co- 表示共同，后半表示黏附。“各部分接得起来”可帮助理解材料黏合、思想连贯与波的相干关系。

<!-- USE:U00901 -->
## coherent /koʊˈhɪrənt/ adj.
`论证、政策、计划或作品等的各部分合乎逻辑、彼此一致，因而清楚易懂的；不仅指文字流畅，也要求内容之间能够相互配合。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00901a USE:U00901 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00901a1 USE:U00901 -->
He has failed to work out a coherent strategy for modernising the service.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00901a -->
他未能为这项服务的现代化制定出一套连贯的策略。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00901a -->
<!-- USE:U00902 -->
## coherent /koʊˈhɪrənt/ adj.
`能够清楚、连贯地表达思想，让别人听懂的。形容人时常作表语，强调当时的表达状态。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00902a USE:U00902 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00902a1 -->
He's so calm when he answers questions in interviews.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00902a2 USE:U00902 -->
I wish I could be that coherent.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00902a -->
他在访谈中回答问题时十分镇定。我希望自己也能表达得那么清楚。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00902a -->
<!-- USE:U00903 -->
## coherent /koʊˈhɪrənt/ adj.
`材料或物体的各部分黏在一起、连接成整体的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00903a USE:U00903 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00903a1 USE:U00903 -->
The particles form a coherent layer rather than a loose powder.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00903a -->
这些颗粒形成了一层黏结的材料，而不是松散粉末。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00903a -->
<!-- USE:U00904 -->
## coherent /koʊˈhɪrənt/ adj.
`物理学中，波之间具有恒定相位关系的，即“相干的”；这里不评价语言是否有条理。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00904a USE:U00904 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00904a1 USE:U00904 -->
The two coherent waves maintain a fixed phase relationship.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00904a -->
这两列相干波保持固定的相位关系。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00904a -->
<!-- USE:U00905 -->
## cohere /koʊˈhɪr/ v.
`各部分黏在一起，或整体保持团结、连结。不及物，既可描述物质黏合，也可描述组织整体的维系。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00905a USE:U00905 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00905a1 USE:U00905 -->
The empire could not cohere as a legitimate whole.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00905a -->
这个帝国无法作为一个有合法基础的整体维系在一起。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00905a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00905b USE:U00905 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00905b1 USE:U00905 -->
The damp particles cohere to form a solid lump.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00905b -->
这些湿润的颗粒黏结成了一个实心团块。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00905b -->
<!-- USE:U00906 -->
## cohere /koʊˈhɪr/ v.
`思想、推理或作品的各部分相互吻合，构成一致的整体。不及物；cohere with something 表示与另一观点或事实相符。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00906a USE:U00906 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00906a1 USE:U00906 -->
The various elements of the novel fail to cohere.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00906a -->
这部小说的各个要素未能形成连贯的整体。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00906a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00906b USE:U00906 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00906b1 USE:U00906 -->
This coheres with Peel's championing of alternative music.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00906b -->
这与皮尔对非主流音乐的支持是一致的。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00906b -->
<!-- USE:U00907 -->
## coherence /koʊˈhɪrəns/ n.; coherency /koʊˈhɪrənsi/ n.
`思想、作品或表达的各部分彼此一致、清楚连贯的性质，也可描述一个人说话时的连贯、清醒程度。coherence 通常不可数，coherency 是相应的另一名词形式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00907a USE:U00907 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00907a1 USE:U00907 -->
The anthology has a surprising sense of coherence.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00907a -->
这部选集呈现出令人意外的整体连贯感。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00907a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00907b USE:U00907 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00907b1 USE:U00907 -->
She lost consciousness and when she came round she still lacked coherence and focus.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00907b -->
她曾失去意识，苏醒后说话仍不连贯，也难以集中注意力。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00907b -->
<!-- USE:U00908 -->
## coherence /koʊˈhɪrəns/ n.; coherency /koʊˈhɪrənsi/ n.
`物质或材料的各部分相互黏结、结合成整体的性质。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00908a USE:U00908 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00908a1 USE:U00908 -->
The binder improves the coherence of the pressed powder.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00908a -->
黏结剂改善了压制粉末的结合性。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00908a -->
<!-- USE:U00909 -->
## coherently /koʊˈhɪrəntli/ adv.
`以条理清楚、前后一致的方式，常说明说话、解释或论证的方式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00909a USE:U00909 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00909a1 USE:U00909 -->
She explained the sequence of events coherently.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00909a -->
她有条理地解释了事情的先后经过。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00909a -->
<!-- USE:U00910 -->
## coherently /koʊˈhɪrəntli/ adv.
`物理语境中，波等在保持稳定相位关系的情况下，即“相干地”。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00910a USE:U00910 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00910a1 USE:U00910 -->
The beams combine coherently at the detector.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00910a -->
这些光束在探测器处相干叠加。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00910a -->
<!-- USE:U00911 -->
## incoherent /ˌɪnkoʊˈhɪrənt/ adj.
`无法把话说得清楚、让人理解的，形容人的表达状态；可用 incoherent with fear 等说明原因。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00911a USE:U00911 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00911a1 USE:U00911 -->
The man was almost incoherent with fear.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00911a -->
这个男人吓得几乎语无伦次。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00911a -->
<!-- USE:U00912 -->
## incoherent /ˌɪnkoʊˈhɪrənt/ adj.
`说法、政策或目标的各部分缺乏逻辑与一致性的；评价政策等时常带批评意味。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00912a USE:U00912 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00912a1 USE:U00912 -->
The report identifies an incoherent set of objectives.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00912a -->
报告指出了一组彼此不一致的目标。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00912a -->
<!-- USE:U00913 -->
## incoherent /ˌɪnkoʊˈhɪrənt/ adj.
`物理学中，波之间没有明确、稳定相位关系的，即“非相干的”，与 coherent 相对。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00913a USE:U00913 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00913a1 USE:U00913 -->
The sources emit mutually incoherent light.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00913a -->
这些光源发出的光彼此非相干。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00913a -->
<!-- USE:U00914 -->
## incoherence /ˌɪnkoʊˈhɪrəns/ n.; incoherency /ˌɪnkoʊˈhɪrənsi/ n.
`人的表达难以理解，或论述、政策各部分缺乏一致性的状态。incoherency 还可用复数表示具体的不连贯之处。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00914a USE:U00914 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00914a1 USE:U00914 -->
Beth's incoherence told Amy that something was terribly wrong.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00914a -->
贝丝说话语无伦次，让艾米意识到出了很严重的问题。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00914a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00914b USE:U00914 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00914b1 USE:U00914 -->
The debate exposed the general incoherence of government policy.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00914b -->
这场辩论暴露出政府政策整体上缺乏一致性。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00914b -->
<!-- USE:U00915 -->
## incoherence /ˌɪnkoʊˈhɪrəns/ n.; incoherency /ˌɪnkoʊˈhɪrənsi/ n.
`物理学中，波之间缺少稳定相位关系的性质，即非相干性。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00915a USE:U00915 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00915a1 USE:U00915 -->
The incoherence of the sources prevents a stable interference pattern.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00915a -->
光源之间的非相干性使稳定干涉条纹无法形成。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00915a -->
<!-- USE:U00916 -->
## incoherently /ˌɪnkoʊˈhɪrəntli/ adv.
`以缺乏条理、难以理解的方式，常描述说话或表达。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00916a USE:U00916 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00916a1 USE:U00916 -->
He collapsed on the floor, mumbling incoherently.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00916a -->
他倒在地上，含混不清地喃喃说着。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00916a -->
<!-- USE:U00917 -->
## incoherently /ˌɪnkoʊˈhɪrəntli/ adv.
`物理语境中，波等在没有稳定相位关系的情况下，即“非相干地”。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00917a USE:U00917 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00917a1 USE:U00917 -->
Light from independent thermal sources usually adds incoherently.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00917a -->
来自独立热光源的光通常以非相干方式叠加。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00917a -->
<!-- USE:U00918 -->
## coherence /koʊˈhɪrəns/ n.; coherency /koʊˈhɪrənsi/ n.
`物理学中，波之间保持稳定相位关系的性质；与材料黏结成整体的性质区分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU00918r USE:U00918 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"光波如何保持同步","sourceUrls":["https://www.nist.gov/history/nists-role-laser-measurements-and-applications/what-laser"],"displayContext":"NIST激光科普说明，正文缩编；本段只说明相干性，不讨论材料黏结。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00918r1 USE:U00918 -->
Laser light has a property called coherence: the peaks and troughs of its waves travel in step.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU00918r2 -->
This helps the light stay in a narrow beam over long distances.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU00918r -->
激光有一种称为相干性的性质：光波的波峰与波谷保持同步。这有助于光在长距离传播时仍保持成束。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU00918r -->

<!-- WORD:W010 -->
# commodity

**词源**：经法语来自拉丁语 commoditas，早期含合宜、便利等意思。现代“可买卖的有用之物”保留了这一线索，后来尤其常指商品与原料。

<!-- USE:U01001 -->
## commodity /kəˈmɑdəti/ n.
`能够买卖的商品或原材料，尤其指有形产品而非服务，属于商业用语。可数，复数 commodities，可指面包、肉类、石油等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01001a USE:U01001 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01001a1 USE:U01001 -->
The government increased prices on several basic commodities like bread and meat.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01001a -->
政府提高了面包、肉类等几种基本商品的价格。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01001a -->
<!-- USE:U01002 -->
## commodity /kəˈmɑdəti/ n.
`有用、可供需要或被人珍视的东西，可数；也可引申指时间等，不一定指市场上实际交易的商品。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01002a USE:U01002 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01002a1 USE:U01002 -->
For a parent working two jobs, free time is a precious commodity.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01002a -->
对一位做两份工作的家长来说，空闲时间十分宝贵。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01002a -->

<!-- WORD:W011 -->
# compromise

**词源**：来自法语及拉丁语表示“共同承诺”的词，最初可指共同同意服从仲裁决定。由互相承诺理解协商和解；动词又发展出因让步或轻率行为而损害原则、名誉与安全的意思。

<!-- USE:U01101 -->
## compromise /ˈkɑmprəmaɪz/ n.
`通过彼此让步达成的解决办法，或在冲突要求之间形成的中间状态，可数或不可数。reach a compromise 表示达成和解，a compromise between A and B 表示两种要求之间的折中。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01101a USE:U01101 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01101a1 USE:U01101 -->
Encourage your child to reach a compromise between what he wants and what you want.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01101a -->
鼓励孩子在他的愿望和你的愿望之间达成折中。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01101a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01101b USE:U01101 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01101b1 USE:U01101 -->
The proposal is a compromise between ideals and material necessity.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01101b -->
这一提案是在理想与物质需求之间作出的折中。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01101b -->
<!-- USE:U01102 -->
## compromise /ˈkɑmprəmaɪz/ v.
`各自放弃部分原先要求，以解决争执或达成协议。不及物，常用 compromise with somebody over/on something；compromise on an amount 可表示接受较低或折中的数额。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01102a USE:U01102 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01102a1 -->
'Nine,' said I.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01102a2 -->
'Nine thirty,' tried he.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01102a3 USE:U01102 -->
We compromised on nine fifteen.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01102a -->
“九点。”我说。“九点半。”他试着提议。我们折中定在九点十五分。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01102a -->
<!-- USE:U01103 -->
## compromise /ˈkɑmprəmaɪz/ v.
`通过相互让步解决某项争执。属于较古的及物结构，直接以 dispute、claim 等为宾语，须与现代常见的不及物协商结构区分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01103r USE:U01103 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"退还欺诈所得与认罪条件的互让","sourceUrls":["https://www.gao.gov/products/b-220172-0"],"displayContext":"GAO B-220172，1986年3月4日决定；具体案件背景缩编，及物动词为原法律措辞的现代重组。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01103r1 USE:U01103 -->
The Department of Justice compromised the claims over false travel vouchers: each of the three defendants agreed to repay the fraudulent amounts in return for being allowed to plead guilty to a misdemeanour rather than face possible felony charges.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01103r -->
司法部对虚假差旅报销引发的债权作了和解：三名被告各自同意退还欺诈所得，以换取获准对轻罪认罪，而不面对可能提出的重罪指控。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01103r -->
<!-- USE:U01104 -->
## compromise /ˈkɑmprəmaɪz/ v.
`使名誉、原则或安全等受损、陷入危险，直接接受损对象。compromise oneself、compromise one's principles 常带批评意味，可指因接受贿赂或轻率行为而自损立场。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01104a USE:U01104 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01104a1 USE:U01104 -->
He compromised himself by accepting a bribe.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01104a -->
他收受贿赂，损害了自己的名誉。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01104a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01104b USE:U01104 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01104b1 USE:U01104 -->
She refused the promotion rather than compromise her principles.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01104b -->
她宁可放弃晋升，也不愿损害自己的原则。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01104b -->
<!-- USE:U01105 -->
## compromiser /ˈkɑmprəmaɪzər/ n.
`通过互让寻求解决办法的人，可数，由 compromise 加 -er 构成。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01105a USE:U01105 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01105a1 USE:U01105 -->
The chair acted as a compromiser, looking for terms both sides could accept.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01105a -->
主席充当寻求折中的人，寻找双方都能接受的条件。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01105a -->
<!-- USE:U01106 -->
## compromising /ˈkɑmprəmaɪzɪŋ/ adj.
`会暴露某人令人尴尬或有罪的秘密、使其名誉受损的，常置于名词前，如 compromising information、a compromising situation。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01106a USE:U01106 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01106a1 USE:U01106 -->
How had this compromising picture come into the possession of the press?
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01106a -->
这张会使人名誉受损的照片是怎么落到媒体手里的？
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01106a -->
<!-- USE:U01107 -->
## compromisingly /ˈkɑmprəmaɪzɪŋli/ adv.
`以使名誉、立场或安全受损的方式；具体受损的对象须由上下文明确。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01107a USE:U01107 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01107a1 USE:U01107 -->
The photograph showed them standing compromisingly close together.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01107a -->
照片显示两人站得过分亲密，足以损害他们的名誉。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01107a -->
<!-- USE:U01108 -->
## uncompromising /ʌnˈkɑmprəmaɪzɪŋ/ adj.
`坚定不改变意见或目标、不愿妥协的。既可强调坚韧坚定，也可批评固执，具体评价由语境决定。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01108a USE:U01108 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01108a1 USE:U01108 -->
She was a tough and uncompromising politician.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01108a -->
她是一位强硬而不肯让步的政治人物。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01108a -->
<!-- USE:U01109 -->
## uncompromising /ʌnˈkɑmprəmaɪzɪŋ/ adj.
`不试图把震撼、严酷或鲜明的特征修饰得容易接受的，可形容影片对残酷的直接表现、设计的鲜明风格等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01109a USE:U01109 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01109a1 USE:U01109 -->
The review discusses a film of uncompromising brutality.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01109a -->
这篇影评讨论了一部毫不掩饰残酷场面的电影。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01109a -->
<!-- USE:U01110 -->
## uncompromisingly /ʌnˈkɑmprəmaɪzɪŋli/ adv.
`坚定不让步地，或毫不缓和其鲜明特征地；既可说明表态方式，也可修饰形容词以突出彻底的风格。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01110a USE:U01110 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01110a1 USE:U01110 -->
He states uncompromisingly that he is opposed to any practices which oppress animals.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01110a -->
他毫不退让地表明，自己反对任何压迫动物的做法。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01110a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01110b USE:U01110 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01110b1 USE:U01110 -->
Visitors noticed the uncompromisingly modern decor.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01110b -->
来访者注意到了那种彻底现代、毫不折中的装饰风格。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01110b -->
<!-- USE:U01111 -->
## uncompromisingness /ʌnˈkɑmprəmaɪzɪŋnəs/ n.
`不愿妥协、坚持原有立场的性质或态度。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01111a USE:U01111 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01111a1 USE:U01111 -->
Her uncompromisingness made agreement difficult.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01111a -->
她不肯妥协的态度使达成协议变得困难。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01111a -->

<!-- WORD:W012 -->
# conserve

**词源**：经法语来自拉丁语 conservare“保护、保存”，后半 servare 有看护、保持的意思。资源节用、艺术保存与糖渍水果都保留这一线索；conservative 又发展出保守态度、估计偏谨慎和政治称谓等含义，不能只按同一中文词理解。

<!-- USE:U01201 -->
## conserve /kənˈsɝv/ v.
`谨慎使用某种有限供应，使它能维持更久，直接接所节省的资源，如 conserve energy/water。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01201a USE:U01201 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01201a1 USE:U01201 -->
The republic's factories have closed for the weekend to conserve energy.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01201a -->
共和国的工厂周末停工，以节约能源。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01201a -->
<!-- USE:U01202 -->
## conserve /kənˈsɝv/ v.
`保护某物，使其免受损害、流失或改变，直接接保护对象，如森林、历史物件或艺术作品。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01202r USE:U01202 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"当作品会随环境消失","sourceUrls":["https://www.getty.edu/news/artist-dialogues-getty-conservation-lita-albuquerque-malibu-line/"],"displayContext":"Getty，Lilibeth Garcia《In Their Own Words》，2024年11月；作品创作于1978年，导入与问题缩编，非访谈原话。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01202r1 -->
Lita Albuquerque's Malibu Line was a trench filled with blue pigment.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01202r2 -->
Two years later it had disappeared under flowers and grass.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01202r3 USE:U01202 -->
How do you conserve site-specific art exposed to environmental change?
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01202r -->
莉塔·阿尔伯克基的《马里布之线》是一条填入蓝色颜料的浅沟。两年后，它消失在花草之下。面对暴露于环境变化中的场地艺术，应该怎样保护？
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01202r -->
<!-- USE:U01203 -->
## conserve /kənˈsɝv/ v.
`物理学中，使某个物理量保持不变。须明确系统与成立条件，不能把热与总能量等概念随意互换。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01203a USE:U01203 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01203a1 USE:U01203 -->
The isolated collision model conserves total momentum.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01203a -->
这个孤立系统的碰撞模型保持总动量守恒。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01203a -->
<!-- USE:U01204 -->
## conserve /kənˈsɝv/ v.
`保存食品，尤其指用糖保存水果，直接接所保存的食物。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01204a USE:U01204 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01204a1 USE:U01204 -->
They conserve the fruit in sugar.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01204a -->
他们用糖保存水果。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01204a -->
<!-- USE:U01205 -->
## conserve /ˈkɑnsɝv/ n.
`用糖保存的水果等，即蜜饯或果脯，可数或不可数；名词的重音与动词不同。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01205a USE:U01205 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01205a1 USE:U01205 -->
A small dish of fruit conserve accompanied the meal.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01205a -->
餐点配了一小碟糖渍水果。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01205a -->
<!-- USE:U01206 -->
## conserve /ˈkɑnsɝv/ n.
`含大量果实、常保留整块果肉的果酱，也可指鲜果酱。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01206a USE:U01206 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01206a1 USE:U01206 -->
She spread strawberry conserve on the toast.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01206a -->
她把高果量草莓酱抹在吐司上。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01206a -->
<!-- USE:U01207 -->
## conservation /ˌkɑnsərˈveɪʃən/ n.
`保护自然环境、物种等的行动，通常不可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01207a USE:U01207 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01207a1 USE:U01207 -->
The delegates attended a four-nation regional meeting on elephant conservation.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01207a -->
代表们参加了一个四国大象保护地区会议。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01207a -->
<!-- USE:U01208 -->
## conservation /ˌkɑnsərˈveɪʃən/ n.
`保护、保存历史物件、艺术品或建筑的工作，通常不可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01208a USE:U01208 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01208a1 USE:U01208 -->
The second image was discovered during conservation of the painting.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01208a -->
这幅画在保存修复过程中，被发现还藏有第二个图像。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01208a -->
<!-- USE:U01209 -->
## conservation /ˌkɑnsərˈveɪʃən/ n.
`谨慎使用有限资源，使其不被过快耗尽，通常不可数，如 energy conservation。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01209a USE:U01209 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01209a1 USE:U01209 -->
The fund supports projects aimed at promoting energy conservation.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01209a -->
这只基金支持旨在促进节能的项目。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01209a -->
<!-- USE:U01210 -->
## conservation of energy /ˌkɑnsərˈveɪʃən əv ˈenərdʒi/ n.; conservation of mass /ˌkɑnsərˈveɪʃən əv mæs/ n.; conservation of momentum /ˌkɑnsərˈveɪʃən əv moʊˈmentəm/ n.
`物理学中，在相应系统条件下，总能量、质量或动量保持不变的原理。不同守恒量各有成立条件，须明确所研究的系统及外部影响。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01210a USE:U01210 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01210a1 USE:U01210 -->
Conservation of energy requires us to include the energy transferred to the surroundings.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01210a -->
运用能量守恒时，必须把传到周围环境中的能量也计算在内。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01210a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01210b USE:U01210 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01210b1 USE:U01210 -->
In an ordinary chemical reaction in a closed system, conservation of mass lets us compare the total mass before and after the reaction.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01210b -->
在封闭系统的一般化学反应中，质量守恒使我们能够比较反应前后的总质量。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01210b -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01210c USE:U01210 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01210c1 USE:U01210 -->
Conservation of momentum applies to a system when there is no net external impulse.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01210c -->
系统没有受到净外冲量时，动量守恒成立。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01210c -->
<!-- USE:U01211 -->
## conservational /ˌkɑnsərˈveɪʃənəl/ adj.
`与保护、保存或守恒有关的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01211a USE:U01211 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01211a1 USE:U01211 -->
The study discusses conservational measures for the wetland.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01211a -->
这项研究讨论保护湿地的措施。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01211a -->
<!-- USE:U01212 -->
## conservationist /ˌkɑnsərˈveɪʃənɪst/ n.
`关心并从事自然环境保护的人，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01212a USE:U01212 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01212a1 USE:U01212 -->
A conservationist monitored the nesting site.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01212a -->
一位自然保护工作者监测了这处筑巢地。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01212a -->
<!-- USE:U01213 -->
## conservator /kənˈsɝvətər/ n.
`以清洁、修复历史物件或艺术品为工作的专业人员，即文物修复师，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01213a USE:U01213 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01213a1 USE:U01213 -->
The conservator cleaned the painting's surface.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01213a -->
文物修复师清洁了画作表面。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01213a -->
<!-- USE:U01214 -->
## conservator /kənˈsɝvətər/ n.
`美式法律用语，指被依法赋予对另一人承担责任的监护人；具体权限依法院裁定而定。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01214r USE:U01214 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"先查看法院授予的权限","sourceUrls":["https://www.utcourts.gov/en/self-help/case-categories/gc/authority-conservator.html"],"displayContext":"Utah Courts受任命者指南缩编；此处指财产监护，范围以法院文书为准。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01214r1 USE:U01214 -->
When a Utah court appoints you as conservator to manage another person's property, it gives you an order and a Letter of Conservatorship.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01214r2 -->
Check the letter to find out exactly what you are allowed to do.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01214r -->
犹他州法院任命你担任财产监护人、管理他人财产时，会交给你裁定和财产监护授权书。查看授权书，确认自己具体获准做哪些事。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01214r -->
<!-- USE:U01215 -->
## conservator /kənˈsɝvətər/ n.
`负责保护某物或正式保管博物馆等机构藏品的人，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01215a USE:U01215 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01215a1 USE:U01215 -->
The conservator was responsible for the museum's collection.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01215a -->
这位保管者负责博物馆的藏品。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01215a -->
<!-- USE:U01216 -->
## conservatism /kənˈsɝvəˌtɪzəm/ n.
`主张社会若需改变，也应以渐进方式进行的政治观念，通常不可数。也可指某国保守派政党的政治信念，后一用法可写作 Conservatism。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01216a USE:U01216 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01216a1 USE:U01216 -->
The book examines the philosophy of modern Conservatism.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01216a -->
这本书考察现代保守主义的政治哲学。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01216a -->
<!-- USE:U01217 -->
## conservatism /kənˈsɝvəˌtɪzəm/ n.
`不愿接受变化、新想法的态度，通常不可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01217a USE:U01217 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01217a1 USE:U01217 -->
The conservatism of the literary establishment in this country is astounding.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01217a -->
这个国家文学界权威阶层的守旧程度令人惊讶。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01217a -->
<!-- USE:U01218 -->
## Conservative /kənˈsɝvətɪv/ adj.
`属于英国保守党或投票支持该党的，首字母大写。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01218a USE:U01218 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01218a1 USE:U01218 -->
Most Conservative MPs appear happy with the government's reassurances.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01218a -->
多数保守党议员似乎对政府的保证感到满意。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01218a -->
<!-- USE:U01219 -->
## Conservative /kənˈsɝvətɪv/ n.
`英国保守党的成员或支持者，可数，首字母大写。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01219a USE:U01219 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01219a1 USE:U01219 -->
In 1951, the Conservatives were returned to power.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01219a -->
1951年，保守党重新执政。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01219a -->
<!-- USE:U01220 -->
## conservative /kənˈsɝvətɪv/ adj.
`在政治上持右翼立场的，具体政治内容依国家和语境而异。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01220a USE:U01220 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01220a1 USE:U01220 -->
The article describes counties whose citizens invariably support the most conservative candidate in any election.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01220a -->
这篇文章描述了一些县，在那里，选民在任何选举中总是支持最保守的候选人。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01220a -->
<!-- USE:U01221 -->
## conservative /kənˈsɝvətɪv/ n.
`持保守政治观念或不愿快速改变的人，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01221a USE:U01221 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01221a1 USE:U01221 -->
As a conservative, she prefers gradual reform to abrupt political change.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01221a -->
作为一名保守派人士，她更倾向于渐进改革，而非突然的政治变化。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01221a -->
<!-- USE:U01222 -->
## conservative /kənˈsɝvətɪv/ adj.
`不愿接受变化、新思想的，可形容人、观点或做法。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01222a USE:U01222 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01222a1 USE:U01222 -->
It is essentially a narrow and conservative approach to child care.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01222a -->
这本质上是一种狭隘、守旧的育儿方式。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01222a -->
<!-- USE:U01223 -->
## conservative /kənˈsɝvətɪv/ adj.
`观点、品味等保持温和、避免极端的；这一含义不等于支持某一特定政党。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01223a USE:U01223 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01223a1 USE:U01223 -->
His proposals were conservative in tone, avoiding extremes.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01223a -->
他的提议基调温和，避免极端。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01223a -->
<!-- USE:U01224 -->
## conservative /kənˈsɝvətɪv/ adj.
`服装风格符合惯常方式、不追求新奇的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01224a USE:U01224 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01224a1 USE:U01224 -->
The girl was well dressed, as usual, though in a more conservative style.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01224a -->
那个女孩像往常一样衣着得体，不过风格更传统些。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01224a -->
<!-- USE:U01225 -->
## conservative /kənˈsɝvətɪv/ adj.
`估计时有意偏低、稳妥谨慎的，常置于 estimate、guess 前。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01225a USE:U01225 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01225a1 USE:U01225 -->
A conservative estimate of the bill, so far, is about £22,000.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01225a -->
到目前为止，对这笔费用作保守估计，大约是两万两千英镑。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01225a -->
<!-- USE:U01226 -->
## conservative /kənˈsɝvətɪv/ adj.
`倾向于保存某物、使某种量保持不变的；在物理等技术语境中可表示守恒的性质。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01226r USE:U01226 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"做功是否依赖路径","sourceUrls":["https://openstax.org/books/university-physics-volume-1/pages/8-2-conservative-and-non-conservative-forces"],"displayContext":"OpenStax《University Physics Volume1》§8.2，缩编与中译，CC BY-NC-SA4.0。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01226r1 USE:U01226 -->
A conservative force does the same work along any path between two fixed points.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01226r2 -->
Gravity and spring forces are examples; friction and air resistance are non-conservative.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01226r -->
保守力在两个固定点之间沿任意路径做的功都相同。重力和弹簧力属于保守力，摩擦力和空气阻力则是非保守力。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01226r -->
<!-- USE:U01227 -->
## conservatively /kənˈsɝvətɪvli/ adv.
`以传统、不求新变或避免过激的方式，可说明衣着等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01227a USE:U01227 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01227a1 USE:U01227 -->
She was always very conservatively dressed when we went out.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01227a -->
我们外出时，她总是穿得很传统。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01227a -->
<!-- USE:U01228 -->
## conservatively /kənˈsɝvətɪvli/ adv.
`估算时稳妥、偏低地，常与 estimated 等连用。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01228a USE:U01228 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01228a1 USE:U01228 -->
The bequest is conservatively estimated at £30 million.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01228a -->
这笔遗赠保守估计有三千万英镑。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01228a -->
<!-- USE:U01229 -->
## conservativeness /kənˈsɝvətɪvnəs/ n.
`保守、谨慎或避免改变的性质。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01229a USE:U01229 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01229a1 USE:U01229 -->
Her conservativeness made sudden changes unlikely.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01229a -->
她谨慎保守的性格，使突然改变不大可能发生。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01229a -->
<!-- USE:U01230 -->
## conservatory /kənˈsɝvəˌtɔri/ n.
`为栽培或展示植物而设计的玻璃房间、温室，可数，常与住宅相连。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01230r USE:U01230 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"走进不同气候的植物温室","sourceUrls":["https://www.kew.org/kew-gardens/whats-in-the-gardens/princess-of-wales-conservatory"],"displayContext":"Kew植物园馆所介绍缩编；仅取温室植物展示，不采用闭馆排期或照片。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01230r1 USE:U01230 -->
Explore the Princess of Wales Conservatory through its ten computer-controlled climate zones.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01230r2 -->
Move from the dry tropics into steamy areas filled with orchids and bromeliads.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01230r -->
沿着十个由电脑控制的气候区，探索威尔士王妃温室。从干燥的热带区域，走进兰花与凤梨科植物生长的湿热区域。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01230r -->
<!-- USE:U01231 -->
## conservatory /kənˈsɝvəˌtɔri/ n.
`以大窗户接收阳光、常与住宅相连的房间。在英式语境中可与 sun lounge 同义，不一定以种植物为主要用途。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01231a USE:U01231 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01231a1 USE:U01231 -->
They read in the conservatory, enjoying the light through its large windows.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01231a -->
他们在日光房里阅读，享受从大窗户透进的阳光。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01231a -->
<!-- USE:U01232 -->
## conservatory /kənˈsɝvəˌtɔri/ n.
`训练音乐家的学校或机构，这一用法尤其见于北美英语。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01232a USE:U01232 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01232a1 USE:U01232 -->
The brochure describes the New England Conservatory of Music.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01232a -->
这本手册介绍了新英格兰音乐学院。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01232a -->
<!-- USE:U01233 -->
## conservatoire /kənˈsɝvəˌtwɑr/ n.
`音乐或其他艺术学校，通常指欧洲的此类机构，可数，常见于校名。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01233a USE:U01233 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01233a1 USE:U01233 -->
The musician studied at the Paris Conservatoire.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01233a -->
这位音乐家曾在巴黎音乐学院学习。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01233a -->

<!-- WORD:W013 -->
# constrain

**词源**：经法语来自拉丁语 constringere“紧紧绑在一起”。由被绑住、行动受限，理解限制、强迫与不自在的表现；constraint 是相应的名词。

<!-- USE:U01301 -->
## constrain /kənˈstreɪn/ v.
`限制发展、行为或可选择的行动范围，属于正式用语，直接接受限制的对象；常用 be constrained by something 说明限制因素。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01301a USE:U01301 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01301a1 USE:U01301 -->
Many working parents are too often constrained by inflexible working hours.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01301a -->
许多有工作的家长常常受到僵化工作时间的限制。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01301a -->
<!-- USE:U01302 -->
## constrain /kənˈstreɪn/ v.
`以压力、必要性或不可抗拒的要求迫使某人行动，常用 constrain somebody to do something。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01302a USE:U01302 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01302a1 USE:U01302 -->
The shortage constrained them to postpone the work.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01302a -->
物资短缺迫使他们推迟了工作。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01302a -->
<!-- USE:U01303 -->
## constrain /kənˈstreɪn/ v.
`强行限制某人的身体自由，把人关押或禁闭；这里指实际限制自由，而非一般条件造成的行动限制。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01303a USE:U01303 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01303a1 USE:U01303 -->
The captives were constrained within the guarded enclosure.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01303a -->
俘虏被关押在有守卫的围栏内。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01303a -->
<!-- USE:U01304 -->
## constrain /kənˈstreɪn/ v.
`通过强迫促成某种行为或结果，直接以被迫作出的行为或结果为宾语，如 assent。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01304a USE:U01304 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01304a1 USE:U01304 -->
Threats constrained his assent rather than winning his agreement.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01304a -->
威胁迫使他表示赞同，却没有真正说服他。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01304a -->
<!-- USE:U01305 -->
## constrained /kənˈstreɪnd/ adj.
`在 feel constrained to do something 中，表示觉得自己不得不做某事，尽管本来不愿如此，常说明情势、礼节或内心压力造成的被迫感；to 后接动词原形。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01305a USE:U01305 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01305a1 USE:U01305 -->
For some reason he felt constrained to lower his voice.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01305a -->
不知为什么，他觉得自己不得不压低声音。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01305a -->
<!-- USE:U01306 -->
## constrained /kənˈstreɪnd/ adj.
`表现勉强、拘谨或不自然的，常描述 voice、manner 等；形容声音时不特指某种音高的假声。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01306a USE:U01306 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01306a1 USE:U01306 -->
He answered in a constrained voice.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01306a -->
他用拘谨而不自然的声音回答。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01306a -->
<!-- USE:U01307 -->
## constrainedly /kənˈstreɪnɪdli/ adv.
`拘谨、勉强或不自然地，描述 constrained 所表示的表现状态。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01307a USE:U01307 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01307a1 USE:U01307 -->
She smiled constrainedly during the awkward introduction.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01307a -->
在尴尬的介绍过程中，她勉强笑了笑。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01307a -->
<!-- USE:U01308 -->
## constraint /kənˈstreɪnt/ n.
`施加限制的行为、受限制的状态，或自由受到剥夺。可涉及行动控制或实际禁闭，泛指约束时常不可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01308a USE:U01308 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01308a1 USE:U01308 -->
The prisoners lived under constant constraint.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01308a -->
囚犯们一直处于自由受限的状态。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01308a -->
<!-- USE:U01309 -->
## constraint /kənˈstreɪnt/ n.
`限制行动或选择的某项条件或事物，可数，常用复数 constraints，如 financial constraints、constraints on something。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01309a USE:U01309 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01309a1 USE:U01309 -->
Their decision to abandon the trip was made because of financial constraints.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01309a -->
他们因为经费限制而决定放弃这次旅行。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01309a -->
<!-- USE:U01310 -->
## constraint /kənˈstreɪnt/ n.
`对自然感情或表达的克制、抑制，也指由此显出的拘谨，通常不可数；须与具体的预算等限制因素区分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01310a USE:U01310 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01310a1 USE:U01310 -->
There was a noticeable constraint in their conversation after the disagreement.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01310a -->
发生分歧后，他们的谈话显得明显拘谨。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01310a -->

<!-- WORD:W014 -->
# convention

**词源**：来自拉丁语 conventio“聚集、集会、约定”，与 convenire“走到一起、会合”有关。人聚在一起开会，或共同接受某种约定，解释了会议、协议、社会惯例之间的联系。

<!-- USE:U01401 -->
## convention /kənˈvenʃən/ n.
`多数人默契接受的行为准则或惯常做法，可数或不可数，尤其涉及礼貌、习俗；也可指刻板、形式化的惯例。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01401a USE:U01401 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01401a1 USE:U01401 -->
It's just a social convention that men don't wear skirts.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01401a -->
男人不穿裙子，只是一种社会习俗。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01401a -->
<!-- USE:U01402 -->
## convention /kənˈvenʃən/ n.
`艺术、文学或戏剧中传统采用的手法或风格，可数，常接 of。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01402a USE:U01402 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01402a1 USE:U01402 -->
The essay examines the stylistic conventions of Egyptian art.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01402a -->
这篇文章考察埃及艺术的风格惯例。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01402a -->
<!-- USE:U01403 -->
## convention /kənˈvenʃən/ n.
`群体或国家之间正式达成的协议，可数。国际用法常译作公约，有时指形式不如 treaty 正式的协议。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01403a USE:U01403 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01403a1 USE:U01403 -->
The discussion concerns the U.N. convention on climate change.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01403a -->
讨论涉及联合国气候变化公约。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01403a -->
<!-- USE:U01404 -->
## convention /kənˈvenʃən/ n.
`为共同目的召开的正式会议或组织大会，可数，如行业年会、政党大会。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01404a USE:U01404 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01404a1 USE:U01404 -->
She attended the annual convention of the Society of Professional Journalists.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01404a -->
她参加了职业新闻工作者协会的年会。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01404a -->
<!-- USE:U01405 -->
## convention /kənˈvenʃən/ n.
`美式政治用语，指政党代表为选定公职候选人而举行的大会。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01405r USE:U01405 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"1968年大会现场的无线报道","sourceUrls":["https://siarchives.si.edu/blog/science-service-technology-political-conventions"],"displayContext":"Smithsonian Archives，2016年历史文章；缩编1968年大会及器材部分，不采用照片。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01405r1 USE:U01405 -->
For television coverage of the 1968 national political conventions, NBC equipped four commentators with special backpacks.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01405r2 -->
The system let a reporter communicate wirelessly with a director in the booth.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01405r -->
为了报道1968年的全国政党代表大会，美国全国广播公司为四名评论员配备了特殊背包。这套系统使记者能够与转播间的导演无线通话。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01405r -->
<!-- USE:U01406 -->
## convention /kənˈvenʃən/ n.
`历史上，未经君主召集而举行的议会会议。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01406r USE:U01406 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"为何称作Convention","sourceUrls":["https://www.parliament.uk/about/living-heritage/evolutionofparliament/parliamentaryauthority/revolution/overview/invasiondesertion/"],"displayContext":"英国议会史资料，1688–1689年历史语境；对原段作简短缩编。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01406r1 USE:U01406 -->
William of Orange summoned a Convention of Lords and MPs to work out a political settlement.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01406r2 -->
It was not called a Parliament because the King had not summoned it.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01406r -->
奥兰治亲王威廉召集了由贵族与议员参加的国民公会，以商定政治安排。它没有被称为正式议会，因为并非由国王召集。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01406r -->
<!-- USE:U01407 -->
## convention /kənˈvenʃən/ n.
`牌手约定用来向同伴传递信息的叫牌、首攻等方法，常见于桥牌，不只限于叫牌。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01407a USE:U01407 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01407a1 USE:U01407 -->
In bridge, a bidding convention can convey information about a hand to a partner.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01407a -->
在桥牌中，约定叫可以向同伴传达一手牌的信息。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01407a -->
<!-- USE:U01408 -->
## convention /kənˈvenʃən/ n.
`把人员召集起来开会的行为，属于较正式的表达。`
