<!-- CONTEXT:CU01408a USE:U01408 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01408a1 USE:U01408 -->
The convention of the assembly required agreement on a meeting place.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01408a -->
召集大会需要先就开会地点取得一致。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01408a -->
<!-- USE:U01409 -->
## conventional /kənˈvenʃənəl/ adj.
`人的观念、行为符合社会通常习俗的；可说明遵循惯例，也可能带缺乏新意的评价。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01409a USE:U01409 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01409a1 USE:U01409 -->
The character is a respectable married woman with conventional opinions.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01409a -->
这个角色是一位体面的已婚女性，观念循规蹈矩。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01409a -->
<!-- USE:U01410 -->
## conventional /kənˈvenʃənəl/ adj.
`方法或产品按常规采用、沿用已久的，常放在名词前。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01410a USE:U01410 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01410a1 USE:U01410 -->
The report examines the risks and drawbacks of conventional family planning methods.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01410a -->
这份报告考察常规计划生育方法的风险和缺点。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01410a -->
<!-- USE:U01411 -->
## conventional /kənˈvenʃənəl/ adj.
`武器或战争不使用核爆炸物的，常修饰 weapons、arms、war；具体武备分类须依语境判断。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01411a USE:U01411 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01411a1 USE:U01411 -->
We must reduce the danger of war by controlling nuclear, chemical and conventional arms.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01411a -->
我们必须通过控制核武器、化学武器和常规武器来降低战争危险。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01411a -->
<!-- USE:U01412 -->
## conventional /kənˈvenʃənəl/ adj.
`动力并非来自核能的，用于动力系统等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01412a USE:U01412 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01412a1 USE:U01412 -->
The submarine uses conventional rather than nuclear power.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01412a -->
这艘潜艇使用常规动力，而非核动力。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01412a -->
<!-- USE:U01413 -->
## conventional /kənˈvenʃənəl/ adj.
`符号等具有共同约定、普遍认可的意义的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01413a USE:U01413 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01413a1 USE:U01413 -->
The symbol has a conventional meaning shared by the users of the map.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01413a -->
这个符号具有地图使用者共同约定的意义。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01413a -->
<!-- USE:U01414 -->
## conventional /kənˈvenʃənəl/ adj.
`表现只是循例应付、并非自发或真诚的，也可指缺乏独创性，如套式的礼貌表达。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01414a USE:U01414 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01414a1 USE:U01414 -->
His conventional expression of sympathy sounded insincere.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01414a -->
他那套式的同情表达听起来缺少诚意。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01414a -->
<!-- USE:U01415 -->
## conventional /kənˈvenʃənəl/ adj.
`艺术中，遵循既有传统程式而非直接依照自然形态的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01415a USE:U01415 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01415a1 USE:U01415 -->
The figures are conventional, following an inherited artistic pattern rather than nature.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01415a -->
这些人物形象采用因袭的画法，遵循传承的艺术程式，而非自然形态。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01415a -->
<!-- USE:U01416 -->
## conventional wisdom /kənˈvenʃənəl ˈwɪzdəm/ n.
`人们普遍接受的看法，通常用 the conventional wisdom about/that；普遍被接受并不保证看法正确。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01416a USE:U01416 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01416a1 USE:U01416 -->
The essay questions the conventional wisdom that soccer is a minor sport in America.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01416a -->
这篇文章质疑了足球在美国是一项小众运动的普遍看法。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01416a -->
<!-- USE:U01417 -->
## conventionalism /kənˈvenʃənəlɪzəm/ n.
`遵循惯例、拘守传统的态度或做法。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01417a USE:U01417 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01417a1 USE:U01417 -->
The critic objected to the conventionalism of the design.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01417a -->
评论者反对这一设计拘守惯例。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01417a -->
<!-- USE:U01418 -->
## conventionalist /kənˈvenʃənəlɪst/ n.
`遵循或维护惯例的人，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01418a USE:U01418 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01418a1 USE:U01418 -->
A conventionalist may prefer established forms to experimentation.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01418a -->
遵循惯例的人可能更喜欢既有形式，而不是实验。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01418a -->
<!-- USE:U01419 -->
## conventionality /kənˌvenʃəˈnæləti/ n.
`符合惯例的性质，或具体的一种惯常形式；复数形式为 conventionalities。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01419a USE:U01419 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01419a1 USE:U01419 -->
The conventionality of the ending disappointed her.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01419a -->
结局落入俗套，令她失望。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01419a -->
<!-- USE:U01420 -->
## conventionalize /kənˈvenʃənəlaɪz/ v.; conventionalise /kənˈvenʃənəlaɪz/ v.
`使某物符合惯例或成为约定形式，直接接所规范化的对象；conventionalise 是另一拼写。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01420a USE:U01420 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01420a1 USE:U01420 -->
Repeated use can conventionalize a once unfamiliar symbol.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01420a -->
反复使用可以使一个原本陌生的符号成为约定形式。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01420a -->
<!-- USE:U01421 -->
## conventionally /kənˈvenʃənəli/ adv.
`按社会惯例或通常方法，可说明着装、生产等行为的方式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01421a USE:U01421 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01421a1 USE:U01421 -->
People still wore their hair short and dressed conventionally.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01421a -->
人们仍然留着短发，穿着传统。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01421a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01421b USE:U01421 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01421b1 USE:U01421 -->
The farmer was fined for passing off conventionally-produced food as organic.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01421b -->
这位农民因把常规生产的食品冒充有机食品而被罚款。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01421b -->
<!-- USE:U01422 -->
## unconventional /ˌʌnkənˈvenʃənəl/ adj.
`人的态度或行为不按社会惯常方式的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01422a USE:U01422 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01422a1 USE:U01422 -->
He had rather unconventional work habits, preferring to work through the night.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01422a -->
他的工作习惯很不寻常，喜欢通宵工作。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01422a -->
<!-- USE:U01423 -->
## unconventional /ˌʌnkənˈvenʃənəl/ adj.
`做事方式不同寻常、可能令人意外的；这一性质本身不说明方法有效还是无效。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01423a USE:U01423 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01423a1 USE:U01423 -->
Despite his unconventional methods, he has inspired pupils more than anyone else.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01423a -->
尽管方法不循常规，他对学生的激励却超过其他任何人。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01423a -->
<!-- USE:U01424 -->
## unconventionalism /ˌʌnkənˈvenʃənəlɪzəm/ n.
`不受惯例约束的态度或做法。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01424a USE:U01424 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01424a1 USE:U01424 -->
The essay defends artistic unconventionalism.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01424a -->
这篇文章为艺术上不拘惯例的态度辩护。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01424a -->
<!-- USE:U01425 -->
## unconventionality /ˌʌnkənˌvenʃəˈnæləti/ n.
`不同寻常、不循惯例的性质。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01425a USE:U01425 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01425a1 USE:U01425 -->
The unconventionality of the arrangement attracted attention.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01425a -->
这种安排的不同寻常之处引起了关注。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01425a -->
<!-- USE:U01426 -->
## unconventionally /ˌʌnkənˈvenʃənəli/ adv.
`以不循惯例、不同于通常做法的方式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01426a USE:U01426 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01426a1 USE:U01426 -->
The musicians arranged the instruments unconventionally.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01426a -->
乐手们不按惯例布置乐器。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01426a -->

<!-- WORD:W015 -->
# convey

**词源**：经法语追溯到中世纪拉丁语中“伴随同行、沿路带送”的表达，含表示共同的成分和 via“道路”。从带送人或货物，联系到把意义、感情和产权传给另一方。

<!-- USE:U01501 -->
## convey /kənˈveɪ/ v.
`使信息、观点、意义或感情被别人知道、理解，常用 convey something to somebody；图像、作品等也可作主语，表示它们传达了某种感受或意义。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01501a USE:U01501 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01501a1 USE:U01501 -->
In every one of her pictures she conveys a sense of immediacy.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01501a -->
她的每一幅画都传达出一种现场感。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01501a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01501b USE:U01501 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01501b1 USE:U01501 -->
He also conveyed his views and the views of the bureaucracy.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01501b -->
他也转达了自己的看法及官僚机构的看法。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01501b -->
<!-- USE:U01502 -->
## convey /kənˈveɪ/ v.
`把人、货物等从一地载送到另一地，属于正式用语，直接接运送对象，常说明目的地或路线。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01502a USE:U01502 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01502a1 USE:U01502 -->
The railway company extended a branch line to Brightlingsea to convey fish direct to Billingsgate.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01502a -->
铁路公司把一条支线延伸到布赖特灵西，以便将鱼直接运到比林斯盖特。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01502a -->
<!-- USE:U01503 -->
## convey /kənˈveɪ/ v.
`法律上，把财产所有权转给另一方。宾语通常是财产或产权，须与仅把物品搬到别处区分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01503a USE:U01503 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01503a1 USE:U01503 -->
The deed conveyed the property to the buyer.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01503a -->
这份契据将房产所有权转给了买方。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01503a -->
<!-- USE:U01504 -->
## convey /kənˈveɪ/ v.
`把声音、气味等传导、传播出去，直接接所传送的内容；空气、管道或其他介质可以作主语。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01504a USE:U01504 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01504a1 USE:U01504 -->
The duct conveys sound from one room to another.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01504a -->
管道把声音从一个房间传到另一个房间。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01504a -->
<!-- USE:U01505 -->
## conveyable /kənˈveɪəbəl/ adj.
`能够被运送、传达或转让的，具体含义由所修饰的对象和语境决定。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01505a USE:U01505 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01505a1 USE:U01505 -->
These boxes are light enough to be conveyable by hand.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01505a -->
这些箱子足够轻，可以用手搬运。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01505a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01505b USE:U01505 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01505b1 USE:U01505 -->
Some details of the sound are not easily conveyable in words.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01505b -->
声音的某些细节不容易用文字传达。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01505b -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01505c USE:U01505 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01505c1 USE:U01505 -->
The agreement identifies which rights are conveyable and which must remain with the original holder.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01505c -->
协议列明了哪些权利可以转让，哪些必须仍由原持有人保留。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01505c -->
<!-- USE:U01506 -->
## conveyance /kənˈveɪəns/ n.
`把人或物从一地运到另一地的行为、过程，属于正式用语，通常不可数，常接 of。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01506a USE:U01506 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01506a1 USE:U01506 -->
The notice explains the conveyance of bicycles on Regional Railways trains.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01506a -->
这则通知说明了区域铁路列车载运自行车的安排。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01506a -->
<!-- USE:U01507 -->
## conveyance /kənˈveɪəns/ n.
`把思想、意义等表达、传给别人的过程，重在信息的传达。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01507a USE:U01507 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01507a1 USE:U01507 -->
A diagram can help with the conveyance of a complex idea.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01507a -->
示意图可以帮助传达复杂的想法。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01507a -->
<!-- USE:U01508 -->
## conveyance /kənˈveɪəns/ n.
`载送人或物的工具、车辆，可数；属于用来称呼车辆的文学性表达。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01508a USE:U01508 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01508a1 USE:U01508 -->
Mahoney had never seen such a conveyance before.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01508a -->
马奥尼以前从未见过这样的交通工具。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01508a -->
<!-- USE:U01509 -->
## conveyance /kənˈveɪəns/ n.
`财产所有权从一方转给另一方的法律行为。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01509a USE:U01509 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01509a1 USE:U01509 -->
The solicitor completed the conveyance of the house.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01509a -->
律师办妥了这所房屋的产权转让。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01509a -->
<!-- USE:U01510 -->
## conveyance /kənˈveɪəns/ n.
`使财产转让生效的法律文件，可数，须与产权转让行为本身区分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01510a USE:U01510 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01510a1 USE:U01510 -->
Both parties signed the conveyance.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01510a -->
双方都在产权转让契据上签了字。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01510a -->
<!-- USE:U01511 -->
## conveyancer /kənˈveɪənsər/ n.
`办理财产产权转让事务的人，可数，属于法律用语。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01511r USE:U01511 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"谁处理买卖合同","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/buying-a-home/transferring-ownership"],"displayContext":"GOV.UK购房指南缩编，保英国购房语境，不是本人的购房建议。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01511r1 USE:U01511 -->
If the seller has hired a solicitor or conveyancer, they will draft the initial contract and answer questions from your representative.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01511r2 -->
They may also negotiate the contract details.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01511r -->
如果卖方聘请了律师或产权转让事务人员，对方会起草初步合同，并回答买方代表提出的问题；必要时还会协商合同细节。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01511r -->
<!-- USE:U01512 -->
## conveyancing /kənˈveɪənsɪŋ/ n.
`办理财产产权转让的法律业务或过程，通常不可数，主要见于英式法律语境。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01512r USE:U01512 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"产权转让需要明确的合同内容","sourceUrls":["https://www.gov.uk/buying-a-home/transferring-ownership"],"displayContext":"GOV.UK Transferring ownership(conveyancing)，标题与合同段非连续缩编；英国购房指南。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01512r1 USE:U01512 -->
Conveyancing: once the offer is accepted, the seller is responsible for arranging a contract to transfer ownership.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01512r2 -->
Check what it says about the price, property boundaries and completion date.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01512r -->
办理产权转让时，一旦报价被接受，卖方就负责安排拟订转移所有权的合同。查看合同对价格、房产边界和交易完成日期的约定。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01512r -->
<!-- USE:U01513 -->
## conveyor /kənˈveɪər/ n.; conveyer /kənˈveɪər/ n.
`传送、传达的人或事物，也可指运送物品的设备，可数；conveyer 是 conveyor 的另一拼写。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01513a USE:U01513 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01513a1 USE:U01513 -->
A conveyor carries the packed boxes to the loading area.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01513a -->
一台传送装置把装好的箱子运到装货区。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01513a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01513b USE:U01513 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01513b1 USE:U01513 -->
She became a conveyor of news between the two villages.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01513b -->
她成了两个村庄之间传递消息的人。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01513b -->
<!-- USE:U01514 -->
## conveyance /kənˈveɪəns/ n.
`声音、信号等通过介质被传送的过程，强调物理传导，不一定涉及向人表达思想。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01514a USE:U01514 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01514a1 USE:U01514 -->
The material reduces the conveyance of sound through the wall.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01514a -->
这种材料减少了声音通过墙体的传导。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01514a -->

<!-- WORD:W016 -->
# cultivate

**词源**：追溯到拉丁语 colere“耕作”及其相关形式，后来发展出 cultivate。可以把耕地的持续照料联想到技能、心智与关系的培养；cultivation 是相应过程名词。

<!-- USE:U01601 -->
## cultivate /ˈkʌltəveɪt/ v.
`整理并使用土地，以便种植庄稼或经营园圃，直接接 land、soil、a garden 等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01601r USE:U01601 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"沙质土好耕作，也有弱点","sourceUrls":["https://www.rhs.org.uk/soil-composts-mulches/soil-types"],"displayContext":"RHS Soil types园艺说明，沙土段事实性缩编，不采用图片或完整操作指南。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01601r1 USE:U01601 -->
Sandy soils are easy to cultivate and warm up quickly in spring.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01601r2 -->
But they also dry out quickly, and rain can wash their nutrients away.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01601r -->
沙质土壤容易耕作，在春天也较快变暖。但它们也干得快，雨水还会把其中的养分冲走。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01601r -->
<!-- USE:U01602 -->
## cultivate /ˈkʌltəveɪt/ v.
`用耕耘机等打碎、翻松土壤，强调中耕、松土的具体作业，直接接所处理的土地。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01602a USE:U01602 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01602a1 USE:U01602 -->
The farmer cultivated the soil between the rows to remove weeds.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01602a -->
农民给行间的土壤松土，以清除杂草。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01602a -->
<!-- USE:U01603 -->
## cultivate /ˈkʌltəveɪt/ v.
`种植、培育农作物或园艺植物，直接接被栽培的作物等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01603a USE:U01603 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01603a1 USE:U01603 -->
They cultivate rice in the flooded fields.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01603a -->
他们在灌水的田里种水稻。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01603a -->
<!-- USE:U01604 -->
## cultivate /ˈkʌltəveɪt/ v.
`在适合的条件下使细菌、细胞等生物材料生长，即培养生物材料。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01604r USE:U01604 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"培养基要不要特意抑制某类菌","sourceUrls":["https://openstax.org/books/microbiology/pages/9-6-media-used-for-bacterial-growth"],"displayContext":"OpenStax《Microbiology》§9.6，缩编与中译，CC BY-NC-SA4.0；不是实验操作指导。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01604r1 USE:U01604 -->
Non-selective media can be used to cultivate organisms without specifically inhibiting particular microbes.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01604r2 -->
Mueller-Hinton agar is one example used in antibiotic testing.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01604r -->
非选择性培养基可用于培养生物，而不特意抑制某类微生物。用于抗生素试验的穆勒—辛顿琼脂便是一例。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01604r -->
<!-- USE:U01605 -->
## cultivate /ˈkʌltəveɪt/ v.
`着力发展心智、态度、技能、形象或举止，强调持续培养与改善，直接接所培养的素养。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01605a USE:U01605 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01605a1 USE:U01605 -->
Cultivating a positive mental attitude towards yourself can reap tremendous benefits.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01605a -->
培养积极看待自己的心态，能够带来很大益处。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01605a -->
<!-- USE:U01606 -->
## cultivate /ˈkʌltəveɪt/ v.
`主动结交、照顾某人或培养友谊、关系。可直接以人为宾语，也可用 cultivate a friendship/relationship；有时含为取得对方好感而刻意结交的意味。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01606a USE:U01606 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01606a1 USE:U01606 -->
He may be monarch one day so we must cultivate him.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01606a -->
他有朝一日可能成为君主，所以我们必须设法与他搞好关系。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01606a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01606b USE:U01606 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01606b1 USE:U01606 -->
The President has carefully cultivated relationships with at least five influential ministers.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01606b -->
总统一直悉心经营与至少五位有影响力的部长的关系。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01606b -->
<!-- USE:U01607 -->
## cultivable /ˈkʌltəvəbəl/ adj.; cultivatable /ˈkʌltəveɪtəbəl/ adj.
`可以耕作、栽培或培养的，具体对象由语境决定；cultivable 和 cultivatable 是相应的两种形式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01607a USE:U01607 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01607a1 USE:U01607 -->
Only a narrow strip of the hillside is cultivable.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01607a -->
这片山坡只有一条狭窄地带可以耕作。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01607a -->
<!-- USE:U01608 -->
## cultivated /ˈkʌltəveɪtɪd/ adj.
`受过良好教育、有文化修养和优雅举止的，属于描述人的素养的正式用语。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01608a USE:U01608 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01608a1 USE:U01608 -->
His mother was an elegant, cultivated woman.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01608a -->
他的母亲是一位举止优雅、很有修养的女性。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01608a -->
<!-- USE:U01609 -->
## cultivated /ˈkʌltəveɪtɪd/ adj.
`经人耕作、培育的，而非野生或未开发的，可形容土地、植物或品种。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01609a USE:U01609 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01609a1 USE:U01609 -->
They crossed the few patches of cultivated land.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01609a -->
他们穿过了那几小片耕地。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01609a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01609b USE:U01609 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01609b1 USE:U01609 -->
The collection includes a mixture of wild and cultivated varieties.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01609b -->
这批收藏中既有野生品种，也有栽培品种。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01609b -->
<!-- USE:U01610 -->
## cultivation /ˌkʌltəˈveɪʃən/ n.
`耕作土地、栽培作物的过程，通常不可数。cultivation of crops 指作物栽培，land under cultivation 指投入耕作的土地；须与培养细菌、细胞等的生物学用法区分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01610a USE:U01610 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01610a1 USE:U01610 -->
The book covers the cultivation of fruits and vegetables.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01610a -->
这本书介绍水果和蔬菜的栽培。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01610a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01610b USE:U01610 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01610b1 USE:U01610 -->
Farmers with many acres under cultivation profited.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01610b -->
有许多英亩耕地投入耕作的农民获得了收益。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01610b -->
<!-- USE:U01611 -->
## cultivation /ˌkʌltəˈveɪʃən/ n.
`着力培养某种心智、态度、能力或关系的过程，通常不可数，常接 of。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01611a USE:U01611 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01611a1 USE:U01611 -->
The discussion concerns the cultivation of a positive approach to life and health.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01611a -->
讨论涉及如何培养积极对待生活与健康的态度。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01611a -->
<!-- USE:U01612 -->
## cultivator /ˈkʌltəveɪtər/ n.
`从事耕种、栽培或培养工作的人或事物，可数，强调执行耕作、培养的主体。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01612a USE:U01612 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01612a1 USE:U01612 -->
Small cultivators sell part of their harvest at the local market.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01612a -->
小农户把部分收成拿到当地市场出售。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01612a -->
<!-- USE:U01613 -->
## cultivator /ˈkʌltəveɪtər/ n.
`打碎土块、翻松土地或清除杂草的工具、机械，可数，指农业或园艺设备。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01613a USE:U01613 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01613a1 USE:U01613 -->
The cultivator loosens the top layer of soil.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01613a -->
这台耕耘机翻松表层土壤。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01613a -->
<!-- USE:U01614 -->
## cultivation /ˌkʌltəˈveɪʃən/ n.
`生物学中，在受控条件下培养细菌、细胞等生物材料的过程，通常不可数，如 cultivation of bacteria/cells；与耕地栽培区分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01614a USE:U01614 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[],"displayContext":"为辨别本用法构造的例句，不是实验操作指导。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01614a1 USE:U01614 -->
Cultivation of the bacteria requires a suitable nutrient medium.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01614a -->
培养这些细菌需要适当的营养培养基。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01614a -->

<!-- WORD:W017 -->
# diminish

**词源**：英语形式由两个表示“使变小”的早期动词融合而来，最终联系到拉丁语 minuere“使小”。记住“减小”这条主线，再区分实际减少和把人的价值说小。

<!-- USE:U01701 -->
## diminish /dɪˈmɪnɪʃ/ v.
`使大小、数量、重要性或强度变小，或自身减少，可及物或不及物，也可涉及人的实际权势、影响力或声誉。diminishing 表示正在减少，diminished 表示已降低的状态。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01701r USE:U01701 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"减轻损伤显现出的程度","sourceUrls":["https://www.getty.edu/news/artist-dialogues-getty-conservation-lita-albuquerque-malibu-line/"],"displayContext":"Getty，Lilibeth Garcia，2024年文章开头说明的缩编；不是修复操作指南。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01701r1 USE:U01701 -->
Art conservation has traditionally aimed to keep original materials intact for longer and diminish the appearance of damage.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01701r2 -->
This helps museum visitors see works made centuries ago.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01701r -->
传统的艺术品保存工作，旨在让原材料更长久地保存，并减轻损伤的外观。这使博物馆观众仍能看到数百年前创作的作品。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01701r -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01701a USE:U01701 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01701a1 USE:U01701 -->
Universities are facing grave problems because of diminishing resources.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01701a -->
资源不断减少，使大学面临严重问题。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01701a -->
<!-- USE:U01702 -->
## diminish /dɪˈmɪnɪʃ/ v.
`以言语或待遇使某人、某事显得不如实际重要，直接接被贬低的对象，如 diminish somebody；不只指客观数量变小。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01702a USE:U01702 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01702a1 USE:U01702 -->
He never put her down or diminished her.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01702a -->
他从不奚落或贬低她。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01702a -->
<!-- USE:U01703 -->
## diminishable /dɪˈmɪnɪʃəbəl/ adj.
`能够被减少、削弱的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01703a USE:U01703 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01703a1 USE:U01703 -->
The intensity is diminishable by adjusting the control.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01703a -->
调节控制器可以减弱其强度。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01703a -->
<!-- USE:U01704 -->
## diminution /ˌdɪməˈnuʃən/ n.
`大小、重要性或强度减少的行为或实例，属于正式用语，常用 diminution in/of something。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01704a USE:U01704 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01704a1 USE:U01704 -->
They continued the project despite a slight diminution in asset value.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01704a -->
尽管资产价值略有下降，他们仍继续推进项目。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01704a -->
<!-- USE:U01705 -->
## diminution /ˌdɪməˈnuʃən/ n.
`某事物减少的那一部分数量或程度，强调减去多少，而不只是减少过程。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01705a USE:U01705 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01705a1 USE:U01705 -->
The diminution amounted to one tenth of the original volume.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01705a -->
减少量相当于原体积的十分之一。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01705a -->
<!-- USE:U01706 -->
## diminution /ˌdɪməˈnuʃən/ n.
`音乐中，重复一个乐段时改用比原来更短的音符时值，即减值；改变的是音符时值，而非音量。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01706a USE:U01706 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU01706a1 USE:U01706 -->
The second voice repeats the theme in diminution, using notes half as long.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01706a -->
第二声部以减值方式重复主题，音符时值缩短为原来的一半。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01706a -->

<!-- WORD:W018 -->
# discriminate

**词源**：来自拉丁语中表示区分的词，最终联系到 discernere“辨明、分开”。先记住“看出并作出区别”，再区分中性的辨别、褒义的鉴赏与不公平的差别待遇。

<!-- USE:U01801 -->
## discriminate /dɪˈskrɪməneɪt/ v.
`看出、辨明两者的差别。常用不及物结构 discriminate between A and B，也可用及物结构 discriminate A from B。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01801a USE:U01801 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01801a USE:U01801 -->
He is incapable of discriminating between a good idea and a terrible one.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01801a -->
他分不清一个好主意和一个糟糕主意。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01801a -->
<!-- USE:U01802 -->
## discriminate /dɪˈskrɪməneɪt/ v.
`因群体、种族、年龄或性别等作区别待遇，尤指不公平待遇。discriminate against somebody 指使其受到不利待遇，discriminate in favour of somebody 指给予优待；具体公平性依语境判断，不能把辨别颜色等中性用法译成社会歧视。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01802a USE:U01802 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01802a USE:U01802 -->
They believe the law discriminates against women.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01802a -->
他们认为这项法律歧视女性。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01802a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01802b USE:U01802 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01802b USE:U01802 -->
They proposed legislation which would discriminate in favour of racial minorities.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01802b -->
他们提出了会给予少数族群优待的立法。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01802b -->
<!-- USE:U01803 -->
## discriminate /dɪˈskrɪməneɪt/ v.
`成为把一者与另一者区分开的特征。常用 discriminate A from B，主语可以是构成区别的特征。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01803a USE:U01803 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01803a USE:U01803 -->
Its unusual roofline discriminates this building from the others.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01803a -->
与众不同的屋顶轮廓使这栋建筑区别于其他建筑。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01803a -->
<!-- USE:U01804 -->
## discriminate /dɪˈskrɪməneɪt/ v.
`细察区别，表现出良好的判断、鉴赏能力。不及物，强调识别品质。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01804a USE:U01804 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01804a USE:U01804 -->
When buying old furniture, learn to discriminate: a fresh coat of paint can hide poor construction.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01804a -->
购买旧家具时，要学会鉴别品质：新刷的一层漆可能掩盖粗劣的结构。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01804a -->
<!-- USE:U01805 -->
## discriminately /dɪˈskrɪmənətli/ adv.
`有区别、有选择地，强调并非一律或盲目处理。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01805a USE:U01805 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01805a USE:U01805 -->
The editor selected the photographs discriminately.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01805a -->
编辑有选择地挑选了这些照片。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01805a -->
<!-- USE:U01806 -->
## discriminative /dɪˈskrɪmənətɪv/ adj.
`具有辨别能力、用来作区别的，强调识别、区分的功能。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01806a USE:U01806 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01806a USE:U01806 -->
The task tests the ear's discriminative ability.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01806a -->
这项任务测试耳朵的辨别能力。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01806a -->
<!-- USE:U01807 -->
## discriminator /dɪˈskrɪməneɪtər/ n.
`辨别差异的人或执行辨别功能的事物，可数，可指人，也可指装置等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01807a USE:U01807 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01807a USE:U01807 -->
The circuit includes a discriminator that separates signals by frequency.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01807a -->
这个电路包含一个按频率区分信号的鉴别器。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01807a -->
<!-- USE:U01808 -->
## discriminatory /dɪˈskrɪmənəˌtɔri/ adj.
`不公正地给予某一群体较差待遇的，常修饰 laws、practices 等，具有负面评价。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01808a USE:U01808 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01808a USE:U01808 -->
The rule was challenged as a discriminatory hiring practice.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01808a -->
这项规定被质疑是一种带有歧视性的招聘做法。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01808a -->
<!-- USE:U01809 -->
## discriminating /dɪˈskrɪməneɪtɪŋ/ adj.
`能够细察差别、识别优良品质的，通常为褒义，重在鉴赏、品位；与表示歧视性的 discriminatory 不同。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01809a USE:U01809 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01809a USE:U01809 -->
A discriminating buyer looks beyond the label and checks how the jacket is made.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01809a -->
有鉴赏力的买家不会只看标签，还会检查夹克的做工。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01809a -->
<!-- USE:U01810 -->
## discriminatingly /dɪˈskrɪməneɪtɪŋli/ adv.
`以敏锐鉴别、具有良好品位的方式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01810a USE:U01810 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01810a USE:U01810 -->
She chose the fabrics discriminatingly, noticing subtle differences in texture.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01810a -->
她挑选布料时很有眼力，注意到了细微的质感差异。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01810a -->
<!-- USE:U01811 -->
## discrimination /dɪˌskrɪməˈneɪʃən/ n.
`基于偏见，对某人或群体作不利、不公平的区别待遇，通常不可数，如 discrimination against somebody、sex/racial discrimination。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01811a USE:U01811 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"Brown II：确认宪法原则","sourceUrls":["https://www.law.cornell.edu/supremecourt/text/349/294"],"displayContext":"1955年美国最高法院判决；以下为判决局部缩编。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01811a USE:U01811 -->
In Brown II (1955), the U.S. Supreme Court reaffirmed that racial discrimination in public education was unconstitutional and that laws requiring or permitting it had to yield to that principle.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01811a -->
在1955年的布朗案第二次判决中，美国最高法院重申：公立教育中的种族歧视违反宪法，要求或允许这种歧视的法律必须服从这一原则。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01811a -->
<!-- USE:U01812 -->
## discrimination /dɪˌskrɪməˈneɪʃən/ n.
`辨别品质优劣的品位或判断能力，通常不可数；用于艺术、饮食等语境时不指歧视。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01812a USE:U01812 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01812a USE:U01812 -->
They cooked without skill and ate without discrimination.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01812a -->
他们做饭没有技巧，吃东西也毫无鉴赏力。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01812a -->
<!-- USE:U01813 -->
## discrimination /dɪˌskrɪməˈneɪʃən/ n.
`识别、理解事物之间差异的能力，如 colour discrimination 指颜色辨别能力。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01813a USE:U01813 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01813a USE:U01813 -->
The test measures colour discrimination.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01813a -->
这项测试测量颜色辨别能力。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01813a -->
<!-- USE:U01814 -->
## discrimination /dɪˌskrɪməˈneɪʃən/ n.
`在思想或行动中作出的一项区别，强调区分本身，不必带有不公平评价。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01814a USE:U01814 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01814a USE:U01814 -->
The argument depends on a discrimination between evidence and assumption.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01814a -->
这个论证以区分证据与假设为基础。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01814a -->
<!-- USE:U01815 -->
## indiscriminate /ˌɪndɪˈskrɪmənət/ adj.
`不作必要区分、缺乏仔细选择或考虑的，常含批评意味，可描述行动、使用方式等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01815a USE:U01815 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01815a USE:U01815 -->
The indiscriminate use of fertilisers is damaging to the environment.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01815a -->
不加选择地使用肥料正在损害环境。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01815a -->
<!-- USE:U01816 -->
## indiscriminate /ˌɪndɪˈskrɪmənət/ adj.
`各种事物混杂、没有清楚选择或整理的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01816a USE:U01816 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01816a USE:U01816 -->
An indiscriminate heap of papers covered the desk.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01816a -->
一堆杂乱的纸张铺满了桌子。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01816a -->
<!-- USE:U01817 -->
## indiscriminately /ˌɪndɪˈskrɪmənətli/ adv.
`不加区别或选择地，常含批评意味。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01817a USE:U01817 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01817a USE:U01817 -->
Do not delete files indiscriminately; check which ones are still needed.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01817a -->
不要不加区分地删除文件；先检查哪些文件仍然需要。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01817a -->
<!-- USE:U01818 -->
## indiscriminateness /ˌɪndɪˈskrɪmənətnəs/ n.; indiscrimination /ˌɪndɪskrɪməˈneɪʃən/ n.
`不加区别、缺乏选择的性质或行为；须由语境明确缺少哪一种必要区分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01818a USE:U01818 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01818a USE:U01818 -->
The weakness of the process is its indiscriminateness: every application is treated as equally suitable.
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01818a2 USE:U01818 -->
Such indiscrimination can let unsuitable applications through.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01818a -->
这个流程的缺点在于不加区分：每份申请都被视为同样合适。这样的无差别处理可能让不合适的申请通过。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01818a -->
<!-- USE:U01819 -->
## indiscriminative /ˌɪndɪˈskrɪmənətɪv/ adj.
`缺乏辨别或选择能力、不作区分的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01819a USE:U01819 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01819a USE:U01819 -->
An indiscriminative approach treats unlike cases as though they were identical.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01819a -->
一种缺乏辨别的做法会把不同情况当成完全相同来处理。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01819a -->
<!-- USE:U01820 -->
## discriminating /dɪˈskrɪməneɪtɪŋ/ adj.
`能敏锐地分辨细微差异的，侧重观察或感知的细致，不必涉及物品品质的好坏。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01820a USE:U01820 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01820a USE:U01820 -->
The task requires a discriminating ear: the two notes differ only slightly in pitch.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01820a -->
这项任务需要敏锐的耳朵：两个音的音高只有细微差别。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01820a -->

<!-- WORD:W019 -->
# diverse

**词源**：经法语来自拉丁语 diversus，原有“朝不同方向转开、彼此不同”的意思。diverse 与 divers 历史同源，后来拼写、读音与常用意义分化；diversity 是多样性，diversify 是使之多样。

<!-- USE:U01901 -->
## diverse /daɪˈvɝs/ adj.
`一组事物由许多不同种类或特征的成员组成的，如 a diverse range，强调群体内部的多样性。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01901a USE:U01901 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"雀鸟喙的多样性","sourceUrls":["https://www.nhm.ac.uk/discover/what-is-natural-selection.html"],"displayContext":"自然历史博物馆介绍；局部保真缩编。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01901a USE:U01901 -->
The Galápagos finches have a diverse array of beak sizes and shapes. These differences correspond to different foods and isolation on separate islands.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01901a -->
加拉帕戈斯群岛的雀鸟有各种大小和形状的喙。这些差异与不同的食物来源及不同岛屿上的隔离相对应。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01901a -->
<!-- USE:U01902 -->
## diverse /daɪˈvɝs/ adj.
`若干人或事物在性质、特点上彼此不同的，强调成员之间有差异。美式英语也可读 /dɪˈvɝs/。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01902a USE:U01902 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"材料与观念不同，保护方法也不同","sourceUrls":["https://www.getty.edu/news/artist-dialogues-getty-conservation-lita-albuquerque-malibu-line/"],"displayContext":"Getty Conservation Institute项目介绍；局部缩编。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01902a USE:U01902 -->
Art from the twentieth century onward is diverse in both its materials and its ideas, so conserving it requires different approaches.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01902a -->
二十世纪以来的艺术，其材料和观念都各不相同，因此保护这些作品需要不同的方法。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01902a -->
<!-- USE:U01903 -->
## diversely /daɪˈvɝsli/ adv.
`以不同方式或呈现不同特征地。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01903a USE:U01903 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01903a USE:U01903 -->
The same theme is diversely expressed in the three works.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01903a -->
同一主题在这三部作品中得到了不同表达。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01903a -->
<!-- USE:U01904 -->
## diversity /daɪˈvɝsəti/ n.
`包含许多不同成分或特点的性质，可数或不可数，如 cultural/linguistic diversity。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01904a USE:U01904 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"栖息地多样性与群落","sourceUrls":["https://www.usgs.gov/publications/coastal-habitat-and-biological-community-response-dam-removal-elwha-river"],"displayContext":"2017年埃尔瓦河拆坝研究摘要；不代表读过论文全文。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01904a USE:U01904 -->
Habitat diversity helps shape ecological communities. Building or removing dams can change habitats and the biological communities associated with them.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01904a -->
栖息地的多样性有助于塑造生态群落。修建或拆除水坝会改变栖息地，以及与之相关的生物群落。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01904a -->
<!-- USE:U01905 -->
## diversity /daɪˈvɝsəti/ n.
`有意识地把不同种族、性别、宗教等背景的人纳入群体或活动的实践与目标，通常不可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01905a USE:U01905 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01905a USE:U01905 -->
To promote diversity, the committee actively invites applicants from different ethnic, religious, and social backgrounds.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01905a -->
为促进多元参与，委员会主动邀请不同族裔、宗教和社会背景的人申请。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01905a -->
<!-- USE:U01906 -->
## diversity /daɪˈvɝsəti/ n.
`彼此不同的一系列事物，或其中一种不同类别。a diversity of something 表示多种事物；复数 diversities 也可指不同种类或差异。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01906a USE:U01906 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01906a USE:U01906 -->
His object is to gather as great a diversity of material as possible.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01906a -->
他的目标是尽可能收集种类丰富的材料。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01906a -->
<!-- USE:U01907 -->
## diversify /daɪˈvɝsəfaɪ/ v.
`使事物更多样，扩大所做、所生产或所涉及的种类，可及物或不及物。diversify one's markets 表示拓展多种市场，diversify into new products 表示开展新产品业务。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01907a USE:U01907 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01907a USE:U01907 -->
The company's troubles started only when it diversified into new products.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01907a -->
这家公司的问题是在它扩展到新产品之后才出现的。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01907a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01907b USE:U01907 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01907b USE:U01907 -->
These firms have been given a tough lesson in the need to diversify their markets.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01907b -->
这些企业受到了一次严厉教训，认识到必须拓展多种市场。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01907b -->
<!-- USE:U01908 -->
## diversify /daɪˈvɝsəfaɪ/ v.
`商业中，把投资分布到多家企业或多种产品，或在企业中引入多方面投资，常以降低单一投资损失的影响为目的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01908a USE:U01908 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01908a USE:U01908 -->
The fund diversified its investments across several industries.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01908a -->
这只基金把投资分散到了几个行业。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01908a -->
<!-- USE:U01909 -->
## diversification /daɪˌvɝsəfɪˈkeɪʃən/ n.
`使业务、产品、活动或投资变得更多样的过程或实例，可数或不可数，具体对象由上下文决定。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01909a USE:U01909 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01909a USE:U01909 -->
The seminar was to discuss diversification of agriculture.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01909a -->
这次研讨会原计划讨论农业多样化。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01909a -->
<!-- USE:U01910 -->
## divers /ˈdaɪvərz/ adj.
`种种的、各式各样的、好几个的，属于古义或文学用语。diverse 也有首音节重读的读法，但 divers 词尾读 /z/，两者的现代用法不同。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU01910a USE:U01910 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU01910a USE:U01910 -->
“Divers towns” means several different towns; the old spelling is not the plural of “diver” in this use.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU01910a -->
“Divers towns”意为好几个不同的城镇；这里的旧词形不是diver（潜水员）的复数。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU01910a -->

<!-- WORD:W020 -->
# emerge

**词源**：来自拉丁语 emergere，表示“从里面出来、浮起”；e- 表示向外，mergere 表示浸入。由浮出水面或从遮蔽处出来，联系到事实显露、走出困境与新事物兴起。

<!-- USE:U02001 -->
## emerge /ɪˈmɝdʒ/ v.
`从封闭、黑暗或被遮住的位置出来，进入视野。不及物，常接 from，可指人走出车辆、昆虫从内部羽化而出等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02001a USE:U02001 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"从干燥区走进湿热区","sourceUrls":["https://www.kew.org/kew-gardens/whats-in-the-gardens/princess-of-wales-conservatory"],"displayContext":"Kew温室页面于旧读件取得；展示游览语言，不承诺当前开放安排。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02001a USE:U02001 -->
Travel through the dry tropics, then emerge into steamy zones filled with tropical orchids and bromeliads.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02001a -->
穿过干燥热带区，随后走进水汽蒸腾、长着热带兰花和凤梨科植物的展区。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02001a -->
<!-- USE:U02002 -->
## emerge /ɪˈmɝdʒ/ v.
`从液体中露出、浮出。不及物，强调此前处于液体之中。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02002a USE:U02002 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02002a USE:U02002 -->
The seal emerged from the water beside the rocks.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02002a -->
海豹在岩石旁从水里冒了出来。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02002a -->
<!-- USE:U02003 -->
## emerge /ɪˈmɝdʒ/ v.
`结束或经历某段困难时期，并以某种结果存留下来。emerge from an ordeal/recession 表示从磨难或衰退中走出，也可接 unscathed 等形容词说明经历后的状态。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02003a USE:U02003 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02003a USE:U02003 -->
There is growing evidence that the economy is at last emerging from recession.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02003a -->
越来越多的证据表明，经济终于正在走出衰退。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02003a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02003b USE:U02003 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02003b USE:U02003 -->
They emerged unscathed.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02003b -->
他们安然无恙地挺了过来。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02003b -->
<!-- USE:U02004 -->
## emerge /ɪˈmɝdʒ/ v.
`事实、结果、问题或情况变得明显、为人所知，常发生在调查、讨论之后。不及物，可由事实、困难等作主语，也可用 it emerges that... 引出所发现的情况。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02004a USE:U02004 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02004a USE:U02004 -->
It soon emerged that neither the July nor August mortgage repayment had been collected.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02004a -->
很快便发现，七月和八月的按揭还款都没有收取。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02004a -->
<!-- USE:U02005 -->
## emerge /ɪˈmɝdʒ/ v.
`变得受认可、突出，或被认作某种重要角色，常见于新闻语境，如 emerge as a leader/contender。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02005a USE:U02005 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02005a USE:U02005 -->
She emerged as a leading contender.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02005a -->
她崭露头角，成为主要竞争者。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02005a -->
<!-- USE:U02006 -->
## emerge /ɪˈmɝdʒ/ v.
`组织、产业等开始存在、发展出来，常见于新闻语境，可由新国家、新产业等作主语。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02006a USE:U02006 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"一种政治运动的兴起","sourceUrls":["https://history.state.gov/milestones/1953-1960/bandung-conf"],"displayContext":"1955年万隆会议的历史介绍；网页已停止维护。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02006a USE:U02006 -->
The United States initially viewed the Bandung Conference and the nonaligned movement that emerged from it with caution.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02006a -->
美国最初对万隆会议及由此兴起的不结盟运动持谨慎态度。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02006a -->
<!-- USE:U02007 -->
## emerging /ɪˈmɝdʒɪŋ/ adj.
`正在显露、产生或发展壮大的，由 emerge 的分词作形容词。具体发展阶段依语境判断，如 emerging industries 指正在兴起的产业。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02007a USE:U02007 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"新兴国家获得发声机会","sourceUrls":["https://history.state.gov/milestones/1953-1960/bandung-conf"],"displayContext":"1955年历史语境；局部缩编。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02007a USE:U02007 -->
Bandung gave emerging nations a voice and showed that they could influence world politics without fitting neatly into the Cold War camps.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02007a -->
万隆会议让新兴国家发出了自己的声音，并显示它们即使不完全归入冷战阵营，也能影响世界政治。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02007a -->
<!-- USE:U02008 -->
## emergence /ɪˈmɝdʒəns/ n.
`出现、显露或产生的过程、事件，通常不可数，常接 of，可用于可见事物或新力量、新情况的出现。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02008a USE:U02008 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02008a USE:U02008 -->
The study examines the emergence of global players.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02008a -->
这项研究考察了全球性重要参与者的出现。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02008a -->
<!-- USE:U02009 -->
## emergent /ɪˈmɝdʒənt/ adj.
`正在出现、形成或获得力量的，主要见于书面语，通常置于名词前。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02009a USE:U02009 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02009a USE:U02009 -->
The report describes an emergent nationalist movement.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02009a -->
这份报告描述了一场新兴的民族主义运动。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02009a -->
<!-- USE:U02010 -->
## emergent /ɪˈmɝdʒənt/ adj.
`国家刚成立或新取得独立的，描述国家形成的阶段。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02010a USE:U02010 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02010a USE:U02010 -->
The novel is set in an emergent state, newly independent and still building its institutions.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02010a -->
小说的背景是一个刚刚独立、仍在建立制度的新国家。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02010a -->

<!-- WORD:W021 -->
# enhance

**词源**：经古法语表示“提高、抬高”的词进入英语，历史词根联系到拉丁语 altus“高”。由“使更高”联系到提升已有的质量、力量或价值。

<!-- USE:U02101 -->
## enhance /ɪnˈhæns/ v.
`提高或加强某物的质量、力量、价值或吸引力，常使已有的好品质更突出。直接接所改善的对象，如 enhance a reputation、enhance the value。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02101a USE:U02101 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"LTA | Improved Bus Connectivity for Commuters with More Services and Enhancements","sourceUrls":["https://www.lta.gov.sg/content/ltagov/en/newsroom/2026/9/news-releases/improved-bus-connectivity-for-commuters-with-more-services-and-e.html"],"displayContext":"新加坡陆路交通管理局2026年9月20日公告；以下仍保留公告当时的将来时。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02101a USE:U02101 -->
On 20 September 2026, Singapore's Land Transport Authority announced that it would enhance two existing bus services in the coming months.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02101a -->
2026年9月20日，新加坡陆路交通管理局宣布，将在此后的几个月改善两条现有公交线路。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02101a -->
<!-- USE:U02102 -->
## enhancement /ɪnˈhænsmənt/ n.
`质量、价值或吸引力的改善、增强，或一次这样的改进，属于正式用语，可数或不可数，常接 of。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02102a USE:U02102 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"LTA | Improved Bus Connectivity for Commuters with More Services and Enhancements","sourceUrls":["https://www.lta.gov.sg/content/ltagov/en/newsroom/2026/9/news-releases/improved-bus-connectivity-for-commuters-with-more-services-and-e.html"],"displayContext":"同一公交公告中的服务改进：新增线路并改善既有线路。将原长句拆为两句，仍为当时计划。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02102a USE:U02102 -->
Singapore's Land Transport Authority announced new bus services and improvements to existing ones on 20 September 2026; these enhancements would give commuters more travel options.
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02102a2 -->
They would improve connectivity in areas such as Tengah and Woodlands.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02102a -->
2026年9月20日，新加坡陆路交通管理局宣布将新增公交线路并改善既有线路；这些改进将为通勤者提供更多出行选择，并改善登加和兀兰等地区的交通衔接。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02102a -->
<!-- USE:U02103 -->
## enhancer /ɪnˈhænsər/ n.
`使某物外观、味道或感受更好的物质或设备，可数，常与前一名词连用，如 flavour enhancer。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02103a USE:U02103 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02103a USE:U02103 -->
Cinnamon is an excellent flavour enhancer.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02103a -->
肉桂是一种出色的增味材料。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02103a -->

<!-- WORD:W022 -->
# equity

**词源**：经法语来自拉丁语 aequitas，联系到 aequus“平、相等、公正”。从公平原则理解一般公平与衡平法；金融中的 equity 则须按具体对象理解为权益或扣除债务后的净值。

<!-- USE:U02201 -->
## equity /ˈekwəti/ n.
`公平、合理地给予各方待遇的性质，通常不可数，如 social justice and equity。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02201a USE:U02201 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"117th Congress House Manual, Rule XVII: Decorum and Debate","sourceUrls":["https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/HMAN-117/html/HMAN-117-pg776.htm"],"displayContext":"美国第117届众议院手册中的特定议事程序；缩编只说明该版本规定的情况。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02201a USE:U02201 -->
Under the U.S. House Manual for the 117th Congress, the Chair prorates the time remaining before 10 p.m. for the specified late-starting special-order speeches to maintain equity between the parties.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02201a -->
按美国第117届众议院手册，对于其中规定的较晚开始的特别发言，主持人按比例分配晚上十点之前的剩余时间，以维持各党之间的公平。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02201a -->
<!-- USE:U02202 -->
## equity /ˈekwəti/ n.
`法律中，运用一般公正原则来纠正或补充普通法律规则的衡平法体系，须与一般道德意义上的公平区分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02202a USE:U02202 KIND:excerpt -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"Brown II, 349 U.S. 294","sourceUrls":["https://www.law.cornell.edu/supremecourt/text/349/294"],"displayContext":"1955年Brown II判决讨论如何落实废除公立学校种族隔离的裁定；仅摘其衡平法救济表述。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02202a USE:U02202 -->
Traditionally, equity has been characterized by a practical flexibility in shaping its remedies.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02202a -->
传统上，衡平法的一个特点，是能够灵活地设计切实可行的救济方式。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02202a -->
<!-- USE:U02203 -->
## equity /ˈekwəti/ n.
`金融、商业中，资产价值扣除相关债务、负担后的净值，通常不可数。房屋 equity 尤指市值扣除未清按揭等负担后属于拥有者的部分；capture one's equity 指把净权益变现，例如出售房屋或再融资，而非说账面权益就是随时可取的现金。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02203a USE:U02203 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"What is negative equity and why it could be a problem","sourceUrls":["https://www.moneyhelper.org.uk/en/homes/buying-a-home/negative-equity-what-it-means-and-what-you-can-do-about-it"],"displayContext":"英国MoneyHelper住房指南的概念说明；缩编不构成个别贷款建议。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02203a USE:U02203 -->
Your equity is the current value of your property minus what you still owe on your mortgage.
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02203a2 USE:U02203 -->
It can grow as you repay the mortgage or as the property’s value rises.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02203a -->
你的房屋净权益，是房屋现值减去尚欠的按揭贷款。随着你偿还按揭，或房屋价值上涨，这部分净权益可能增加。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02203a -->
<!-- USE:U02204 -->
## equity /ˈekwəti/ n.
`公司已发行股份所代表的价值或所有者权益，属于商业用语，可与债务融资等区别。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02204a USE:U02204 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02204a USE:U02204 -->
The company raised money by issuing equity rather than taking out a loan.
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02204a2 USE:U02204 -->
The new investors received a share of the business.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02204a -->
公司通过发行股权筹集资金，而不是借款。新投资者获得了企业的一部分所有权。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02204a -->
<!-- USE:U02205 -->
## equities /ˈekwətiz/ n.
`股票，尤其指收益并非固定利息的股权投资。用复数，属于商业用语，不等同于固定利息债券。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02205a USE:U02205 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"Stocks","sourceUrls":["https://www.investor.gov/introduction-investing/investing-basics/investment-products/stocks"],"displayContext":"美国SEC投资者教育中的股票入门说明；两句合并。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02205a USE:U02205 -->
Stocks give their holders a share of ownership in a company and are also known as equities.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02205a -->
股票使持有人拥有公司的一部分所有权，也被称为equities。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02205a -->
<!-- USE:U02206 -->
## Equity /ˈekwəti/ n.
`英国演艺工作者工会的名称，为首字母大写的专名，不能理解为金融股票。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02206a USE:U02206 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"About us","sourceUrls":["https://www.equity.org.uk/about-us"],"displayContext":"英国Equity工会自我介绍的必要短缩编；第一人称改为机构名。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02206a USE:U02206 -->
Equity brings together performers and creative practitioners, including actors, dancers, singers and stage managers.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02206a -->
Equity工会汇集演艺工作者和创作从业者，其中包括演员、舞者、歌手和舞台监督。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02206a -->
<!-- USE:U02207 -->
## equitable /ˈekwətəbəl/ adj.
`公平、合理地对待各方的，常形容分配、安排或制度。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02207a USE:U02207 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"Languages matter: Global guidance on multilingual education","sourceUrls":["https://www.unesco.org/en/articles/languages-matter-global-guidance-multilingual-education"],"displayContext":"联合国教科文组织2025年多语教学指南的介绍；以下是目标，不是成效报告。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02207a USE:U02207 -->
UNESCO's 2025 guide Languages Matter aims to help education ministries create more inclusive, equitable and effective education systems that benefit all learners.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02207a -->
联合国教科文组织2025年的《语言至关重要》指南，旨在帮助教育部门建立更具包容性、更公平、更有效、让所有学习者受益的教育体系。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02207a -->
<!-- USE:U02208 -->
## equitable /ˈekwətəbəl/ adj.
`法律上，依据衡平法有效或获得承认的，如 equitable interest；须与普通法意义上的权利区别。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02208a USE:U02208 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"Brown II, 349 U.S. 294","sourceUrls":["https://www.law.cornell.edu/supremecourt/text/349/294"],"displayContext":"Brown II判决讨论落实废除学校种族隔离的救济命令；这里equitable是法律术语。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02208a USE:U02208 -->
In Brown II (1955), the U.S. Supreme Court said that courts implementing decrees to end racial segregation in public schools would be guided by equitable principles.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02208a -->
在1955年的布朗案第二次判决中，美国最高法院指出，法院在落实终止公立学校种族隔离的命令时，应以衡平法原则为指导。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02208a -->
<!-- USE:U02209 -->
## equitableness /ˈekwətəbəlnəs/ n.
`公平合理的性质。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02209a USE:U02209 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02209a USE:U02209 -->
The panel questioned the equitableness of a rule that gave one district twice as much funding as another.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02209a -->
审议小组质疑一项规定是否公平：它给一个地区的资金额是另一个地区的两倍。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02209a -->
<!-- USE:U02210 -->
## equitably /ˈekwətəbli/ adv.
`公平合理地，常说明资源分配等的方式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02210a USE:U02210 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02210a USE:U02210 -->
The policy tries to allocate scarce resources more equitably, so that smaller communities also receive support.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02210a -->
这项政策力图更公平地分配稀缺资源，让较小的社区也能得到支持。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02210a -->
<!-- USE:U02211 -->
## inequitable /ɪnˈekwətəbəl/ adj.
`不公平、不公正的，属于正式用语，含批评意味。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02211a USE:U02211 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02211a USE:U02211 -->
A rule that excludes equally qualified applicants solely because of where they live would be inequitable.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02211a -->
一项仅因居住地不同就排除同样合格申请人的规定，会是不公平的。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02211a -->
<!-- USE:U02212 -->
## inequitably /ɪnˈekwətəbli/ adv.
`以不公平、不公正的方式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02212a USE:U02212 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02212a USE:U02212 -->
If one team bears all the extra work while both receive the same reward, the burden is being shared inequitably.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02212a -->
如果额外工作全由一个团队承担，而两个团队得到同样的奖励，负担的分配就是不公平的。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02212a -->
<!-- USE:U02213 -->
## inequity /ɪnˈekwəti/ n.
`不公平、偏私的性质或具体表现，属于正式用语，含批评意味。可数或不可数，复数为 inequities；the inequity of something 表示某事的不公平，如 inequity of income。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02213a USE:U02213 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02213a USE:U02213 -->
The review identified an inequity in the system: people with the same needs were offered different levels of support.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02213a -->
评估指出了制度中的一项不公：需求相同的人获得的支持水平却不同。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02213a -->
<!-- USE:U02214 -->
## negative equity /ˌneɡətɪv ˈekwəti/ n.
`英式商业用语，指房产市值低于以它作担保的未清按揭债务的状态或差额。须比较现值与未清贷款；房价低于当初买价，本身不足以确定存在 negative equity。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02214a USE:U02214 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"What is negative equity and why it could be a problem","sourceUrls":["https://www.moneyhelper.org.uk/en/homes/buying-a-home/negative-equity-what-it-means-and-what-you-can-do-about-it"],"displayContext":"英国住房指南中的假设算例；不是某位房主的真实账目。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02214a USE:U02214 -->
If a property is worth £200,000 but the outstanding mortgage is £220,000, the owner has £20,000 of negative equity.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02214a -->
如果一套房屋现值为20万英镑，而未清按揭为22万英镑，房主就处于两万英镑的负净值状态。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02214a -->

<!-- WORD:W023 -->
# evolve

**词源**：来自拉丁语 evolvere“展开、卷开”，由表示向外的成分与 volvere“滚、卷”组成；evolution 早期也可指展开卷轴。现代的逐渐发展、进化与释放等用法都可借“展开、向外显露”辅助记忆，但具体技术含义仍要分别掌握。

<!-- USE:U02301 -->
## evolve /ɪˈvɑlv/ v.
`生物经过许多世代逐渐改变，形成不同特征或形态。常不及物，evolve from 表示由早先形态发展而来，evolve into 表示发展成另一形态；并不意味着每次变化都更高级。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02301a USE:U02301 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"Who was Alfred Russel Wallace?","sourceUrls":["https://www.nhm.ac.uk/discover/who-was-alfred-russel-wallace.html"],"displayContext":"伦敦自然历史博物馆对华莱士1858年认识的历史叙述；保真缩编。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02301a USE:U02301 -->
In 1858, Wallace came to understand how species evolved: individuals with advantageous characteristics survived and passed those characteristics to their offspring.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02301a -->
1858年，华莱士开始理解物种如何演化：具有有利特征的个体存活下来，并把这些特征传给后代。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02301a -->
<!-- USE:U02302 -->
## evolve /ɪˈvɑlv/ v.
`事物随时间逐渐变化、发展成不同形态，或使它如此发展。可及物或不及物，可谈思想、音乐、机构、方法等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02302a USE:U02302 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"In Their Own Words","sourceUrls":["https://www.getty.edu/news/artist-dialogues-getty-conservation-lita-albuquerque-malibu-line/"],"displayContext":"Getty的Artist Dialogues访谈系列介绍，2024年11月27日；括号理由改为从句。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02302a USE:U02302 -->
The Getty Conservation Institute's Artist Dialogues videos do not aim to preserve a fixed view, because artists' views may evolve over time.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02302a -->
盖蒂保护研究所的“艺术家对话”视频并不试图留住一种一成不变的观点，因为艺术家的看法可能随时间而变化。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02302a -->
<!-- USE:U02303 -->
## evolve /ɪˈvɑlv/ v.
`经过逐步发展、思考，想出或制定一套理论、计划或方法，直接接所形成的方案等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02303a USE:U02303 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02303a USE:U02303 -->
Through repeated trials, the team evolved a method for collecting the water without damaging the roots.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02303a -->
经过反复试验，团队逐步形成了一种既能收集水、又不损伤根系的方法。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02303a -->
<!-- USE:U02304 -->
## evolve /ɪˈvɑlv/ v.
`展开、打开或展示出来，可及物或不及物，保留卷开、显露的意义。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02304a USE:U02304 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02304a USE:U02304 -->
As the folded structure evolved, its tightly packed sections opened out into a broad surface.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02304a -->
随着折叠结构展开，原本紧密收拢的部分打开，形成了一片宽阔表面。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02304a -->
<!-- USE:U02305 -->
## evolve /ɪˈvɑlv/ v.
`产生并释放气体、热量等，直接接释放物，用于化学、物理等具体过程。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02305a USE:U02305 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02305a USE:U02305 -->
When the two substances react, they evolve a gas that must be collected in a separate container.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02305a -->
这两种物质发生反应时会放出一种气体，气体必须收集在另一个容器中。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02305a -->
<!-- USE:U02306 -->
## evolvable /ɪˈvɑlvəbəl/ adj.
`能够逐渐发展或演化的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02306a USE:U02306 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02306a USE:U02306 -->
The simulation needs an evolvable population, not a set of individuals whose inherited traits can never change.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02306a -->
这种模拟需要一个能够演化的种群，而不是一组遗传特征永远不能变化的个体。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02306a -->
<!-- USE:U02307 -->
## evolution /ˌevəˈluʃən/ n.
`事物逐渐形成、改变的过程，可数或不可数，通常较正式，不限于生物。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02307a USE:U02307 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"Languages matter: Global guidance on multilingual education","sourceUrls":["https://www.unesco.org/en/articles/languages-matter-global-guidance-multilingual-education"],"displayContext":"UNESCO2025年多语教学指南的背景说明；两句保真缩编。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02307a USE:U02307 -->
The linguistic landscape has changed considerably in recent years.
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02307a2 USE:U02307 -->
This evolution has been shaped by migration, technological developments and a growing recognition of the benefits of multilingualism.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02307a -->
近年来，语言使用的整体面貌发生了显著变化。这一演变受到人口迁移、技术发展以及人们日益认识到多语能力益处的共同影响。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02307a -->
<!-- USE:U02308 -->
## evolution /ˌevəˈluʃən/ n.
`物种从较早形态逐代发展而来的过程，以及相应的起源解释，通常不可数，强调跨世代变化。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02308a USE:U02308 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"What is natural selection?","sourceUrls":["https://www.nhm.ac.uk/discover/what-is-natural-selection.html"],"displayContext":"伦敦自然历史博物馆的自然选择入门说明；概率与“一种机制”的限定保留。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02308a USE:U02308 -->
Natural selection is one mechanism of evolution.
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02308a2 USE:U02308 -->
Organisms better adapted to their environment are more likely to survive and pass on the genes that help them do so.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02308a -->
自然选择是进化的一种机制。较适应环境的生物更可能存活下来，并把有助于存活的基因传给后代。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02308a -->
<!-- USE:U02309 -->
## evolution /ˌevəˈluʃən/ n.
`事件、情节等按先后次序呈现、发展的过程，如 evolution of the plot。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02309a USE:U02309 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02309a USE:U02309 -->
The slow evolution of the plot allows the reader to discover the family secret a little at a time.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02309a -->
情节缓慢而渐进地展开，让读者一点点发现这个家庭的秘密。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02309a -->
<!-- USE:U02310 -->
## evolution /ˌevəˈluʃən/ n.
`军事语境中，部队或舰船部署、队形的改变。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02310a USE:U02310 KIND:excerpt -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"Tidesurge North Atlantic support","sourceUrls":["https://www.royalnavy.mod.uk/news/2024/october/22/20241022-tidesurge-supports-uk-and-nato-ops-in-north-atlantic"],"displayContext":"2024年Tidesurge油船报道：两船并行靠近并匹配航向、速度进行补给。以下为三副Josh Sylvester的短引语。","speakers":{"SU02310a":"Josh Sylvester, Tidesurge's third officer"}} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02310a USE:U02310 -->
A replenishment at sea is a dangerous and challenging evolution for all involved.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02310a -->
海上补给是一项对所有参与者都充满危险和挑战的操演。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02310a -->
<!-- USE:U02311 -->
## evolution /ˌevəˈluʃən/ n.
`气体、热量等的产生与排放，用于具体科学过程，须与生物进化区分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02311a USE:U02311 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02311a USE:U02311 -->
The apparatus measures the evolution of gas during the reaction and records how the rate changes.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02311a -->
这台装置测量反应过程中气体的释放，并记录释放速率如何变化。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02311a -->
<!-- USE:U02312 -->
## evolution /ˌevəˈluʃən/ n.
`向外打开、展开的行为或过程，保留这个名词的展开意义。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02312a USE:U02312 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02312a USE:U02312 -->
The mechanism controls the evolution of the folded structure, allowing each section to open in sequence.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02312a -->
这一机构控制折叠结构的展开，使各个部分依次打开。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02312a -->
<!-- USE:U02313 -->
## evolution /ˌevəˈluʃən/ n.
`几何语境中，曲线的展开。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02313a USE:U02313 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02313a USE:U02313 -->
The geometric diagram shows the evolution of a curve as it is unfolded into a new line.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02313a -->
这幅几何图展示了一条曲线展开而形成新线的过程。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02313a -->
<!-- USE:U02314 -->
## evolution /ˌevəˈluʃən/ n.
`数学中，从某个乘方结果求出其根，即开方；与表示乘方的 involution 相对。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02314a USE:U02314 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"1911 Encyclopaedia Britannica: Arithmetic","sourceUrls":["https://en.wikisource.org/wiki/1911_Encyclop%C3%A6dia_Britannica/Arithmetic"],"displayContext":"1911年《大英百科全书》Arithmetic第43节的历史术语说明；保真缩编，不作当代用词推荐。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02314a USE:U02314 -->
In the terminology of this 1911 account, involution calculates powers, whereas evolution finds roots.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02314a -->
按这篇1911年文字所用的术语，involution指计算乘方，而evolution指求根。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02314a -->
<!-- USE:U02315 -->
## evolutionary /ˌevəˈluʃəˌneri/ adj.; evolutional /ˌevəˈluʃənəl/ adj.
`与逐渐发展或生物进化过程有关的；evolutionary 常置于名词前，evolutional 是相应的另一形容词形式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02315a USE:U02315 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02315a USE:U02315 -->
The fossils record evolutionary changes in the shape of the limb over many generations.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02315a -->
这些化石记录了肢体形态经过许多世代发生的进化变化。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02315a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02315b USE:U02315 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02315b USE:U02315 -->
The redesign was an evolutional process of small adjustments rather than one sudden change.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02315b -->
这次重新设计是一个由细小调整逐步展开的演变过程，而非一次突然变化。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02315b -->
<!-- USE:U02316 -->
## evolutionarily /ˌevəˈluʃəˌnerəli/ adv.; evolutionally /ˌevəˈluʃənəli/ adv.
`从进化或逐步发展的角度、以相应方式；须由上下文判断是生物进化还是其他事物的发展。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02316a USE:U02316 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02316a USE:U02316 -->
The two species are evolutionarily related, even though they now live in very different environments.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02316a -->
这两个物种在进化上有亲缘关系，尽管它们如今生活的环境非常不同。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02316a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02316b USE:U02316 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02316b USE:U02316 -->
Viewed evolutionally, the design is a stage in a continuing process rather than a finished solution.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02316b -->
从逐步演变的角度看，这种设计是一个持续过程中的阶段，而不是最终完成的解决方案。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02316b -->
<!-- USE:U02317 -->
## evolutionism /ˌevəˈluʃənɪzəm/ n.
`以物种进化解释生物起源的观点。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02317a USE:U02317 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02317a USE:U02317 -->
Evolutionism explains the diversity of living things through descent from earlier forms of life.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02317a -->
进化论用生物由较早生命形式传承演变的过程来解释生物的多样性。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02317a -->
<!-- USE:U02318 -->
## evolutionist /ˌevəˈluʃənɪst/ n.
`接受生物从较早生命形式进化而来的科学解释的人，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02318a USE:U02318 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02318a USE:U02318 -->
An evolutionist accepts that living species developed from earlier forms; this does not mean that every change is an improvement.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02318a -->
进化论支持者接受现存物种由较早形态发展而来；这并不表示每一次变化都是进步。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02318a -->
<!-- USE:U02319 -->
## evolutionistic /ˌevəluʃəˈnɪstɪk/ adj.
`与进化论观点或其支持者有关的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02319a USE:U02319 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02319a USE:U02319 -->
The argument is evolutionistic: it explains the origin of species in terms of descent and gradual change.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02319a -->
这一论证采用进化论的解释框架：它用传承与渐变来解释物种起源。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02319a -->
<!-- USE:U02320 -->
## evolvement /ɪˈvɑlvmənt/ n.
`逐渐发展、演变或形成的过程。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02320a USE:U02320 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02320a USE:U02320 -->
The notes show the gradual evolvement of the theory, with each version answering a problem left by the previous one.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02320a -->
这些笔记显示了理论逐渐形成的过程：每一版都回应前一版留下的一个问题。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02320a -->

<!-- WORD:W024 -->
# exploit

**词源**：经法语进入英语，历史上联系到“展开、做成的事情”及行动成果；名词发展为功绩、壮举，动词则可指取得用处、开发资源，也可指不公平利用人或局势。注意名词和动词的重音不同。

<!-- USE:U02401 -->
## exploit /ɪkˈsplɔɪt/ v.
`利用别人的劳动或想法为自己牟利，却给予很少回报，直接接被利用的人，带负面评价，如 exploited workers。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02401a USE:U02401 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.ilo.org/resource/article/questions-and-answers-forced-labour"],"displayContext":"国际劳工组织2012年问答中的受害者类别说明；不是2026年新的规模估计。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02401a USE:U02401 -->
Victims of state-imposed forced labour may be exploited by military or rebel groups.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02401a -->
国家强迫劳动的受害者可能受到军方或叛乱团体的剥削。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02401a -->
<!-- USE:U02402 -->
## exploit /ɪkˈsplɔɪt/ v.
`利用某种局势谋取自身优势，而不顾他人或应有做法，含批评意味，可用 exploit something to one's advantage。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02402a USE:U02402 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02402a USE:U02402 -->
Do not exploit a friend’s financial difficulties to pressure them into an unfair deal.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02402a -->
不要利用朋友的经济困难，逼对方接受不公平的交易。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02402a -->
<!-- USE:U02403 -->
## exploit /ɪkˈsplɔɪt/ v.
`有效利用某事物，以取得成果或优势，直接接利用对象。此处可为中性或积极含义，如 exploit new opportunities，须与 exploit workers 的剥削义区分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02403a USE:U02403 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"Introducing Mary Quant","sourceUrls":["https://www.vam.ac.uk/articles/introducing-mary-quant"],"displayContext":"V&A对玛丽·奎恩特店铺早期经营的历史介绍；保真缩编。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02403a USE:U02403 -->
At first, Mary Quant stocked her shop with wholesale clothes, exploiting an opportunity to offer women a new style.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02403a -->
起初，玛丽·奎恩特在店里销售从批发市场采购的服装，借此抓住为女性提供新风格的机会。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02403a -->
<!-- USE:U02404 -->
## exploit /ɪkˈsplɔɪt/ v.
`开发并利用自然资源或原料，供工业、商业使用，直接接资源等。是否具有破坏性须看实际做法，这个词本身不一律作负面判断。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02404a USE:U02404 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02404a USE:U02404 -->
The licence allows the company to exploit the coal deposit, but not the nearby forest.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02404a -->
这份许可证允许公司开发该煤矿，但不允许开发附近的森林。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02404a -->
<!-- USE:U02405 -->
## exploit /ˈeksplɔɪt/ n.
`勇敢、引人注意、有趣或令人发笑的所作所为，可数，通常用复数并带所有格；名词重音在第一音节，与动词不同。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02405a USE:U02405 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02405a USE:U02405 -->
Her daring exploits at sea became the stories her family told for years.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02405a -->
她在海上的那些大胆经历，成了家人多年讲述的故事。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02405a -->
<!-- USE:U02406 -->
## exploitable /ɪkˈsplɔɪtəbəl/ adj.
`能够利用、开发以取得收益的，可形容原料、矿藏、油井等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02406a USE:U02406 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02406a USE:U02406 -->
The survey found coal, but not enough to make the deposit commercially exploitable.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02406a -->
勘查发现了煤，但数量不足以使该矿藏具备商业开采价值。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02406a -->
<!-- USE:U02407 -->
## exploitable /ɪkˈsplɔɪtəbəl/ adj.
`能够被人利用来谋取自身优势的，可形容局势、错误等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02407a USE:U02407 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02407a USE:U02407 -->
Leaving the password on a public page would create an easily exploitable weakness in the system.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02407a -->
把密码留在公开网页上，会在系统中造成一个很容易被利用的弱点。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02407a -->
<!-- USE:U02408 -->
## exploitation /ˌeksplɔɪˈteɪʃən/ n.
`不公平地利用他人劳动或想法却给予很少回报的行为，通常不可数，带负面评价。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02408a USE:U02408 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.ilo.org/resource/article/exploitation-vs-forced-labour-what-can-we-learn-international-labour"],"displayContext":"国际劳工组织Tim De Meyer2015年博客的概念辨析；两处相关论述的非连续缩编，不作个案法律裁定。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02408a USE:U02408 -->
Not all labour exploitation is forced labour: the latter removes a worker’s freedom to accept or leave the work.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02408a -->
并非所有劳动剥削都是强迫劳动；后者剥夺了劳动者自由接受或离开这份工作的选择。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02408a -->
<!-- USE:U02409 -->
## exploitation /ˌeksplɔɪˈteɪʃən/ n.
`为自己谋利而利用某种困境或事件的行为，带批评意味；特指一次此类利用时用单数，常用 the exploitation of something。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02409a USE:U02409 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02409a USE:U02409 -->
An emergency is no excuse for the exploitation of tenants who have nowhere else to live.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02409a -->
紧急情况不能成为利用那些无处可去的租户谋利的借口。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02409a -->
<!-- USE:U02410 -->
## exploitation /ˌeksplɔɪˈteɪʃən/ n.
`把资源或机会实际利用起来以获取成果、收益的过程，通常不可数；资源开发语境不自动具有剥削人的含义。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02410a USE:U02410 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02410a USE:U02410 -->
Commercial exploitation of the oil field would require investment in equipment and transport.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02410a -->
对这片油田进行商业开发，需要投入设备和运输方面的资金。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02410a -->
<!-- USE:U02411 -->
## exploitative /ɪkˈsplɔɪtətɪv/ adj.; exploitive /ɪkˈsplɔɪtɪv/ adj.
`不公平地利用他人的劳动或想法供自己获利的，属于正式用语，含批评意味；exploitative 和 exploitive 是相应的两种形式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02411a USE:U02411 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.ilo.org/resource/article/questions-and-answers-forced-labour"],"displayContext":"国际劳工组织2012年问答中的概念区别；仅缩编相关两句，不搬入当年全球人数。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02411a USE:U02411 -->
Poor or exploitative working conditions are not, by themselves, the same as forced labour; restrictions and threats are among the indicators used to identify coercion.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02411a -->
恶劣或具有剥削性的劳动条件，本身并不等于强迫劳动；行动限制和威胁等，是识别胁迫时使用的指标。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02411a -->
<!-- USE:U02412 -->
## exploiter /ɪkˈsplɔɪtər/ n.
`不公平、残酷地利用他人的人，可数，属于正式用语，含批评意味。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02412a USE:U02412 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.ilo.org/resource/article/questions-and-answers-forced-labour"],"displayContext":"国际劳工组织2012年估计的历史表述；十八个月不是本项目核实的2026年现值。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02412a USE:U02412 -->
In its 2012 estimate, the ILO put the average time victims spent in forced labour at nearly eighteen months before rescue or escape from their exploiters.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02412a -->
在2012年的估计中，国际劳工组织认为，受害者在获救或逃离剥削者之前，平均遭受强迫劳动近十八个月。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02412a -->

<!-- WORD:W025 -->
# friction

**词源**：来自拉丁语表示“摩擦、擦拭”的词。实际接触摩擦发展出阻碍运动的物理意义，再比喻为人与人之间相互抵触。

<!-- USE:U02501 -->
## friction /ˈfrɪkʃən/ n.
`因意愿、性格或观点冲突产生的争执、抵触，可数或不可数，常接 between。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02501a USE:U02501 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02501a USE:U02501 -->
Different priorities can create friction between the two teams, even when everyone wants the project to succeed.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02501a -->
即使大家都希望项目成功，优先事项不同也可能造成两个团队之间的不和。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02501a -->
<!-- USE:U02502 -->
## friction /ˈfrɪkʃən/ n.
`一个物体在另一个物体表面擦动的动作，通常不可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02502a USE:U02502 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02502a USE:U02502 -->
Repeated friction polished the edge of the stone.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02502a -->
反复摩擦把石头边缘磨得光滑了。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02502a -->
<!-- USE:U02503 -->
## friction /ˈfrɪkʃən/ n.
`物体接触时阻碍相对滑动或滑动趋势的力，通常不可数；reduce friction 表示减小摩擦阻力。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02503a USE:U02503 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.jpl.nasa.gov/edu/resources/lesson-plan/hovering-on-a-cushion-of-air/"],"displayContext":"NASA/JPL教学页面的汽车对比说明；未进行器材实验。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02503a USE:U02503 -->
Friction can damage an engine without proper lubrication, yet a car also needs friction between its tyres and the road in order to move.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02503a -->
润滑不当时，摩擦可能损坏发动机；但汽车要行驶，也需要轮胎与路面之间的摩擦。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02503a -->
<!-- USE:U02504 -->
## friction clutch /ˈfrɪkʃən klʌtʃ/ n.
`机械中，通过接触面的摩擦传递动力的离合器；名称中的 friction 说明传动原理。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02504a USE:U02504,U02505,U02506 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.mathworks.com/help/sdl/ref/discfrictionclutch.html"],"displayContext":"MathWorks Simscape Driveline R2026b文档的非连续缩编：模块添加、接合原理与几何参数。未运行MATLAB。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02504a USE:U02504 -->
Add a Disc Friction Clutch block to the model.
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02504a2 USE:U02506 -->
When plate pressure exceeds the engagement threshold, frictional torques allow power to pass between the shafts.
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02504a3 USE:U02505 -->
To enter the friction disc’s outer and inner diameters, set Geometry model to Define annular region.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02504a -->
在模型中加入Disc Friction Clutch模块。当摩擦片间的压力超过接合阈值时，摩擦转矩使动力能够在两根轴之间传递。要输入摩擦盘的外径和内径，请把Geometry model设置为Define annular region。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02504a -->
<!-- USE:U02505 -->
## friction disc /ˈfrɪkʃən dɪsk/ n.
`机械中，通过摩擦接触参与动力传递的圆盘、摩擦片。`

<!-- CONTEXT_REF:CU02504a USE:U02505 -->
<!-- USE:U02506 -->
## frictional /ˈfrɪkʃənəl/ adj.
`与摩擦动作或摩擦力有关的。`

<!-- CONTEXT_REF:CU02504a USE:U02506 -->
<!-- USE:U02507 -->
## frictionless /ˈfrɪkʃənləs/ adj.
`没有摩擦或摩擦阻力的，物理模型常把这种状态作为理想条件。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02507a USE:U02507 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.jpl.nasa.gov/edu/resources/lesson-plan/hovering-on-a-cushion-of-air/"],"displayContext":"NASA/JPL所述休斯敦约翰逊航天中心的地面训练平台；virtually表示近乎，非绝对零摩擦。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02507a USE:U02507 -->
On an astronaut-training platform at NASA's Johnson Space Center, air bearings lift the platform clear of the floor, making it virtually frictionless.
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02507a2 -->
The astronaut uses hand-controlled air jets to move across the room.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02507a -->
在NASA约翰逊航天中心的一种宇航员训练平台上，气浮支承把平台抬离地面，使它几乎没有摩擦阻力。宇航员用手控气流喷口推动自己在室内移动。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02507a -->

<!-- WORD:W026 -->
# habitat

**词源**：本来是拉丁语动词的一种形式，意思是“它居住于……”，后来用作生物栖息环境的名称。借“它住在哪里”记住生态意义；这个词也有较泛的住所义。

<!-- USE:U02601 -->
## habitat /ˈhæbəˌtæt/ n.
`动物或植物自然生活、生长的环境，包括栖息的地点和维持其生活的环境条件。可数或不可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02601a USE:U02601 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"Dam Removals on the Elwha River","sourceUrls":["https://www.fisheries.noaa.gov/west-coast/science-data/dam-removals-elwha-river"],"displayContext":"NOAA Fisheries关于埃尔瓦河2011—2014年拆坝历史的说明；保真缩编。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02601a USE:U02601 -->
Removing the two Elwha River dams between 2011 and 2014 reopened more than seventy miles of salmon habitat.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02601a -->
2011至2014年间拆除埃尔瓦河上的两座水坝，使鲑鱼重新能够进入超过七十英里长的栖息地。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02601a -->
<!-- USE:U02602 -->
## habitat /ˈhæbəˌtæt/ n.
`供人或其他居住者生活的住所、居住空间；这里不专指生物的自然栖息环境。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02602a USE:U02602 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.nasa.gov/humans-in-space/chapea/"],"displayContext":"NASA CHAPEA地面火星生活模拟；这里habitat是供乘员居住的人工舱体。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02602a USE:U02602 -->
In CHAPEA, four crew members live in an isolated 1,700-square-foot habitat to simulate a year-long stay on the surface of Mars.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02602a -->
在CHAPEA地面模拟任务中，四名乘员住在一个与外界隔离、面积为1700平方英尺的居住舱内，模拟在火星表面生活一年。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02602a -->

<!-- WORD:W027 -->
# incentive

**词源**：来自拉丁语相关词，早期含“定下曲调、吹奏引领”的意象，后来表示激起行动的因素。可以用“带动别人行动”辅助记忆，不把它与外形相近的词机械合并。

<!-- USE:U02701 -->
## incentive /ɪnˈsentɪv/ n.
`促使人采取行动的理由、刺激或鼓励因素。可数或不可数；an incentive to do something 表示做某事的动力，an incentive to action 表示行动的诱因。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02701a USE:U02701 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02701a USE:U02701 -->
A clear chance to use the skill gives learners an incentive to keep practising, even when progress is slow.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02701a -->
明确知道有机会运用这项技能，会使学习者有动力继续练习，即使进步缓慢。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02701a -->
<!-- USE:U02702 -->
## incentive /ɪnˈsentɪv/ n.
`为鼓励员工提高产出而给予的具体付款、奖励或优惠，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02702a USE:U02702 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.irs.gov/publications/p525"],"displayContext":"IRS2025税年Publication525的假设汽车销售人员示例；将incentive payments改为名词an incentive，保原条件。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02702a USE:U02702 -->
In a car-salesperson example in IRS Publication 525 for 2025, you receive an incentive from the manufacturer each time you sell a particular model of car.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02702a -->
在美国国税局2025税年《第525号刊物》的一个汽车销售人员示例中，每卖出一辆特定型号的汽车，你就会收到制造商提供的一笔奖励。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02702a -->
<!-- USE:U02703 -->
## incentive /ɪnˈsentɪv/ adj.
`用来激励、推动行动的，放在名词前，如 incentive payment 指激励性报酬。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02703a USE:U02703 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":["https://www.irs.gov/publications/p525"],"displayContext":"沿用上一份IRS2025税年汽车销售示例；以下只保留该例的填报说明，不代表每位读者适用的现行报税路径。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02703a USE:U02703 -->
In the car-salesperson example in IRS Publication 525 for 2025, you report the manufacturer's incentive payments on Schedule 1, Form 1040, line 8z.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02703a -->
在美国国税局2025税年《第525号刊物》的汽车销售人员示例中，这些制造商激励报酬填报在1040表的附表1第8z行。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02703a -->

<!-- WORD:W028 -->
# inherit

**词源**：经法语来自表示“成为继承人、作为继承人接受”的拉丁语相关形式。inheritance 是所继承的东西或继承行为，inheritor 是继承者；现代意义既可涉及财产，也可涉及遗传和前人留下的处境。

<!-- USE:U02801 -->
## inherit /ɪnˈherɪt/ v.
`依法从前人承接财产、地位或称号，通常在前人去世后发生。可说 inherit something from somebody；继承对象已明确时也可省略宾语。具体继承资格由相应法律和制度决定。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02801a USE:U02801 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"《求婚》：洛莫夫谈继承土地","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/7986/7986-h/7986-h.htm"],"displayContext":"契诃夫《求婚》早期英译本；洛莫夫对娜塔莉娅的台词缩编，保角色陈述身份。","speakers":{"SU02801a":"Lomov (Chekhov, The Proposal)"}} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02801a USE:U02801 -->
I inherited my land from relatives who are now dead; they always held your parents in the greatest respect.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02801a -->
我的土地继承自已故的亲属；他们一直非常尊敬您的父母。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02801a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02801b USE:U02801 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02801b USE:U02801 -->
She is the only surviving relative who can inherit.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02801b -->
她是唯一在世且能继承遗产的亲属。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02801b -->
<!-- USE:U02802 -->
## inherit /ɪnˈherɪt/ v.
`因接任职位或受到前人影响而接过其留下的任务、问题、态度或处境，不要求前人去世。常说 inherit something from one's predecessors。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02802a USE:U02802 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02802a USE:U02802 -->
The government inherited an impossible situation from its predecessors.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02802a -->
政府从前任那里接手了一个极其棘手的局面。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02802a -->
<!-- USE:U02803 -->
## inherit /ɪnˈherɪt/ v.
`通过遗传而生来具有父母或祖先的某种特征，常说 inherit something from one's parents。称某种状况为 inherited 只说明遗传关系，不等于遗传是唯一或必然的原因；defect 带“缺陷”色彩，描述言语状况可用较中性的 disorder。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02803a USE:U02803 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"后天拉伸的改变能遗传吗？","sourceUrls":["https://www.nhm.ac.uk/discover/what-is-natural-selection.html"],"displayContext":"自然历史博物馆对拉马克式后天遗传解释的反驳；局部缩编。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02803a USE:U02803 -->
Even if stretching made your neck a little longer, your children would not inherit that change.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02803a -->
即使拉伸让你的脖子稍微变长，你的孩子也不会遗传到这种后天变化。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02803a -->
<!-- USE:U02804 -->
## inheritance /ɪnˈherɪtəns/ n.
`从去世者那里继承的金钱、财产等遗产，可数或不可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02804a USE:U02804 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02804a USE:U02804 -->
She put part of her inheritance aside and used the rest to pay off the house.
