<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02804a -->
她把继承来的财产留出一部分，其余用来还清房贷。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02804a -->
<!-- USE:U02805 -->
## inheritance /ɪnˈherɪtəns/ n.
`从前任或前人那里接过的职位相关处境、问题或态度，通常用单数并带所有格，强调留给继任者的局面。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02805a USE:U02805 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02805a USE:U02805 -->
The new director faced a difficult inheritance: unfinished projects and a department short of staff.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02805a -->
新主管接手了一个棘手的局面：项目尚未完成，部门又缺人。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02805a -->
<!-- USE:U02806 -->
## inheritance /ɪnˈherɪtəns/ n.
`从家族或祖先传下、生来具有的特征或品质，通常用单数；genetic inheritance 指遗传特征。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02806a USE:U02806 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02806a USE:U02806 -->
Eye colour shows your genetic inheritance.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02806a -->
眼睛的颜色体现了你的遗传特征。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02806a -->
<!-- USE:U02807 -->
## inheritance /ɪnˈherɪtəns/ n.
`继承的行为、过程或一次继承事件，指承接这件事，而非所继承的财物本身。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02807a USE:U02807 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02807a USE:U02807 -->
The document records when the inheritance took place, rather than how much property changed hands.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02807a -->
文件记录的是继承在何时发生，而不是转移了多少财产。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02807a -->
<!-- USE:U02808 -->
## inheritor /ɪnˈherɪtər/ n.
`从去世者那里继承金钱或财产的人，可数，属于法律用语。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02808a USE:U02808 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02808a USE:U02808 -->
An inheritor may choose to keep the house rather than sell it.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02808a -->
继承房屋的人可以选择保留房屋，而不是将其出售。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02808a -->
<!-- USE:U02809 -->
## inheritor /ɪnˈherɪtər/ n.
`在某种传统建立以后到来、能够承接它并从中受益的人。常用复数 inheritors，后接 of 表明所承接的传统等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02809a USE:U02809 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02809a USE:U02809 -->
They called themselves the proud inheritors of the Prussian military tradition.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02809a -->
他们自称是普鲁士军事传统的自豪承接者。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02809a -->
<!-- USE:U02810 -->
## inheritress /ɪnˈherɪtrɪs/ n.; inheritrix /ɪnˈherɪtrɪks/ n.
`女性继承人，inheritor 的女性形式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02810a USE:U02810 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02810a USE:U02810 -->
The old wording calls the woman who succeeds to the estate an inheritrix, also written inheritress.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02810a -->
旧式措辞把继承这份地产的女性称为inheritrix，也写作inheritress。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02810a -->
<!-- USE:U02811 -->
## inheritable /ɪnˈherɪtəbəl/ adj.
`能够被继承或遗传的，可描述财产、权利、特征等被传承的对象。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02811a USE:U02811 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02811a USE:U02811 -->
The agreement concerns inheritable rights.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02811a -->
这份协议涉及可继承的权利。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02811a -->
<!-- USE:U02812 -->
## inheritable /ɪnˈherɪtəbəl/ adj.
`具备继承资格或能力的，描述能够继承的人，而非可被继承的财物。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02812a USE:U02812 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02812a USE:U02812 -->
In this older legal use, an inheritable person is capable of inheriting; the adjective describes the heir, not the property.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02812a -->
在这一较旧的法律用法中，inheritable person指具备继承能力的人；这个形容词描述继承人，而不是财产。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02812a -->
<!-- USE:U02813 -->
## inheritability /ɪnˌherɪtəˈbɪləti/ n.
`可被继承、遗传，或具有继承资格的性质；具体指继承对象还是继承主体，要由所修饰、谈论的事物确定。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02813a USE:U02813 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02813a USE:U02813 -->
The discussion concerns the inheritability of the title, not the personal merits of the claimant.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02813a -->
讨论的是该称号能否继承，而不是请求继承者个人是否优秀。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02813a -->
<!-- USE:U02814 -->
## inherit /ɪnˈherɪt/ v.
`从家人或前辈那里承袭某种品质、爱好或倾向。这种引申说法可描述家庭影响，不据此断定爱好通过基因传递。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02814a USE:U02814 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02814a USE:U02814 -->
Her children have inherited her love of sport.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02814a -->
她的孩子承袭了她对运动的热爱。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02814a -->

<!-- WORD:W029 -->
# integrate

**词源**：来自拉丁语 integrare“使完整”，联系到 integer“完整的”。由把部分补成整体，理解系统整合；社会用法强调平等参与，数学用法则要按积分的技术意义掌握。

<!-- USE:U02901 -->
## integrate /ˈɪntəɡreɪt/ v.
`使人获得群体的接纳和平等参与，或自己融入群体。可及物或不及物：integrate somebody into/with a community 表示使某人融入社区，integrate into a way of life 表示融入一种生活方式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02901a USE:U02901 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02901a USE:U02901 -->
Integrating the kids with the community, finding them a role, is essential.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02901a -->
让孩子们融入社区、为他们找到参与的角色，这很重要。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02901a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02901b USE:U02901 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02901b USE:U02901 -->
He didn't integrate successfully into the Italian way of life.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02901b -->
他没有成功融入意大利的生活方式。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02901b -->
<!-- USE:U02902 -->
## integrate /ˈɪntəɡreɪt/ v.
`取消学校、组织等原有的种族隔离，使不同群体能够共同参与。这个具体用法尤见于美式英语；既可接机构作宾语，也可由机构作主语而不接宾语，说明它走向融合。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02902a USE:U02902 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02902a USE:U02902 -->
Encouraging teacher transfer would not, by itself, integrate the teaching corps.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02902a -->
仅仅鼓励教师调动，并不能使教师队伍实现种族融合。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02902a -->
<!-- USE:U02903 -->
## integrate /ˈɪntəɡreɪt/ v.
`把各部分密切联结、衔接成整体，或使事物成为整体的一部分。可及物或不及物；integrate A with B 表示使两者相互衔接，integrate A into a whole 表示把部分纳入整体。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02903a USE:U02903 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"把发动机集成到火箭芯级","sourceUrls":["https://www.nasa.gov/centers-and-facilities/marshall/the-marshall-star-408/"],"displayContext":"NASA当时的Artemis II装配报道；不代表当前装配进度。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02903a USE:U02903 -->
In September 2023, NASA said that it and its contractors would continue integrating the remaining engines into the Artemis II SLS core stage and installing its propulsion and electrical systems.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02903a -->
2023年9月，NASA表示，它及其承包商将继续把其余发动机集成到阿尔忒弥斯二号任务的SLS芯级中，并安装推进与电气系统。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02903a -->
<!-- USE:U02904 -->
## integrate /ˈɪntəɡreɪt/ v.
`通过补入缺少的部分使不完善的事物完整，接所补全的事物作宾语，强调补足而非简单并列已有部分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02904a USE:U02904 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02904a USE:U02904 -->
The missing sections were added to integrate the incomplete account.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02904a -->
缺失的部分被补入，使这份不完整的叙述得到补全。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02904a -->
<!-- USE:U02905 -->
## integrate /ˈɪntəɡreɪt/ v.
`数学上求函数或某个量的积分，可接所求积分的函数等作宾语；已知运算对象时也可说 integrate along a path，表示沿路径积分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02905a USE:U02905 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"沿径向路径积分","sourceUrls":["https://openstax.org/books/university-physics-volume-2/pages/8-1-capacitors-and-capacitance"],"displayContext":"OpenStax University Physics Vol2 §8.1（CC BY-NC-SA4.0）；推导局部缩编。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02905a USE:U02905 -->
To find the potential difference between the spherical shells, substitute the electric field into the equation and integrate along a radial path between them.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02905a -->
要求两球壳之间的电势差，先将电场代入方程，再沿两者之间的径向路径积分。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02905a -->
<!-- USE:U02906 -->
## integrated /ˈɪntəɡreɪtɪd/ adj.
`表示某个物理量经累积得到的总量或平均值的，用于数学、测量等语境。具体代表总量还是平均值，要依相应定义和计算方式判断。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02906a USE:U02906 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02906a USE:U02906 -->
The instrument records the integrated value of the signal over one second.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02906a -->
仪器记录该信号在一秒内的累计积分值。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02906a -->
<!-- USE:U02907 -->
## integrate /ˈɪntəɡrət/ adj.
`由各部分合成的、构成完整整体的。末尾读 /ɡrət/，与动词 integrate 的 /ɡreɪt/ 区分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02907a USE:U02907 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02907a USE:U02907 -->
The older adjective appears in “an integrate whole”: a whole made up of connected parts.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02907a -->
较旧的形容词见于“an integrate whole”，指各部分相互连接而成的完整整体。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02907a -->
<!-- USE:U02908 -->
## integrated /ˈɪntəɡreɪtɪd/ adj.
`面向不同种族或宗教群体、使其共同参与的，常放在 school、institution 等名词前。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02908a USE:U02908 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"判决中的融合学校体系","sourceUrls":["https://www.archives.gov/milestone-documents/brown-v-board-of-education"],"displayContext":"1954年美国最高法院判决；局部缩编，保历史语境。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02908a USE:U02908 -->
In Brown v. Board of Education (1954), the U.S. Supreme Court cited a finding that segregation could deprive children of benefits they would receive in a racially integrated school system.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02908a -->
在1954年的布朗诉教育委员会案中，美国最高法院援引一项认定：种族隔离可能使儿童失去他们在种族融合的学校体系中本可获得的益处。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02908a -->
<!-- USE:U02909 -->
## integrated /ˈɪntəɡreɪtɪd/ adj.
`各部分相互衔接、构成统一体系的，可描述政策、系统或社会等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02909a USE:U02909 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02909a USE:U02909 -->
There is, he said, a lack of an integrated national transport policy.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02909a -->
他说，目前缺少一套统一衔接的全国交通政策。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02909a -->
<!-- USE:U02910 -->
## integration /ˌɪntəˈɡreɪʃən/ n.
`各部分结合成整体的行为或实例，通常不可数，可谈市场、系统或政策等的整合。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02910a USE:U02910 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02910a USE:U02910 -->
The article discusses the closer integration of global capital markets.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02910a -->
这篇文章讨论全球资本市场更紧密的整合。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02910a -->
<!-- USE:U02911 -->
## integration /ˌɪntəˈɡreɪʃən/ n.
`原来分隔的人群获得共同、平等参与的过程，包括取消种族隔离后的融合，也可指其他群体融入社会生活。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02911a USE:U02911 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02911a USE:U02911 -->
The proposal supports the integration of people with disabilities into mainstream society.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02911a -->
这项提案支持残障人士融入主流社会。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02911a -->
<!-- USE:U02912 -->
## integration /ˌɪntəˈɡreɪʃən/ n.
`心理学上知觉等不同要素在一个人格中结合起来的整合作用。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02912a USE:U02912 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02912a USE:U02912 -->
In this psychological use, integration means combining elements of perception within a personality; it is not a mathematical operation.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02912a -->
在这一心理学用法中，integration指一个人格中知觉要素的结合，并不是数学运算。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02912a -->
<!-- USE:U02913 -->
## integrationist /ˌɪntəˈɡreɪʃənɪst/ n.
`主张不同群体融合、取消隔离的人，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02913a USE:U02913 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02913a USE:U02913 -->
The integrationist opposed separate schools for different racial groups.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02913a -->
这位主张融合的人反对不同种族分校就读。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02913a -->
<!-- USE:U02914 -->
## integrable /ˈɪntəɡrəbəl/ adj.
`能够被结合、纳入一个整体的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02914a USE:U02914 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02914a USE:U02914 -->
The module is integrable into the existing system.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02914a -->
这个模块可以整合进现有系统。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02914a -->
<!-- USE:U02915 -->
## integrable /ˈɪntəɡrəbəl/ adj.
`数学上能够按所采用的积分定义求积分的，需满足该定义相应的条件。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02915a USE:U02915 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02915a USE:U02915 -->
This continuous function is integrable on the closed interval.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02915a -->
这个连续函数在该闭区间上可积。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02915a -->
<!-- USE:U02916 -->
## integrability /ˌɪntəɡrəˈbɪləti/ n.
`能够被整合或求积分的性质。用于数学时，具体条件取决于采用的积分定义。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02916a USE:U02916 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02916a USE:U02916 -->
The proof establishes the function's integrability.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02916a -->
这个证明确定了该函数的可积性。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02916a -->
<!-- USE:U02917 -->
## integrative /ˈɪntəɡrətɪv/ adj.
`起结合、整合作用的，使原来不同的要素相互联系。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02917a USE:U02917 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02917a USE:U02917 -->
An integrative course brings historical evidence and artistic analysis into the same discussion.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02917a -->
一门采用整合方法的课程，会把历史证据和艺术分析放进同一次讨论中。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02917a -->
<!-- USE:U02918 -->
## integrator /ˈɪntəɡreɪtər/ n.
`用于显示或记录面积、温度等物理量总量或平均值的仪器，即积分仪或求积器，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02918a USE:U02918 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02918a USE:U02918 -->
The integrator registers the accumulated signal.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02918a -->
这台积分仪记录累计信号。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02918a -->
<!-- USE:U02919 -->
## integrator /ˈɪntəɡreɪtər/ n.
`把部分结合起来的人或事物，也可指结合形成的成品，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU02919a USE:U02919 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU02919a USE:U02919 -->
The project needs an integrator who can connect the separate components.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU02919a -->
这个项目需要一个能把独立部件连接起来的整合者。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU02919a -->

<!-- WORD:W030 -->
# intervene

**词源**：联系到拉丁语 intervenire“走到中间”，inter- 表示在中间，venire 表示来到。由介于中间，理解时间、空间上的间隔，也理解介入事态、插话与第三方参加诉讼。

<!-- USE:U03001 -->
## intervene /ˌɪntərˈvin/ v.
`介入某种情势，以制止、调整、帮助等方式改变其结果或进程。不及物，常接 in；词本身不判断介入是否正当。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03001a USE:U03001 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03001a USE:U03001 -->
The situation calmed down when police intervened.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03001a -->
警方介入后，局势平静了下来。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03001a -->
<!-- USE:U03002 -->
## intervene /ˌɪntərˈvin/ v.
`打断交谈以加入自己的话或意见，不及物，也可与直接引语连用。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03002a USE:U03002 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03002a USE:U03002 -->
'I've told you he's not here,' Irena intervened.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03002a -->
“我已经告诉你，他不在这里。”伊雷娜插话说。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03002a -->
<!-- USE:U03003 -->
## intervene /ˌɪntərˈvin/ v.
`某事突然发生，打断、推迟或阻止原定进程，不及物，天气或其他事件可作主语。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03003a USE:U03003 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03003a USE:U03003 -->
The South African mailboat arrived on Friday mornings unless bad weather intervened.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03003a -->
只要没有恶劣天气打断航程，南非邮船就会在星期五早晨抵达。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03003a -->
<!-- USE:U03004 -->
## intervene /ˌɪntərˈvin/ v.
`发生在两个事件之间，或指一段时间在其间经过；不及物，常接 between。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03004a USE:U03004 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03004a USE:U03004 -->
Several years intervened between the discovery and publication.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03004a -->
从发现到发表之间隔了好几年。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03004a -->
<!-- USE:U03005 -->
## intervene /ˌɪntərˈvin/ v.
`位于两个事物之间，不及物，用于描述空间位置。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03005a USE:U03005 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03005a USE:U03005 -->
A narrow valley intervenes between the two ridges.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03005a -->
两道山脊之间隔着一条狭窄山谷。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03005a -->
<!-- USE:U03006 -->
## intervene /ˌɪntərˈvin/ v.
`作为原情势以外的因素介入并产生影响，不及物，不必然阻止原计划。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03006a USE:U03006 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03006a USE:U03006 -->
Chance may intervene even in a carefully planned experiment.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03006a -->
即使在精心规划的实验中，偶然因素也可能介入。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03006a -->
<!-- USE:U03007 -->
## intervene /ˌɪntərˈvin/ v.
`法律上作为第三方加入已有诉讼，不及物。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03007a USE:U03007 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"第三方获准参加诉讼的条件","sourceUrls":["https://www.law.cornell.edu/rules/frcp/rule_24"],"displayContext":"既有读件所展示的美国联邦民诉规则24(b)(1)(B)分支；仅语言例，不替代完整程序。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03007a USE:U03007 -->
Under Rule 24(b)(1)(B) of the U.S. Federal Rules of Civil Procedure, a court may permit someone to intervene on a timely motion if their claim or defense shares a common question of law or fact with the main action.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03007a -->
按美国《联邦民事诉讼规则》第24条(b)(1)(B)这一分支，在及时提出动议的情况下，如果申请人的请求或抗辩与主诉讼有共同的法律或事实问题，法院可以允许其参加诉讼。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03007a -->
<!-- USE:U03008 -->
## intervener /ˌɪntərˈvinər/ n.; intervenor /ˌɪntərˈvinər/ n.
`介入者，也可指加入已有诉讼的第三方，可数；法律语境也常用 intervenor。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03008a USE:U03008 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03008a USE:U03008 -->
The intervenor joins an existing lawsuit; the original parties remain parties to the same case.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03008a -->
参加诉讼的第三方加入的是已有诉讼；原有当事人仍是同一案件的当事人。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03008a -->
<!-- USE:U03009 -->
## intervenient /ˌɪntərˈviniənt/ adj.
`介于中间、在其间发生或介入的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03009a USE:U03009 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03009a USE:U03009 -->
An intervenient strip of land lies between the two estates.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03009a -->
两处地产之间隔着一条狭长土地。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03009a -->
<!-- USE:U03010 -->
## intervention /ˌɪntərˈvenʃən/ n.
`介入事态的行为或一次介入，尤其可指国家干涉他国事务。可数或不可数，常接 in；military intervention 指军事干预。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03010a USE:U03010 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03010a USE:U03010 -->
The debate concerned military interventions.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03010a -->
辩论涉及军事干预。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03010a -->
<!-- USE:U03011 -->
## intervention /ˌɪntərˈvenʃən/ n.
`亲友共同劝告有严重问题的人寻求帮助的一次行动，可数，并不局限于某一种问题。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03011a USE:U03011 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03011a USE:U03011 -->
Her friends planned an intervention: they would meet her together and explain why they thought she needed help.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03011a -->
她的朋友计划共同劝助她：他们会一起见她，解释为什么认为她需要帮助。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03011a -->
<!-- USE:U03012 -->
## intervention /ˌɪntərˈvenʃən/ n.
`为帮助各方解决争执而居间介入的调停行为。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03012a USE:U03012 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03012a USE:U03012 -->
The mediator's intervention helped the parties resume talks.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03012a -->
调停者的介入帮助各方重新开始会谈。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03012a -->
<!-- USE:U03013 -->
## interventionist /ˌɪntərˈvenʃənɪst/ adj.
`倾向介入并非自身直接事务的问题或危机的，常见于新闻语境，尤其描述机构或政府的政策。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03013a USE:U03013 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03013a USE:U03013 -->
An interventionist industrial policy gives the government an active role in shaping which industries receive support.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03013a -->
干预型产业政策使政府积极参与决定哪些产业获得支持。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03013a -->
<!-- USE:U03014 -->
## interventionist /ˌɪntərˈvenʃənɪst/ n.
`支持干预政策的人，可数，常见于新闻语境。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03014a USE:U03014 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03014a USE:U03014 -->
An interventionist argued that the government should act.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03014a -->
一位干预政策支持者认为，政府应当采取行动。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03014a -->
<!-- USE:U03015 -->
## interventionism /ˌɪntərˈvenʃənɪzəm/ n.
`主张或实行干预的政策、观点。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03015a USE:U03015 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03015a USE:U03015 -->
Interventionism is the policy position under discussion, not the name of one particular intervention.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03015a -->
讨论的是干预主义这一政策立场，而不是某一次具体干预的名称。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03015a -->

<!-- WORD:W031 -->
# isolate

**词源**：历史形式经法语、意大利语联系到拉丁语 insula“岛”；基本意象是“使像岛一样分开”。isolate 与 insulate 历史相关，但现代用法须按具体对象区分。

<!-- USE:U03101 -->
## isolate /ˈaɪsəleɪt/ v.
`使人、组织或国家失去朋友、支持或联系。及物，常说 isolate somebody from others，可用于社会关系或政治支持关系。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03101a USE:U03101 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03101a USE:U03101 -->
This policy could isolate the country from the other permanent members of the United Nations Security Council.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03101a -->
这项政策可能使该国在联合国安理会其他常任理事国之间陷入孤立。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03101a -->
<!-- USE:U03102 -->
## isolate /ˈaɪsəleɪt/ v.
`把人或事物在空间上分开，或使人与他人在社会联系上隔绝。及物，也可用 isolate oneself 表示使自己离群，不必涉及传染病。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03102a USE:U03102 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03102a USE:U03102 -->
The partition isolates the work area from the rest of the room.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03102a -->
隔断把工作区与房间其余部分隔开。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03102a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03102b USE:U03102 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03102b USE:U03102 -->
His radicalism and refusal to compromise isolated him.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03102b -->
他的激进和拒绝妥协使他与别人疏离。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03102b -->
<!-- USE:U03103 -->
## isolate /ˈaɪsəleɪt/ v.
`从相互关联的想法、问题等中辨识并分离出某一项，以便单独关注。及物，如 isolate a problem，isolate A from B。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03103a USE:U03103 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03103a USE:U03103 -->
To debug the failure, first isolate the problem instead of changing every component at once.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03103a -->
要排查故障，先把问题单独定位出来，而不是同时改动所有部件。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03103a -->
<!-- USE:U03104 -->
## isolate /ˈaɪsəleɪt/ v.
`用科学方法从混合材料中分离取得某种物质或成分。属于技术、化学用法，及物，常接 from 表明分离来源。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03104a USE:U03104 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"从混合培养物中分离细菌","sourceUrls":["https://openstax.org/books/microbiology/pages/9-6-media-used-for-bacterial-growth"],"displayContext":"OpenStax Microbiology §9.6（CC BY-NC-SA4.0）；实验原理局部缩编。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03104a USE:U03104 -->
To isolate bacteria that break down crude oil, repeatedly transfer the culture into a medium whose only carbon source is crude oil. This enriches the culture with oil-eating bacteria.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03104a -->
若要分离能分解原油的细菌，可以将培养物反复转入唯一碳源是原油的培养基中。这会富集能利用原油的细菌。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03104a -->
<!-- USE:U03105 -->
## isolate /ˈaɪsəleɪt/ v.
`把可能有传染性的病人或动物与其他个体分开，以防疾病传播。及物，与一般的社交孤立区分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03105a USE:U03105 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03105a USE:U03105 -->
The veterinary team isolated the sick animals from the rest of the herd to reduce the risk of infection.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03105a -->
兽医团队把患病动物与其余畜群分开，以降低感染风险。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03105a -->
<!-- USE:U03106 -->
## isolate /ˈaɪsəleɪt/ v.
`使电气设备在电方面隔绝、绝缘，及物，属于电气用法。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03106a USE:U03106 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03106a USE:U03106 -->
The circuit is electrically isolated from the case.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03106a -->
这个电路与外壳在电气上绝缘隔离。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03106a -->
<!-- USE:U03107 -->
## isolable /ˈaɪsələbəl/ adj.; isolatable /ˈaɪsəleɪtəbəl/ adj.
`能够被隔离、分开或分离取得的，具体意义取决于所描述的对象。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03107a USE:U03107 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03107a USE:U03107 -->
The compound is isolable from the mixture.
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03107a2 USE:U03107 -->
It is isolatable without destroying its structure.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03107a -->
这种化合物可以从混合物中分离取得，也可以在不破坏其结构的情况下分离。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03107a -->
<!-- USE:U03108 -->
## isolator /ˈaɪsəleɪtər/ n.
`执行隔离或分离功能的人、设备或装置，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03108a USE:U03108 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"药房中的隔离器","sourceUrls":["https://www.uhleicester.nhs.uk/news/new-5-million-pharmacy-facility-to-support-cancer-care-and-clinical-research-at-leicesters-hospitals/"],"displayContext":"Leicester医院建设项目的当时计划；装置说明局部缩编，不代表当前完工状态。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03108a USE:U03108 -->
In September 2026, University Hospitals of Leicester announced plans for a pharmacy suite with clean rooms and isolators.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03108a_scope USE:U03108 -->
An isolator is a sealed workstation that protects sterile medicines from contamination during preparation.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03108a -->
2026年9月，莱斯特大学医院宣布计划建设配有洁净室和隔离器的药房套间。隔离器是一种密封工作站，在配制期间保护无菌药品免受污染。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03108a -->
<!-- USE:U03109 -->
## isolated /ˈaɪsəleɪtɪd/ adj.
`地点偏远、远离城镇或其他联系点而难以到达的，如 an isolated farmhouse。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03109a USE:U03109 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03109a USE:U03109 -->
They found an isolated farmhouse.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03109a -->
他们找到了一所偏僻的农舍。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03109a -->
<!-- USE:U03110 -->
## isolated /ˈaɪsəleɪtɪd/ adj.
`缺少朋友、帮助或社会联系的，可描述孤独的感受，也可描述与他人隔开的状态。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03110a USE:U03110 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03110a USE:U03110 -->
She described feeling isolated.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03110a -->
她描述了自己孤独无援的感受。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03110a -->
<!-- USE:U03111 -->
## isolated /ˈaɪsəleɪtɪd/ adj.
`单独出现、不常见或不具典型性的，通常放在名词前，如 an isolated case、an isolated example。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03111a USE:U03111 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03111a USE:U03111 -->
They said the allegations related to an isolated case of cheating.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03111a -->
他们说，这些指控涉及一宗个别的作弊事件。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03111a -->
<!-- USE:U03112 -->
## isolation /ˌaɪsəˈleɪʃən/ n.
`使事物分开的行为或实例，以及处于分离的状态，通常不可数，可指外交、社会关系或实际空间上的隔绝。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03112a USE:U03112 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"不同岛屿上的隔离","sourceUrls":["https://www.nhm.ac.uk/discover/what-is-natural-selection.html"],"displayContext":"自然历史博物馆说明；局部缩编。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03112a USE:U03112 -->
Differences in the finches’ beaks correspond to their different foods and to isolation on separate islands.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03112a -->
雀鸟喙的差异，与它们不同的食物及在不同岛屿上的隔离相对应。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03112a -->
<!-- USE:U03113 -->
## isolation /ˌaɪsəˈleɪʃən/ n.
`感到独自一人、缺少朋友或帮助的状态，通常不可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03113a USE:U03113 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03113a USE:U03113 -->
Working from home without regular contact left her with a sense of isolation.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03113a -->
居家工作又缺少经常联系，使她感到孤独。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03113a -->
<!-- USE:U03114 -->
## isolation /ˌaɪsəˈleɪʃən/ n.
`在 in isolation 中表示不结合其他相关事物而单独考虑，常接 from 表明被排除在考虑之外的关联对象。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03114a USE:U03114 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03114a USE:U03114 -->
Punishment cannot be discussed in isolation from social theory.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03114a -->
不能脱离社会理论孤立地讨论惩罚。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03114a -->
<!-- USE:U03115 -->
## isolation /ˌaɪsəˈleɪʃən/ n.
`在 in isolation 中表示没有他人在场或帮助而独自做某事，描述做事方式，不等于感到孤独。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03115a USE:U03115 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03115a USE:U03115 -->
Malcolm works in isolation but I have no doubts about his abilities.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03115a -->
马尔科姆独自工作，但我对他的能力毫不怀疑。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03115a -->
<!-- USE:U03116 -->
## isolation /ˌaɪsəˈleɪʃən/ n.
`作定语表示供传染病人隔离使用，如 isolation ward、isolation hospital；isolation 在这里仍是名词。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03116a USE:U03116 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03116a USE:U03116 -->
The patient was transferred to an isolation ward.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03116a -->
病人被转入了隔离病房。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03116a -->
<!-- USE:U03117 -->
## isolationism /ˌaɪsəˈleɪʃənɪzəm/ n.
`避免密切参与他国或其他群体事务、在其争端中不选边的政策，尤用于政治和国际关系。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03117a USE:U03117 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"精确主义艺术的历史环境","sourceUrls":["https://www.metmuseum.org/essays/precisionism"],"displayContext":"Met艺术史介绍；两段历史背景的局部缩编。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03117a USE:U03117 -->
After the First World War and during the hardships of the Great Depression, the United States entered a period of political isolationism. Cultural critics looked to the country’s own history and regional traditions.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03117a -->
第一次世界大战之后及大萧条的困顿时期，美国进入了一段政治孤立主义阶段。文化评论家把目光转向本国历史和地方传统。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03117a -->
<!-- USE:U03118 -->
## isolationist /ˌaɪsəˈleɪʃənɪst/ n.
`支持孤立主义政策的人，可数，也可放在另一名词前作修饰成分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03118a USE:U03118 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03118a USE:U03118 -->
The government had to overcome isolationist opposition to the plan.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03118a -->
政府必须克服孤立主义支持者对该计划的反对。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03118a -->

<!-- WORD:W032 -->
# medium

**词源**：拉丁语 medium 是 medius“中间的”的中性形式。它既可表示中间程度，也可指夹在两者之间、让某种作用传递的材料或手段；通灵人的名称同样来自居间传话的观念。普通介质、媒介的复数可用 media 或 mediums；通灵人的复数用 mediums。

<!-- USE:U03201 -->
## medium /ˈmidiəm/ adj.
`大小、身高等处于大与小之间的，常放在名词前；medium-sized 也表示中等大小。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03201a USE:U03201 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03201a USE:U03201 -->
He was of medium height with blond hair and light blue eyes.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03201a -->
他中等身高，金发，浅蓝色眼睛。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03201a -->
<!-- USE:U03202 -->
## medium /ˈmidiəm/ adj.
`数量、强度或程度处在两个极端之间、适中的。具体分档取决于对象和尺度，不自动表示统计平均值。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03202a USE:U03202 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03202a USE:U03202 -->
They served a sweetish, medium-strength beer.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03202a -->
他们供应一种略甜、酒精强度中等的啤酒。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03202a -->
<!-- USE:U03203 -->
## medium /ˈmidiəm/ adj.
`颜色深浅介于浅色与深色之间的，如 medium brown。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03203a USE:U03203 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03203a USE:U03203 -->
Andrea has medium brown hair, grey eyes and very pale skin.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03203a -->
安德烈娅的头发是中等深浅的棕色，眼睛灰色，皮肤十分白皙。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03203a -->
<!-- USE:U03204 -->
## medium /ˈmidiəm/ adv.
`以中等程度，用来修饰形容词，如 medium-hot、medium-sized。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03204a USE:U03204 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03204a USE:U03204 -->
Cook under a medium-hot grill.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03204a -->
在中等热度的烤架下烹烤。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03204a -->
<!-- USE:U03205 -->
## medium /ˈmidiəm/ n.
`表达思想或与人交流所用的方式、媒介，可数，如语言、声音、电影、电视；medium of instruction 指教学所用的语言。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03205a USE:U03205 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"语言作为教学媒介","sourceUrls":["https://www.unesco.org/en/articles/new-unesco-report-calls-multilingual-education-unlock-learning-and-inclusion?hub=66908"],"displayContext":"UNESCO于2025年多语教育介绍所引统计；不是当前实时语言普查。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03205a USE:U03205 -->
In an article introducing its 2025 multilingual-education report, UNESCO cited its World Atlas of Languages: 7,000 languages were in use worldwide, but only 351 were used as a medium of instruction.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03205a -->
联合国教科文组织介绍其2025年多语教育报告的文章引用《世界语言地图集》的统计：有七千种语言在使用，但其中只有三百五十一种被用作教学语言。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03205a -->
<!-- USE:U03206 -->
## medium /ˈmidiəm/ n.
`为实现某种用途或效果而使用的物质、材料，可数，如运载氧的血液或供艺术创作的油画颜料。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03206a USE:U03206 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03206a USE:U03206 -->
Blood is the medium in which oxygen is carried to all parts of the body.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03206a -->
血液是把氧输送到全身各处的介质。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03206a -->
<!-- USE:U03207 -->
## medium /ˈmidiəm/ n.
`感觉信息或物理作用所经过的中间物质，即介质，可数，如光通过的空气、水或玻璃。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03207a USE:U03207 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03207a USE:U03207 -->
Light can change direction as it passes from air into water, from one medium into another.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03207a -->
光从空气进入水、也就是从一种介质进入另一种介质时，传播方向可能改变。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03207a -->
<!-- USE:U03208 -->
## medium /ˈmidiəm/ n.
`生物生长、储存或运输所处的物理环境或条件，可数，属于生物学用法。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03208a USE:U03208 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03208a USE:U03208 -->
The shape of a fish is ideal for its fluid medium.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03208a -->
鱼的外形非常适合它所处的流体环境。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03208a -->
<!-- USE:U03209 -->
## medium /ˈmidiəm/ n.
`供微生物等生长的培养环境或物质，即培养基，可数，是生物环境义的一种具体应用。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03209a USE:U03209 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"培养基为什么有不同类型？","sourceUrls":["https://openstax.org/books/microbiology/pages/9-6-media-used-for-bacterial-growth"],"displayContext":"OpenStax Microbiology §9.6（CC BY-NC-SA4.0）；局部缩编。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03209a USE:U03209 -->
An all-purpose medium can support many kinds of microorganisms. Other media supply extra nutrients needed by organisms that cannot make those nutrients themselves.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03209a -->
通用培养基可以支持多种微生物生长。另一些培养基则提供某些生物无法自行合成、因而需要额外获得的营养物质。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03209a -->
<!-- USE:U03210 -->
## medium /ˈmidiəm/ n.
`实现某事的途径、机构或手段，可数；the medium through which 后说明借此完成的事情。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03210a USE:U03210 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03210a USE:U03210 -->
The charity used the concert as the medium through which it raised money for repairs.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03210a -->
慈善机构把音乐会用作筹集修缮资金的途径。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03210a -->
<!-- USE:U03211 -->
## medium /ˈmidiəm/ n.
`艺术家、作曲家等采用的创作材料或表现形式，可数，如语言、雕塑或绘画。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03211a USE:U03211 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"艺术家如何谈自己的媒介","sourceUrls":["https://www.getty.edu/news/artist-dialogues-getty-conservation-lita-albuquerque-malibu-line/"],"displayContext":"Getty Conservation Institute项目介绍；局部缩编。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03211a USE:U03211 -->
The Artist Dialogues videos interview contemporary artists working in different genres and mediums, exploring their materials, methods, and views on conservation.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03211a -->
“艺术家对话”视频采访了运用不同类型和媒介创作的当代艺术家，探讨他们的材料、方法和对作品保护的看法。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03211a -->
<!-- USE:U03212 -->
## medium /ˈmidiəm/ n.
`绘画时与色料混合的油、凝胶等调和材料，可数；可起黏结或调节性能的作用，不一概等同于挥发性溶剂。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03212a USE:U03212 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03212a USE:U03212 -->
The painter mixed the pigment with an oil-based medium.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03212a -->
画家把色料与油性调和剂混合。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03212a -->
<!-- USE:U03213 -->
## medium /ˈmidiəm/ n.
`声称能与死者灵魂接触并在生者与死者之间传话的人，可数，复数用 mediums。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03213a USE:U03213 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03213a USE:U03213 -->
The medium claimed to carry messages from the dead.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03213a -->
那位通灵人声称能传递死者的信息。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03213a -->
<!-- USE:U03214 -->
## medium /ˈmidiəm/ n.
`两个极端之间的中间状态、质量或程度，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03214a USE:U03214 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03214a USE:U03214 -->
They sought a medium between the two extremes.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03214a -->
他们寻求两个极端之间的中间状态。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03214a -->
<!-- USE:U03215 -->
## a happy medium /ə ˈhæpi ˈmidiəm/ n.
`相反极端之间合理、合适的折中。常说 strike/find a happy medium between A and B；happy 在这里指恰当、令人满意。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03215a USE:U03215 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03215a USE:U03215 -->
I aim to strike a happy medium between playing entertaining football and getting results.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03215a -->
我的目标是在踢出好看的足球和取得比赛成绩之间找到合理的平衡。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03215a -->
<!-- USE:U03216 -->
## mediumism /ˈmidiəmɪzəm/ n.; mediumship /ˈmidiəmʃɪp/ n.
`通灵人的活动、身份或所声称的能力，限于 medium 的通灵人义，不用于大众传播或物理介质。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03216a USE:U03216 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03216a USE:U03216 -->
The exhibition compares mediumism with other spiritualist practices and examines claims of mediumship.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03216a -->
展览比较通灵活动与其他唯灵论实践，并考察有关通灵能力的声称。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03216a -->
<!-- USE:U03217 -->
## mediumistic /ˌmidiəˈmɪstɪk/ adj.
`与通灵人或其活动有关的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03217a USE:U03217 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03217a USE:U03217 -->
A mediumistic performance may be presented as communication with the dead, but that description does not establish that the claim is true.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03217a -->
通灵表演可能被呈现为与死者沟通，但这样的描述并不能证明所声称的能力真实。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03217a -->
<!-- USE:U03218 -->
## media /ˈmidiə/ n.
`作为整体看待的报纸、广播、电视等大众传播媒介，常用 the media，可配单数或复数谓语。media 也可以只是 medium 的复数形式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03218a USE:U03218 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03218a USE:U03218 -->
It is hard work and not a glamorous job as portrayed by the media.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03218a -->
这份工作很辛苦，并不像媒体描绘的那样光鲜。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03218a -->
<!-- USE:U03219 -->
## mass media /ˌmæs ˈmidiə/ n.
`向大量公众提供新闻和信息的各种传播渠道，尤其指电视、广播、报纸和杂志。作为整体名称，可配单数或复数谓语。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03219a USE:U03219 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03219a USE:U03219 -->
Radio, television, and newspapers carried the announcement to the public through the mass media.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03219a -->
公告通过广播、电视和报纸这些大众传播渠道传向公众。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03219a -->
<!-- USE:U03220 -->
## multimedia /ˌmʌltiˈmidiə/ adj.
`艺术、教育等采用不止一种表达或传播媒介的，通常放在名词前。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03220a USE:U03220 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03220a USE:U03220 -->
The school staged a multimedia performance combining dance, projected images and music.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03220a -->
学校上演了一场结合舞蹈、投影图像与音乐的多媒体演出。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03220a -->
<!-- USE:U03221 -->
## multimedia /ˌmʌltiˈmidiə/ n.; hypermedia /ˌhaɪpərˈmidiə/ n.
`把文字、声音、图像、影片等结合并相互链接的计算机内容或产品，不可数，也可作名词修饰语；hypermedia 尤其强调超文本中的跨媒介链接。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03221a USE:U03221 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03221a USE:U03221 -->
Hypermedia links a passage of text to audio and video.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03221a -->
超媒体把一段文字与音频和视频链接起来。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03221a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03221b USE:U03221 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03221b USE:U03221 -->
The multimedia application combines text, recorded speech, pictures, and short films.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03221b -->
这个多媒体应用把文字、录音讲话、图片和短片结合在一起。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03221b -->
<!-- USE:U03222 -->
## multimedia /ˌmʌltiˈmidiə/ n.
`教学中把书本、电视等不同媒介结合使用，不可数，不限于单一计算机程序。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03222a USE:U03222 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03222a USE:U03222 -->
Multimedia in the lesson combined books with television recordings.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03222a -->
这节课的多媒体教学把书本和电视录像结合起来。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03222a -->

<!-- WORD:W033 -->
# migrate

**词源**：联系到拉丁语 migrare“从一处移到另一处”。migration 是相应的过程名词；由地点改变，区分人的移居、动物季节迁徙与自然力造成的移动。

<!-- USE:U03301 -->
## migrate /ˈmaɪɡreɪt/ v.
`人从一个居住地迁往另一处，尤其为寻找工作或短期居住，也可涉及跨国移居。不及物，migrate from/to 后说明迁移的起点或目的地。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03301a USE:U03301 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03301a USE:U03301 -->
People migrate to cities like Jakarta in search of work.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03301a -->
人们迁往雅加达这样的城市寻找工作。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03301a -->
<!-- USE:U03302 -->
## migrate /ˈmaɪɡreɪt/ v.
`鸟、鱼等动物随季节改变生活区域，通常为繁殖或觅食。不及物，可说明起点和终点；是否迁徙及迁徙距离因物种而异。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03302a USE:U03302 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03302a USE:U03302 -->
These fish migrate from freshwater streams to the ocean and later return to breed.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03302a -->
这些鱼从淡水溪流迁往海洋，之后返回繁殖。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03302a -->
<!-- USE:U03303 -->
## migrate /ˈmaɪɡreɪt/ v.
`在自然力量作用下移动、迁移，不及物，主语不限于人或动物。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03303a USE:U03303 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03303a USE:U03303 -->
The dune migrates inland under the prevailing wind.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03303a -->
沙丘在盛行风作用下向内陆移动。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03303a -->
<!-- USE:U03304 -->
## migration /maɪˈɡreɪʃən/ n.
`迁移、移居或迁徙的过程，也可指一次这样的移动，可数或不可数；migration of 后接迁移的主体。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03304a USE:U03304 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"劳动力迁移与照护需求","sourceUrls":["https://www.ilo.org/publications/migrant-workers-care-economy"],"displayContext":"ILO政策简报发表页；仅采用可见摘要，不声称读过报告全文。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03304a USE:U03304 -->
Growing demand for migrant care workers raises questions about labour migration and the protection of workers' rights.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03304a_scope -->
An ILO policy brief calls for policies that address both.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03304a -->
对移民照护工作者不断增长的需求，带来了劳动力迁移和劳动者权利保护方面的问题。国际劳工组织的一份政策简报呼吁政策同时回应这两方面。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03304a -->
<!-- USE:U03305 -->
## migrational /maɪˈɡreɪʃənəl/ adj.
`与迁移、迁徙有关的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03305a USE:U03305 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03305a USE:U03305 -->
The researchers compared the two populations’ migrational patterns.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03305a -->
研究人员比较了两个种群的迁徙模式。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03305a -->
<!-- USE:U03306 -->
## migrator /ˈmaɪɡreɪtər/ n.
`进行迁移、迁徙的人或动物等，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03306a USE:U03306 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03306a USE:U03306 -->
The salmon is a long-distance migrator.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03306a -->
鲑鱼是一种长距离迁徙动物。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03306a -->
<!-- USE:U03307 -->
## migratory /ˈmaɪɡrəˌtɔri/ adj.
`每年随季节迁徙的，用来描述鸟、鱼或其他动物。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03307a USE:U03307 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03307a USE:U03307 -->
The wetland provides a resting place for migratory birds.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03307a -->
这片湿地为候鸟提供歇脚处。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03307a -->
<!-- USE:U03308 -->
## migratory /ˈmaɪɡrəˌtɔri/ adj.
`与人或动物的迁移、迁徙有关的，如 migratory farm labour 指随地区和农时迁移的农业劳动力。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03308a USE:U03308 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03308a USE:U03308 -->
Migratory farm labour follows the harvest from one region to another.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03308a -->
流动性农业劳动力随收获季节从一个地区转往另一个地区。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03308a -->
<!-- USE:U03309 -->
## migrant /ˈmaɪɡrənt/ n./adj.
`从一处迁往另一处的人，尤其为寻找工作而迁移者。作名词时可数，作形容词如 migrant workers；migrant 一词本身不确定其法律身份。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03309a USE:U03309 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"移民照护工作者","sourceUrls":["https://www.ilo.org/publications/migrant-workers-care-economy"],"displayContext":"ILO政策简报发表页摘要；不据migrant一词推定个别人的法律身份。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03309a USE:U03309 -->
An ILO policy brief focuses on migrant care workers, especially women, and the difficulties they face in obtaining decent work and adequate protection.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03309a -->
国际劳工组织的一份政策简报关注移民照护工作者，尤其是女性，以及她们在获得体面工作和充分保护方面面临的困难。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03309a -->
<!-- USE:U03310 -->
## migrant /ˈmaɪɡrənt/ n./adj.
`在不同区域间迁移的动物，尤其鸟类。作名词时可数，也可作形容词，如 migrant birds。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03310a USE:U03310 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"图森巴赫谈迁徙的鸟","sourceUrls":["https://www.gutenberg.org/files/7986/7986-h/7986-h.htm"],"displayContext":"契诃夫《三姐妹》早期英译本；图森巴赫台词节选，文学比喻。","speakers":{"SU03310a":"Tuzenbach (Chekhov, Three Sisters)"}} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03310a USE:U03310 -->
Migrant birds, cranes for example, fly and fly, and whatever thoughts, high or low, enter their heads, they will still fly and not know why or where.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03310a -->
迁徙的鸟，比如鹤，总是飞呀飞。不论高深还是浅薄的念头进入它们脑中，它们仍会飞，却不知道为什么飞、飞向哪里。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03310a -->

<!-- WORD:W034 -->
# modify

**词源**：经法语来自拉丁语 modificare“限定、调节”，联系到 modus“尺度、方式”和表示制作的成分。现代既指局部改动，也可指减轻程度；语法、语音和化学中有专门对象。

<!-- USE:U03401 -->
## modify /ˈmɑdəfaɪ/ v.
`对事物作局部修改、改变或改装，通常为了改善。及物，过去式和过去分词均为 modified。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03401a USE:U03401 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"修改动议的程序条件","sourceUrls":["https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/HMAN-117/html/HMAN-117-pg776.htm"],"displayContext":"美国众议院第117届手册所记规则；局部改写，不作现行程序指导。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03401a USE:U03401 -->
Under the U.S. House procedure recorded in the 117th Congress's manual, once the proponent of a pending motion has been recognized for debate, a unanimous-consent request to modify the motion may be entertained only if that proponent yields for that purpose.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03401a -->
按美国第117届众议院手册记载的程序，一项待决动议的提出者已获准就其辩论后，只有该提出者为此让出发言机会，才可受理请求一致同意修改动议的要求。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03401a -->
<!-- USE:U03402 -->
## modify /ˈmɑdəfaɪ/ v.
`使要求或程度不那么严厉、极端，即缓和、减轻，及物，如 modify one's demands。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03402a USE:U03402 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03402a USE:U03402 -->
They agreed to modify their demands.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03402a -->
他们同意降低要求。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03402a -->
<!-- USE:U03403 -->
## modify /ˈmɑdəfaɪ/ v.
`语法上一个词或词组描述、限定或扩展另一个词的意义，及物；起修饰作用的成分不限于形容词。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03403a USE:U03403 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"名词也能修饰名词","sourceUrls":["https://dictionary.cambridge.org/grammar/british-grammar/adjective-phrases-position"],"displayContext":"Cambridge English Grammar Today；语法说明局部改写。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03403a USE:U03403 -->
A noun can modify another noun by giving more information about it. In “sports car,” “sports” is the noun modifier.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03403a -->
一个名词可以通过提供更多信息来修饰另一个名词。在sports car中，sports就是名词修饰语。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03403a -->
<!-- USE:U03404 -->
## modify /ˈmɑdəfaɪ/ v.
`语音学上改变某个语言单位的语音形式，及物。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03404a USE:U03404 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03404a USE:U03404 -->
The sound change modified the vowel in that syllable.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03404a -->
这一音变改变了该音节中的元音。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03404a -->
<!-- USE:U03405 -->
## modify /ˈmɑdəfaɪ/ v.
`化学上通过改变或替换聚合物的取代基等来改变其溶解性等物理性质，及物，如 modified starch 指改性淀粉。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03405a USE:U03405 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03405a USE:U03405 -->
Modified starch can have properties different from those of the original starch.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03405a -->
改性淀粉的性质可能与原淀粉不同。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03405a -->
<!-- USE:U03406 -->
## modifiable /ˈmɑdəfaɪəbəl/ adj.
`能够被修改、调节或改变的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03406a USE:U03406 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03406a USE:U03406 -->
The settings remain modifiable after installation.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03406a -->
安装后，这些设置仍可修改。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03406a -->
<!-- USE:U03407 -->
## modificatory /ˌmɑdəfɪˈkeɪtəri/ adj.
`起修改、改变作用的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03407a USE:U03407 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03407a USE:U03407 -->
The clause has a modificatory effect on the earlier provision.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03407a -->
这一条款对先前规定起修改作用。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03407a -->
<!-- USE:U03408 -->
## modification /ˌmɑdəfɪˈkeɪʃən/ n.
`修改、改变或缓和的行为、过程或实例，可数或不可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03408a USE:U03408 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03408a USE:U03408 -->
Modification of the design took several weeks.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03408a -->
修改这项设计花了几个星期。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03408a -->
<!-- USE:U03409 -->
## modification /ˌmɑdəfɪˈkeɪʃən/ n.
`已经作出的一项改变、改装或调整，可数，常用复数 modifications。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03409a USE:U03409 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03409a USE:U03409 -->
Relatively minor modifications were required.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03409a -->
需要作出的改动相对较小。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03409a -->
<!-- USE:U03410 -->
## modifier /ˈmɑdəfaɪər/ n.
`起修改或调节作用的人、事物或添加剂，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03410a USE:U03410 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03410a USE:U03410 -->
The additive acts as a viscosity modifier.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03410a -->
这种添加剂起黏度调节剂的作用。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03410a -->
<!-- USE:U03411 -->
## modifier /ˈmɑdəfaɪər/ n.
`语法上限定另一个词意义的词或词组，尤指作定语的形容词或名词；某些语法描述把这个名称限用于名词前的修饰成分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03411a USE:U03411 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"形容词与名词修饰语的次序","sourceUrls":["https://dictionary.cambridge.org/grammar/british-grammar/adjective-phrases-position"],"displayContext":"Cambridge English Grammar Today；局部改写。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SU03411a USE:U03411 -->
Adjectives come before noun modifiers: in “a red sports car,” “red” comes before “sports.”
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03411a -->
形容词位于名词修饰语之前：在a red sports car中，red位于sports之前。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03411a -->

<!-- WORD:W035 -->
# momentum

**词源**：借自拉丁语 momentum，意为运动、推动力量；与 moment 历史相关。把“动起来以后具有的力量”作为记忆线索，但物理定义要用质量与速度的乘积。

<!-- USE:U03501 -->
## momentum /moʊˈmentəm/ n.
`某过程或运动不断发展、加快并越来越难停下来的势头，通常不可数；gain/build momentum 表示形成、增强势头，lose momentum 表示势头减弱。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03501a USE:U03501 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03501a USE:U03501 -->
This campaign is really gaining momentum.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03501a -->
这场行动确实越来越有势头。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03501a -->
<!-- USE:U03502 -->
## momentum /moʊˈmentəm/ n.
`经典力学中运动物体的质量与其速度矢量的乘积，即动量，方向是定义的一部分；复数为 momenta /moʊˈmentə/。动量与使动量改变的冲量是不同概念。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03502a USE:U03502 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"DART撞击后的碎屑","sourceUrls":["https://www.nasa.gov/missions/dart/nasas-dart-mission-changed-orbit-of-asteroid-didymos-around-sun/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03502a USE:U03502 -->
When DART struck Dimorphos, the rocky debris carried momentum away from the asteroid and gave it an extra thrust.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03502a -->
DART撞上迪莫弗斯时，岩石碎屑从小行星带走动量，并给它施加了额外的推力。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03502a -->
<!-- USE:U03503 -->
## momentum /moʊˈmentəm/ n.
`运动开始后形成、使运动继续的推动势头，用于一般运动描述，不必是在计算物理量。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03503a USE:U03503 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03503a USE:U03503 -->
The bicycle's momentum carried it a little farther up the slope.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03503a -->
自行车已有的冲势使它又向坡上滑行了一小段。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03503a -->

<!-- WORD:W036 -->
# mutual

**词源**：经法语来自拉丁语 mutuus“相互的”，历史上联系到交换、改变。先记“彼此作用”，再区分两方互相感受与多方共同拥有。

<!-- USE:U03601 -->
## mutual /ˈmjutʃuəl/ adj.
`双方或多方彼此感受、作出或经历的，如 mutual affection、mutual benefit；the feeling is mutual 表示感情是相互的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03601a USE:U03601 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"1955年万隆会议的原则","sourceUrls":["https://history.state.gov/milestones/1953-1960/bandung-conf"],"displayContext":"1955年会议的历史叙述；该Milestones系列已停止维护。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03601a USE:U03601 -->
The Bandung Conference's principles included mutual respect for sovereignty, non-aggression, and equality.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03601a -->
万隆会议的原则包括相互尊重主权、互不侵犯和平等。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03601a -->
<!-- USE:U03602 -->
## mutual /ˈmjutʃuəl/ adj.
`两人或多人共同具有、涉及同一对象的，如 a mutual friend 指双方共同认识的朋友，a mutual interest 指共同兴趣。这一义曾有用法争议，但在现代英语中广泛使用。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03602a USE:U03602 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03602a USE:U03602 -->
We were introduced by a mutual friend.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03602a -->
一位共同的朋友介绍我们认识。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03602a -->
<!-- USE:U03603 -->
## mutual /ˈmjutʃuəl/ adj.
`彼此处于某种对应、受益或支持关系中的，如 mutual beneficiaries 指互惠者，mutual well-wishers 指彼此祝福的人。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03603a USE:U03603 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03603a USE:U03603 -->
The two organisations are mutual beneficiaries of the arrangement.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03603a -->
这项安排使两个组织彼此受益。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03603a -->
<!-- USE:U03604 -->
## mutual /ˈmjutʃuəl/ adj.
`由顾客或成员、而非外部股东拥有，并让成员分享收益的。属于英式商业用法，可描述保险机构、建房互助协会等的组织性质。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03604a USE:U03604 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"成员所有的金融机构","sourceUrls":["https://www.bsa.org.uk/about-us/about-the-bsa"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03604a USE:U03604 -->
Building societies are mutual institutions: their members own them, and they operate for their members' benefit.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03604a -->
建房互助协会是成员互助型机构：机构归成员所有，并为成员的利益运营。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03604a -->
<!-- USE:U03605 -->
## mutuality /ˌmjutʃuˈæləti/ n.
`相互性、共同性或互助关系的性质，具体意义取决于所谈关系。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03605a USE:U03605 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03605a USE:U03605 -->
The partnership depends on mutuality rather than one-sided assistance.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03605a -->
这种伙伴关系依赖相互性，而非单方面援助。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03605a -->
<!-- USE:U03606 -->
## mutually /ˈmjutʃuəli/ adv.
`相互地，或由相关各方共同地，可放在形容词、副词或动词前，如 mutually agreed 表示各方共同同意的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03606a USE:U03606 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03606a USE:U03606 -->
Attempts to reach a mutually agreed solution had been fruitless.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03606a -->
试图达成各方共同同意的解决方案，结果毫无收获。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03606a -->
<!-- USE:U03607 -->
## mutually exclusive /ˌmjutʃuəli ɪkˈsklusɪv/ adj.
`相互排斥、不能同时成立或发生的；强调不能并存，而非仅仅彼此不同。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03607a USE:U03607 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03607a USE:U03607 -->
They both have learnt that ambition and successful fatherhood can be mutually exclusive.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03607a -->
他们都已体会到，事业雄心和成功履行父亲职责有时可能无法兼得。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03607a -->

<!-- WORD:W037 -->
# orbit

**词源**：来自拉丁语 orbita“车轮留下的轨迹、路线”。英语早期就有眼窝义，后来又借来表达天体运行轨道；orbital 是相关形容词，也有量子物理名词义，不能全都理解成一条可画出的行星路线。

<!-- USE:U03701 -->
## orbit /ˈɔrbɪt/ n.
`行星、卫星等围绕另一天体运行所沿的弯曲路径，可数或不可数；an orbit around a star 指围绕恒星的轨道。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03701a USE:U03701 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"撞击改变轨道","sourceUrls":["https://www.nasa.gov/missions/dart/nasas-dart-mission-changed-orbit-of-asteroid-didymos-around-sun/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03701a USE:U03701 -->
DART's impact on Dimorphos also shifted the orbit of the asteroid pair around the Sun.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03701a -->
DART对迪莫弗斯的撞击，也改变了这对小行星绕太阳运行的轨道。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03701a -->
<!-- USE:U03702 -->
## orbit /ˈɔrbɪt/ n.
`沿轨道运动的状态。in orbit 表示在轨运行，into orbit 表示进入轨道，out of orbit 表示脱离轨道。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03702a USE:U03702 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03702a USE:U03702 -->
The planet is probably in orbit around a small star.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03702a -->
这颗行星很可能正围绕一颗小恒星运行。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03702a -->
<!-- USE:U03703 -->
## orbit /ˈɔrbɪt/ n.
`围绕某个天体完整运行的一圈，可数，强调一次完整周回。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03703a USE:U03703 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03703a USE:U03703 -->
The spacecraft completed three orbits before the manoeuvre.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03703a -->
航天器在机动操作前完成了三圈运行。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03703a -->
<!-- USE:U03704 -->
## orbit /ˈɔrbɪt/ n.
`原子模型中电子围绕原子核运动的轨道图式，属于物理学用法；不能直接等同于现代量子力学中以波函数描述的 orbital。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03704a USE:U03704 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03704a USE:U03704 -->
The Bohr model represents the electron in an orbit around the nucleus.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03704a -->
玻尔模型把电子表示为沿原子核周围的一条轨道运动。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03704a -->
<!-- USE:U03705 -->
## orbit /ˈɔrbɪt/ n.
`某人或组织发挥行动、影响的范围，常用单数；within/in the orbit of somebody/something 表示处于其活动或影响范围内。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03705a USE:U03705 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03705a USE:U03705 -->
He is a man who still commands enormous respect within the orbit of football club management.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03705a -->
在足球俱乐部管理界，他仍然深受尊敬。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03705a -->
<!-- USE:U03706 -->
## orbit /ˈɔrbɪt/ n.
`解剖学上容纳眼球的眼眶、眼窝。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03706a USE:U03706 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03706a USE:U03706 -->
The skull preserves the left orbit.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03706a -->
这件头骨保存着左侧眼窝。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03706a -->
<!-- USE:U03707 -->
## orbit /ˈɔrbɪt/ n.
`鸟或昆虫眼睛周围的区域，用于动物形态描述，不一定指骨性的眼窝。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03707a USE:U03707 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03707a USE:U03707 -->
The bird has a conspicuous ring around its orbit.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03707a -->
这只鸟的眼周有一圈醒目的环纹。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03707a -->
<!-- USE:U03708 -->
## orbit /ˈɔrbɪt/ v.
`沿轨道围绕某个天体运行，既可及物说 orbit the Earth，也可不及物并接 around 说明环绕对象。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03708a USE:U03708 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"双小行星的绕行","sourceUrls":["https://www.nasa.gov/missions/dart/nasas-dart-mission-changed-orbit-of-asteroid-didymos-around-sun/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03708a USE:U03708 -->
Linked by gravity, Didymos and Dimorphos orbit each other around a shared centre of mass.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03708a -->
在引力联系下，迪迪莫斯与迪莫弗斯围绕共同质心彼此绕行。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03708a -->
<!-- USE:U03709 -->
## orbit /ˈɔrbɪt/ v.
`沿圆形路线飞行、盘旋，不及物，不要求飞行器处于太空轨道。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03709a USE:U03709 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"飓风眼周围的观测飞行","sourceUrls":["https://www.nasa.gov/centers-and-facilities/marshall/the-marshall-star-408/"],"displayContext":"2023年9月飓风观测报道。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03709a USE:U03709 -->
During a September 2023 research flight, a P-3 aircraft orbited around Hurricane Lee's eye while its tail-mounted Doppler radar collected data on both eyewalls.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03709a -->
在2023年9月的一次科研飞行中，一架P-3飞机围绕飓风“李”的风眼盘旋，同时由尾部安装的多普勒雷达收集两层眼墙的数据。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03709a -->
<!-- USE:U03710 -->
## orbit /ˈɔrbɪt/ v.
`把某物送上轨道，使其在轨运行，及物，宾语是被送入轨道的对象。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03710a USE:U03710 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03710a USE:U03710 -->
The launcher can orbit a small satellite.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03710a -->
这枚运载火箭能够把一颗小卫星送入轨道。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03710a -->
<!-- USE:U03711 -->
## orbital /ˈɔrbɪtəl/ adj.
`与天体或电子轨道有关的，用于天文学、物理学，通常放在名词前。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03711a USE:U03711 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"测量轨道周期","sourceUrls":["https://www.nasa.gov/missions/dart/nasas-dart-mission-changed-orbit-of-asteroid-didymos-around-sun/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03711a USE:U03711 -->
After DART's impact, the asteroid pair's 770-day orbital period around the Sun changed by a fraction of a second.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03711a -->
DART撞击之后，这对小行星绕太阳的770天轨道周期改变了不足一秒。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03711a -->
<!-- USE:U03712 -->
## orbital /ˈɔrbɪtəl/ adj.
`解剖学上与眼窝、眼眶有关的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03712a USE:U03712 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03712a USE:U03712 -->
The scan shows the orbital bones.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03712a -->
扫描图像显示了眼眶骨。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03712a -->
<!-- USE:U03713 -->
## orbital /ˈɔrbɪtəl/ adj.
`道路沿城市外围绕行的，主要为英式用法；美式英语常把这类道路称为 beltway。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03713a USE:U03713 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03713a USE:U03713 -->
An early idea for a London orbital road, a predecessor of the M25, was proposed in 1905.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03713a -->
1905年曾有人提出伦敦环城道路的早期设想，这是后来M25的前身。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03713a -->
<!-- USE:U03714 -->
## orbital /ˈɔrbɪtəl/ n.
`物理学上用波函数表示的原子核周围电子密度模式或相应状态，即轨函数，可数；不表示电子像行星一样沿某条确定路线转动。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03714a USE:U03714 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03714a USE:U03714 -->
An orbital describes a possible electron-density pattern around an atomic nucleus.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03714a -->
一个轨函数描述原子核周围一种可能的电子密度模式。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03714a -->
<!-- USE:U03715 -->
## orbiter /ˈɔrbɪtər/ n.
`设计为沿轨道运行、尤其长时间留在轨道上的航天器，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03715a USE:U03715 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"奥德赛号的测绘任务","sourceUrls":["https://www.nasa.gov/centers-and-facilities/jpl/nasas-odyssey-orbiter-marks-20-historic-years-of-mapping-mars/"],"displayContext":"2021年任务二十周年报道中的任务回顾。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03715a USE:U03715 -->
NASA's Odyssey orbiter was sent to map the composition of the Martian surface, helping scientists investigate how the planet evolved.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03715a -->
NASA的奥德赛号轨道飞行器被送去测绘火星表面组成，帮助科学家研究这颗行星如何演变。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03715a -->

<!-- WORD:W038 -->
# persist

**词源**：来自拉丁语 persistere“坚定地继续站住”，含加强意味的前缀和表示站立的成分。由不退开，联系到人的坚持，也联系到某种状态持续不消失；坚持并不总是褒义。

<!-- USE:U03801 -->
## persist /pərˈsɪst/ v.
`现象、制度或状况继续存在而未消失，不及物，尤其常用于不希望继续的情况。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03801a USE:U03801 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"华莱士的一项个人信念","sourceUrls":["https://www.nhm.ac.uk/discover/who-was-alfred-russel-wallace.html"],"displayContext":"人物思想史材料，以下是华莱士个人信念，不是得到验证的科学结论。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03801a USE:U03801 -->
Wallace believed that natural selection could not explain human intellect and that the human spirit persisted after death.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03801a -->
华莱士相信，自然选择无法解释人类智力，而且人的灵魂在死后仍继续存在。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03801a -->
<!-- USE:U03802 -->
## persist /pərˈsɪst/ v.
`尽管遇到困难、反对或劝阻，仍坚定或固执地坚持意见或行动。不及物，常说 persist in doing something、persist with/in one's efforts；也可与直接引语连用，表示坚持追问，或在上下文明确时单用 persist。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03802a USE:U03802 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03802a USE:U03802 -->
He urged the United States to persist with its efforts to bring about peace.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03802a -->
他敦促美国继续坚持促成和平的努力。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03802a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03802b USE:U03802 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03802b USE:U03802 -->
'You haven't answered me,' she persisted.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03802b -->
“你还没有回答我。”她坚持追问。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03802b -->
<!-- USE:U03803 -->
## persistence /pərˈsɪstəns/ n.; persistency /pərˈsɪstənsi/ n.
`面对困难或反对仍继续努力的品质、行为，通常不可数；persistency 是相应的另一名词形式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03803a USE:U03803 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03803a USE:U03803 -->
Skill comes only with practice, patience and persistence.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03803a -->
技能只能通过练习、耐心和坚持获得。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03803a -->
<!-- USE:U03804 -->
## persistence /pərˈsɪstəns/ n.; persistency /pərˈsɪstənsi/ n.
`状态或现象长时间继续存在的事实，常接 of，尤其用于问题等不希望延续的事物。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03804a USE:U03804 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03804a USE:U03804 -->
The statement expressed concern at the persistence of inflation and high interest rates.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03804a -->
这份声明对通货膨胀和高利率持续存在表示担忧。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03804a -->
<!-- USE:U03805 -->
## persistent /pərˈsɪstənt/ adj.
`长时间不消失或持续发生的，尤其常描述不良状况、雨或症状等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03805a USE:U03805 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"初步研究中的持续疲劳","sourceUrls":["https://www.nccih.nih.gov/health/complementary-alternative-or-integrative-health-whats-in-a-name"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03805a USE:U03805 -->
A preliminary study suggested that yoga may relieve the persistent fatigue experienced by some women after breast cancer treatment.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03805a -->
一项初步研究提示，瑜伽可能缓解部分女性在乳腺癌治疗后经历的持续疲劳。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03805a -->
<!-- USE:U03806 -->
## persistent /pərˈsɪstənt/ adj.
`遇到困难或反对仍继续尝试的，可描述人或其行为，也可能带有执意不改的意味。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03806a USE:U03806 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03806a USE:U03806 -->
She is a persistent critic of the government's transport policies.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03806a -->
她一直坚持批评政府的交通政策。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03806a -->
<!-- USE:U03807 -->
## persistent /pərˈsɪstənt/ adj.
`不断重复发生的，强调反复，如 persistent nagging 指不停的唠叨。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03807a USE:U03807 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03807a USE:U03807 -->
He complained about the persistent nagging.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03807a -->
他抱怨那些没完没了的唠叨。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03807a -->
<!-- USE:U03808 -->
## persistent /pərˈsɪstənt/ adj.
`生物学上指通常应脱落的叶、角等仍保留着的，即宿存的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03808a USE:U03808 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03808a USE:U03808 -->
A persistent calyx remains around the fruit after flowering.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03808a -->
宿存花萼在开花后仍留在果实周围。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03808a -->
<!-- USE:U03809 -->
## persistently /pərˈsɪstəntli/ adv.
`长时间或一次又一次地，说明状态持续或动作反复。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03809a USE:U03809 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03809a USE:U03809 -->
The allegations have been persistently denied by ministers.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03809a -->
部长们一再否认这些指控。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03809a -->
<!-- USE:U03810 -->
## persistently /pərˈsɪstəntli/ adv.
`尽管遇到困难或反对，仍有决心地继续，说明行动的执着。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03810a USE:U03810 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03810a USE:U03810 -->
Rachel gently but persistently imposed her will upon Douglas.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03810a -->
雷切尔温和却坚持不懈地让道格拉斯接受她的意愿。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03810a -->

<!-- WORD:W039 -->
# pigment

**词源**：来自拉丁语 pigmentum“颜料、涂色物”，联系到表示涂色的动词。由给物体颜色的材料，扩展到生物组织中的天然色素；pigmentation 可指呈色或色素沉积。

<!-- USE:U03901 -->
## pigment /ˈpɪɡmənt/ n.
`用于着色、涂绘或染色的物质，通常以不溶性悬浮颗粒存在，可数或不可数。属于较正式的用语，可用于织物、墙面或绘画等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03901a USE:U03901 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"《马里布线》的材料","sourceUrls":["https://www.getty.edu/news/artist-dialogues-getty-conservation-lita-albuquerque-malibu-line/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03901a USE:U03901 -->
In 1978, Lita Albuquerque filled a shallow trench overlooking the Pacific with ultramarine pigment, creating her work Malibu Line.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03901a -->
1978年，莉塔·阿尔伯克基把群青颜料填入一道俯瞰太平洋的浅沟，创作了作品《马里布线》。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03901a -->
<!-- USE:U03902 -->
## pigment /ˈpɪɡmənt/ n.
`赋予动物、植物组织天然颜色的物质，即色素，如叶绿素、血红蛋白等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03902a USE:U03902 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"黑色素与肤色变化","sourceUrls":["https://www.cancer.gov/publications/dictionaries/cancer-terms/def/hyperpigmentation"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03902a USE:U03902 -->
Hyperpigmentation occurs when special skin cells produce too much of the pigment melanin.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03902a -->
当皮肤中的特殊细胞产生过多黑色素这种色素时，就会发生色素沉着过度。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03902a -->
<!-- USE:U03903 -->
## pigment /ˈpɪɡmənt/ v.
`用颜料或类似方式给某物赋予颜色，及物。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03903a USE:U03903 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03903a USE:U03903 -->
The maker pigments the resin before casting it.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03903a -->
制作者在浇铸前给树脂着色。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03903a -->
<!-- USE:U03904 -->
## pigmental /pɪɡˈmentəl/ adj.; pigmentary /ˈpɪɡmənˌteri/ adj.
`与颜料、色素或其作用有关的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03904a USE:U03904 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03904a USE:U03904 -->
The study examines pigmentary changes in the tissue.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03904a -->
这项研究考察组织中的色素相关变化。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03904a -->
<!-- USE:U03905 -->
## pigmentation /ˌpɪɡmənˈteɪʃən/ n.
`动物、植物等由色素形成的天然颜色。属于较正式的用语，通常不可数，可接 of 或 in 说明呈色的部位、对象。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03905a USE:U03905 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03905a USE:U03905 -->
I have a skin disorder that destroys the natural pigmentation in my skin.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03905a -->
我有一种皮肤疾病，会破坏皮肤的天然色素呈色。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03905a -->
<!-- USE:U03906 -->
## pigmentation /ˌpɪɡmənˈteɪʃən/ n.
`色素在组织中过度积聚而造成的呈色，用于描述组织的颜色变化。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU03906a USE:U03906 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU03906a USE:U03906 -->
The examination noted excessive pigmentation in the affected tissue.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU03906a -->
检查记录了受影响组织中过度的色素沉积。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU03906a -->

<!-- WORD:W040 -->
# precise

**词源**：经法语来自拉丁语表示“切断、截短”的形式；“切去含混、多余之处”可辅助记住精确。后来也发展出遵守细节、过于刻板的意味。

<!-- USE:U04001 -->
## precise /prɪˈsaɪs/ adj.
`恰好是所指的那一个，而非含糊所指或另一个，常放在名词前起强调作用；at that precise moment 指恰恰在那个时刻，the precise location 指确切位置。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04001a USE:U04001 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04001a USE:U04001 -->
The precise location of the wreck was discovered in 1988.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04001a -->
沉船的确切位置于1988年被发现。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04001a -->
<!-- USE:U04002 -->
## precise /prɪˈsaɪs/ adj.
`说法、叙述在各项细节上准确清楚的，可描述说明、描述等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04002a USE:U04002 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04002a USE:U04002 -->
He does not talk too much and what he has to say is precise and to the point.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04002a -->
他话不多，所说的内容准确而切题。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04002a -->
<!-- USE:U04003 -->
## precise /prɪˈsaɪs/ adj.
`测量、数值或金额等确切而准确的，与大致估算区分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04003a USE:U04003 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"掩星观测的测量","sourceUrls":["https://www.nasa.gov/missions/dart/nasas-dart-mission-changed-orbit-of-asteroid-didymos-around-sun/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04003a USE:U04003 -->
Stellar occultations provide extremely precise measurements of an asteroid’s speed, shape, and position.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04003a -->
恒星掩星观测能极其精确地测量小行星的速度、形状和位置。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04003a -->
<!-- USE:U04004 -->
## precise /prɪˈsaɪs/ adj.
`在细节、规则等方面一丝不苟、严格求准的，可描述人或行为，也可能带有刻板的意味。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04004a USE:U04004 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04004a USE:U04004 -->
He was precise in observing every procedural rule.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04004a -->
他对每一条程序规则都遵守得一丝不苟。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04004a -->
<!-- USE:U04005 -->
## precise /prɪˈsaɪs/ adj.
`在插入语 to be precise 中表示“确切地说”，用来补充比刚才的说法更准确、具体的信息。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04005a USE:U04005 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04005a USE:U04005 -->
More than a week ago, Thursday evening to be precise, she was at her evening class.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04005a -->
一个多星期前，确切说是星期四晚上，她当时在上夜校。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04005a -->
<!-- USE:U04006 -->
## preciseness /prɪˈsaɪsnəs/ n.
`精确、严谨的性质。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04006a USE:U04006 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04006a USE:U04006 -->
The editor valued the preciseness of the wording.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04006a -->
编辑重视措辞的精确性。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04006a -->
<!-- USE:U04007 -->
## precisely /prɪˈsaɪsli/ adv.
`准确地、恰好地，可说明动作做得准确，也可说明恰好是某个时间或数量。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04007a USE:U04007 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04007a USE:U04007 -->
The meeting began at precisely 4.00 p.m.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04007a -->
会议在下午四点整开始。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04007a -->
<!-- USE:U04008 -->
## precisely /prɪˈsaɪsli/ adv.
`恰恰、正是，用来强调某一理由或事实正是关键所在。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04008a USE:U04008 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04008a USE:U04008 -->
Children come to zoos precisely to see captive animals.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04008a -->
孩子们来动物园，恰恰就是为了看圈养的动物。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04008a -->
<!-- USE:U04009 -->
## precisely /prɪˈsaɪsli/ adv.
`用作应答，明确而有力地肯定对方刚说的话，相当于“正是如此”。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04009a USE:U04009 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04009a USE:U04009 -->
'Did you find yourself wondering what went wrong?'—'Precisely.'
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04009a -->
“你是不是不由得想，到底出了什么问题？”——“正是。”
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04009a -->
<!-- USE:U04010 -->
## precision /prɪˈsɪʒən/ n.
`准确完成某事、符合所需细节的状态或性质，通常不可数，常用 with precision。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04010a USE:U04010 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"韦布遮阳板的展开","sourceUrls":["https://www.nasa.gov/news-release/sunshield-successfully-deploys-on-nasas-next-flagship-telescope/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04010a USE:U04010 -->
Thousands of parts had to work with precision for Webb’s sunshield to unfold fully.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04010a -->
为了让韦布望远镜的遮阳板完全展开，数以千计的零件必须精确配合。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04010a -->
<!-- USE:U04011 -->
## precision /prɪˈsɪʒən/ n.
`测量等细化、精密到什么程度，即精密度；技术语境中须按具体测量定义使用。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04011a USE:U04011 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"测量宇宙膨胀","sourceUrls":["https://science.nasa.gov/mission/hubble/science/science-behind-the-discoveries/hubble-cosmological-redshift/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04011a USE:U04011 -->
The Hubble Space Telescope improved the precision of measurements of the universe’s expansion rate.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04011a -->
哈勃太空望远镜提高了宇宙膨胀率测量的精密程度。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04011a -->
<!-- USE:U04012 -->
## precision /prɪˈsɪʒən/ n.
`作定语表示具有高精度或为精密操作设计，如 precision instruments、precision timing；这里的 precision 仍是名词。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04012a USE:U04012 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04012a USE:U04012 -->
The laboratory uses precision instruments.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04012a -->
实验室使用精密仪器。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04012a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04012b USE:U04012 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04012b USE:U04012 -->
The experiment requires precision timing.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04012b -->
这个实验需要精确计时。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04012b -->
<!-- USE:U04013 -->
## imprecise /ˌɪmprɪˈsaɪs/ adj.
`不清楚、不准确或不精确的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04013a USE:U04013 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04013a USE:U04013 -->
The charges were vague and imprecise.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04013a -->
这些指控含糊不清，不够准确。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04013a -->
<!-- USE:U04014 -->
## imprecisely /ˌɪmprɪˈsaɪsli/ adv.
`以不精确、不准确的方式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04014a USE:U04014 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04014a USE:U04014 -->
The boundary was imprecisely drawn.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04014a -->
这条边界画得不准确。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04014a -->
<!-- USE:U04015 -->
## imprecision /ˌɪmprɪˈsɪʒən/ n.; impreciseness /ˌɪmprɪˈsaɪsnəs/ n.
`不准确、不精确的性质。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04015a USE:U04015 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04015a USE:U04015 -->
This served to hide the confusion and imprecision in their thinking.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04015a -->
这掩盖了他们思路中的混乱与不精确。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04015a -->
<!-- USE:U04016 -->
## Precisionism /prɪˈsɪʒənɪzəm/ n.
`精确主义，主要指二十世纪二三十年代以清晰轮廓、抽象化几何形式和静态构图表现工业、建筑等主题的美国绘画风格；常首字母大写。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04016a USE:U04016 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"机器时代的精确主义","sourceUrls":["https://www.metmuseum.org/essays/precisionism"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04016a USE:U04016 -->
Precisionism coincided with exhibitions in Paris in 1925 and New York in 1927 that brought art, design, and industry together.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04016a -->
在精确主义的发展期间，1925年的巴黎和1927年的纽约都举办了把艺术、设计与工业结合起来的展览。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04016a -->
<!-- USE:U04017 -->
## precisionist /prɪˈsɪʒənɪst/ n.
`对语言、仪式等的精确性极为重视并严格遵行的人，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04017a USE:U04017 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04017a USE:U04017 -->
As a precisionist, she insisted that every term be defined.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04017a -->
作为一个严格求准的人，她坚持要求每个术语都得到定义。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04017a -->
<!-- USE:U04018 -->
## Precisionist /prɪˈsɪʒənɪst/ n.
`与精确主义绘画风格相关的艺术家，可数，艺术用法常首字母大写。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04018a USE:U04018 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"并未正式结社的艺术家","sourceUrls":["https://www.metmuseum.org/essays/precisionism"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04018a USE:U04018 -->
The artists known as the Precisionists shared styles and subjects, but never formally organized a group or issued a manifesto.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04018a -->
被称为精确主义艺术家的这些人，在风格与题材上有共同点，却从未正式结社或发表宣言。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04018a -->
<!-- USE:U04019 -->
## Precisionist /prɪˈsɪʒənɪst/ adj.
`与精确主义绘画风格有关的，艺术用法。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04019a USE:U04019 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"静物也能采用这种风格","sourceUrls":["https://www.metmuseum.org/essays/precisionism"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04019a USE:U04019 -->
Even familiar still-life subjects, such as fruit and flowers, received a fresh treatment in the Precisionist style.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04019a -->
即使水果和花卉这样熟悉的静物题材，也在精确主义风格中得到了新的处理。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04019a -->

<!-- WORD:W041 -->
# restore

**词源**：经古法语来自拉丁语 restaurare“修理、重建、更新”，re- 有回去、再次的意思。以“使回到先前状态”联系恢复、修复、归还与复原；restoration 还有特定政治历史义。

<!-- USE:U04101 -->
## restore /rɪˈstɔr/ v.
`使某种情况、实践或秩序重新存在，及物，如 restore order、restore relations。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04101a USE:U04101 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04101a USE:U04101 -->
The army has recently been brought in to restore order.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04101a -->
军队最近被调来恢复秩序。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04101a -->
<!-- USE:U04102 -->
## restore /rɪˈstɔr/ v.
`使人或事物回到先前较好的状态，及物；restore somebody to health 表示使某人恢复健康，也可谈恢复经济等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04102a USE:U04102 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04102a USE:U04102 -->
We will restore her to health but it may take time.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04102a -->
我们会帮助她恢复健康，但这可能需要时间。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04102a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04102b USE:U04102 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04102b USE:U04102 -->
His country desperately needs Western aid to restore its ailing economy.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04102b -->
他的国家急需西方援助，以恢复陷入困境的经济。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04102b -->
<!-- USE:U04103 -->
## restore /rɪˈstɔr/ v.
`通过修理、清洁、重建或重新绘制等，使旧物恢复或接近原貌。及物，可用于建筑、画作、家具等，具体修复目标由对象决定。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04103a USE:U04103 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"棕榈温室的修缮计划","sourceUrls":["https://www.kew.org/kew-gardens/palm-house-renovation"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04103a USE:U04103 -->
Kew's Palm House renovation plan calls for Sir William Nesfield's original ornamental garden layout to be restored.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04103a -->
邱园棕榈温室修缮计划要求恢复威廉·内斯菲尔德爵士最初设计的装饰花园布局。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04103a -->
<!-- USE:U04104 -->
## restore /rɪˈstɔr/ v.
`把失去、被偷或被拿走的东西归还原主。属于正式用语，及物，常用被动式 be restored to somebody。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04104a USE:U04104 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04104a USE:U04104 -->
The following day their horses and goods were restored to them.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04104a -->
第二天，他们的马匹和货物被归还给了他们。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04104a -->
<!-- USE:U04105 -->
## restore /rɪˈstɔr/ v.
`让人重新获得先前的职位、尊严或权利，及物，可接 to 说明恢复到的位置。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04105a USE:U04105 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"重登王位之后的剧场特许","sourceUrls":["https://www.vam.ac.uk/articles/the-story-of-theatre"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04105a USE:U04105 -->
After Charles II was restored to the throne in 1660, William Davenant and Thomas Killigrew received royal patents to present drama in London.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04105a -->
1660年查理二世复位之后，威廉·达文南特与托马斯·基利格鲁获得了在伦敦演出戏剧的皇家特许。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04105a -->
<!-- USE:U04106 -->
## restore /rɪˈstɔr/ v.
`把某物重新放回原来的位置或状态，及物，强调回到原处。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04106a USE:U04106 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04106a USE:U04106 -->
After reading, he restored the book to its place.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04106a -->
读完后，他把书放回原处。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04106a -->
<!-- USE:U04107 -->
## restore /rɪˈstɔr/ v.
`依据现有材料表现遗迹、灭绝动物等原来可能的样子，及物，指重建其推测原形，不表示所有细节已经确定。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04107a USE:U04107 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04107a USE:U04107 -->
The drawing restores the building to its supposed original form.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04107a -->
这幅图按推测复原了建筑的原始形态。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04107a -->
<!-- USE:U04108 -->
## restore /rɪˈstɔr/ v.
`依据推测补回文本中缺失的词句或部分，及物，用于文本校勘等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04108a USE:U04108 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"伊萨卡与缺损铭文","sourceUrls":["https://deepmind.google/blog/predicting-the-past-with-ithaca/"],"displayContext":"2022年研究介绍；补文为推断，不保证正确。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04108a USE:U04108 -->
Ithaca, an AI system, can help restore missing text in damaged ancient Greek inscriptions by suggesting reconstructions, and can help identify where and when the inscriptions were created.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04108a -->
人工智能系统伊萨卡可提出补文候选，帮助补复受损古希腊铭文中的缺失文字，并辅助判断铭文形成的地点和时间。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04108a -->
<!-- USE:U04109 -->
## restorable /rɪˈstɔrəbəl/ adj.
`能够被恢复、修复或归还的，具体含义取决于对象。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04109a USE:U04109 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04109a USE:U04109 -->
The damaged frame is still restorable.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04109a -->
这个受损的画框仍然可以修复。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04109a -->
<!-- USE:U04110 -->
## restorer /rɪˈstɔrər/ n.
`修复旧建筑、画作、家具等的人，可数，常指以此为职业的修复人员。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04110a USE:U04110 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04110a USE:U04110 -->
She works as an antiques restorer.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04110a -->
她从事古董修复工作。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04110a -->
<!-- USE:U04111 -->
## restoration /ˌrestəˈreɪʃən/ n.
`使关系、制度或情况重新存在的行为、过程，通常不可数，常接 of 说明恢复的对象。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04111a USE:U04111 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04111a USE:U04111 -->
His visit is expected to lead to the restoration of diplomatic relations.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04111a -->
预计他的访问将促成外交关系恢复。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04111a -->
<!-- USE:U04112 -->
## restoration /ˌrestəˈreɪʃən/ n.
`健康、身体功能或其他状况恢复的过程，通常不可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04112a USE:U04112 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04112a USE:U04112 -->
I owe the restoration of my hearing to this remarkable new technique.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04112a -->
我的听力能够恢复，要归功于这项了不起的新技术。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04112a -->
