<!-- USE:U04113 -->
## restoration /ˌrestəˈreɪʃən/ n.
`通过修理、重建使旧物恢复原貌的过程或一次修复，可数或不可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04113a USE:U04113 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"艺术家对修复的取舍","sourceUrls":["https://www.getty.edu/news/artist-dialogues-getty-conservation-lita-albuquerque-malibu-line/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04113a USE:U04113 -->
Helen Pashgian prefers a full restoration of her sculptures even when they are only slightly chipped or scratched.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04113a -->
海伦·帕什吉安即使在自己的雕塑只是轻微磕缺或刮伤时，也更倾向于全面修复。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04113a -->
<!-- USE:U04114 -->
## restoration /ˌrestəˈreɪʃən/ n.
`表现灭绝动物、废墟等推测原形的模型或图画，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04114a USE:U04114 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04114a USE:U04114 -->
The museum displays a restoration of the animal's skeleton.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04114a -->
博物馆展示了一个根据推测复原的动物骨架模型。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04114a -->
<!-- USE:U04115 -->
## restoration /ˌrestəˈreɪʃən/ n.
`重新确立君主统治，或其后的一段时期，属于一般历史政治用法，不限定某一国家。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04115a USE:U04115 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04115a USE:U04115 -->
The historian compares two periods of restoration.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04115a -->
这位历史学家比较了两个王政复辟时期。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04115a -->
<!-- USE:U04116 -->
## Restoration /ˌrestəˈreɪʃən/ n.
`1660 年查理二世在无君主统治时期之后重新成为英格兰、苏格兰和爱尔兰国王的事件。用作专名时首字母大写，通常带 the。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04116a USE:U04116 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"英国复辟与剧场重开","sourceUrls":["https://www.rsc.org.uk/restoration-plays"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04116a USE:U04116 -->
The Restoration period followed the return of the English, Scottish, and Irish monarchies under Charles II in 1660.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04116a -->
英国王政复辟时期始于1660年查理二世治下英格兰、苏格兰和爱尔兰君主制的恢复之后。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04116a -->
<!-- USE:U04117 -->
## Restoration /ˌrestəˈreɪʃən/ adj.
`查理二世复辟后时期的，尤其描述该时期戏剧、文学或建筑的风格，通常放在名词前，如 Restoration comedy。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04117a USE:U04117 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"皇家莎士比亚剧团的演出介绍","sourceUrls":["https://www.rsc.org.uk/restoration-plays"],"displayContext":"这里的we指皇家莎士比亚剧团。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04117a USE:U04117 -->
The Royal Shakespeare Company regularly stages Restoration dramas and comedies, written from around 1660 to 1710.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04117a -->
皇家莎士比亚剧团经常上演约1660年至1710年间写成的复辟时期正剧和喜剧。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04117a -->
<!-- USE:U04118 -->
## restorative /rɪˈstɔrətɪv/ adj.
`使疲惫、虚弱或情绪低落的人恢复健康、力量或愉快的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04118a USE:U04118 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04118a USE:U04118 -->
She opened the door to her bedroom, thinking how restorative a hot bath would feel tonight.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04118a -->
她打开卧室门，想着今晚洗个热水澡该有多解乏。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04118a -->
<!-- USE:U04119 -->
## restorative /rɪˈstɔrətɪv/ n.
`使人恢复健康或力量的药物、食品、饮料等，也可比喻令人恢复精神的事物，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04119a USE:U04119 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04119a USE:U04119 -->
Seven days off could be a wonderful restorative.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04119a -->
休息七天可能是恢复精神的好办法。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04119a -->
<!-- USE:U04120 -->
## restoratively /rɪˈstɔrətɪvli/ adv.
`以帮助恢复健康或力量的方式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04120a USE:U04120 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04120a USE:U04120 -->
The quiet walk acted restoratively after the long journey.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04120a -->
长途旅行后，那次安静的散步使人恢复了精神。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04120a -->

<!-- WORD:W042 -->
# retain

**词源**：经法语来自拉丁语 retinere“留住、保持”，re- 表示回、住，tenere 表示持有。以“继续握住、不放掉”理解保留、记住与固定；预付费用留住专业服务也由此相联。

<!-- USE:U04201 -->
## retain /rɪˈteɪn/ v.
`继续拥有、保持、实行或承认某物，不丢弃、废止或改变，及物，属于正式用语。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04201a USE:U04201 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"修缮时保留旧结构","sourceUrls":["https://www.kew.org/kew-gardens/palm-house-renovation"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04201a USE:U04201 -->
Kew plans to conserve, retain, and reuse as much of the existing structure as possible during the Palm House renovation.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04201a -->
邱园计划在棕榈温室修缮期间，尽可能养护、保留并重新利用现有结构。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04201a -->
<!-- USE:U04202 -->
## retain /rɪˈteɪn/ v.
`把经历、知识等保存在记忆中，及物。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04202a USE:U04202 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"记忆中的保存","sourceUrls":["https://openstax.org/books/psychology-2e/pages/8-1-how-memory-functions"],"displayContext":"OpenStax《Psychology 2e》缩编／节录，CC BY-NC-SA 4.0"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04202a USE:U04202 -->
Once information has been encoded, we must retain it; storage is the creation of a lasting record of that information.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04202a -->
信息经过编码后，还必须将它保持在记忆中；存储就是形成这份信息的持久记录。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04202a -->
<!-- USE:U04203 -->
## retain /rɪˈteɪn/ v.
`把某物保持在一定位置，使其不移开或流失，及物，可用于具体物体、液体等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04203a USE:U04203 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04203a USE:U04203 -->
The clip retains the lid in position.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04203a -->
这个夹子把盖子固定在位。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04203a -->
<!-- USE:U04204 -->
## retain /rɪˈteɪn/ v.
`预先付费以确保某人在需要时提供服务，及物，尤用于聘请律师等专业人员。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04204a USE:U04204 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04204a USE:U04204 -->
He decided to retain him for the trial.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04204a -->
他决定预付费用，请这位律师在庭审中代表他。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04204a -->
<!-- USE:U04205 -->
## retainable /rɪˈteɪnəbəl/ adj.
`能够被保留、留住的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04205a USE:U04205 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04205a USE:U04205 -->
Only some of the records are retainable under the agreement.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04205a -->
根据协议，只有部分记录可以保留。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04205a -->
<!-- USE:U04206 -->
## retainability /rɪˌteɪnəˈbɪləti/ n.
`能够被保留、留住的性质。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04206a USE:U04206 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04206a USE:U04206 -->
The review considers the retainability of the evidence.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04206a -->
审查考虑了这些证据能否保留。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04206a -->
<!-- USE:U04207 -->
## retainment /rɪˈteɪnmənt/ n.
`保留、继续持有的行为或状态。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04207a USE:U04207 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04207a USE:U04207 -->
The clause permits the retainment of a copy.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04207a -->
这项条款允许保留一份副本。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04207a -->
<!-- USE:U04208 -->
## retainer /rɪˈteɪnər/ n.
`起保留、固定作用的人或事物，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04208a USE:U04208 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04208a USE:U04208 -->
The metal retainer holds the ring in its groove.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04208a -->
金属固定件把环保持在凹槽中。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04208a -->
<!-- USE:U04209 -->
## retainer /rɪˈteɪnər/ n.
`为确保某人在需要时提供服务而预付的费用，可数，尤用于律师等，也可定期支付。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04209a USE:U04209 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04209a USE:U04209 -->
Liz was being paid a regular monthly retainer.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04209a -->
利兹每月定期领取一笔预付服务费。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04209a -->
<!-- USE:U04210 -->
## retainer /rɪˈteɪnər/ n.
`历史上依附于有地位者的随从、家臣，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04210a USE:U04210 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04210a USE:U04210 -->
The nobleman's retainer carried his messages.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04210a -->
这位贵族的家臣替他传递信息。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04210a -->
<!-- USE:U04211 -->
## old retainer /ˌoʊld rɪˈteɪnər/ n.
`忠实的老朋友或服务多年的仆人，常说 old retainer，带诙谐意味。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04211a USE:U04211 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04211a USE:U04211 -->
He jokingly called his oldest assistant the old retainer.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04211a -->
他开玩笑地把自己资历最老的助手称为“老家臣”。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04211a -->
<!-- USE:U04212 -->
## retainer /rɪˈteɪnər/ n.
`暂不居住期间为保留住处而支付的较低租金，属于英式用法，与聘请律师的服务费区分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04212a USE:U04212 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"暑期离校时的保房费用","sourceUrls":["https://www.stmarys.ac.uk/student-services/docs/guide-to-off-campus-accommodation.pdf"],"displayContext":"2016年英国大学指南；不代表当前各地租赁规则。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04212a -->
St Mary's University's 2016 housing guide offered advice to students leaving for the summer.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04212a_1 USE:U04212 -->
It suggested asking whether a retainer would let them keep their room at reduced rent while they were away.
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04212a_2 -->
The guide said that only a few local landlords agreed; most charged the full rent.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04212a -->
圣玛丽大学2016年的住宿指南向暑期离校的学生提出建议：问问能否支付保房费用，在不入住期间以较低租金保留房间。指南说，当地只有少数房东同意，多数仍收全额房租。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04212a -->
<!-- USE:U04213 -->
## retainer /rɪˈteɪnər/ n.
`牙科学上固定在牙齿上、帮助牙桥保持位置的结构，即固位体。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04213a USE:U04213 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04213a USE:U04213 -->
The dental retainer secures the bridge to the supporting tooth.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04213a -->
牙桥固位体把牙桥固定在支撑牙上。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04213a -->
<!-- USE:U04214 -->
## retention /rɪˈtenʃən/ n.
`保留的行为、实例或被保留的状态，通常不可数，常接 of，属于正式用语。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04214a USE:U04214 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04214a USE:U04214 -->
They supported the retention of a strong central government.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04214a -->
他们支持保留强有力的中央政府。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04214a -->
<!-- USE:U04215 -->
## retention /rɪˈtenʃən/ n.
`把经历或学过的东西保存在记忆中的能力。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04215a USE:U04215 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"短时记忆的干扰","sourceUrls":["https://openstax.org/books/psychology-2e/pages/8-1-how-memory-functions"],"displayContext":"OpenStax《Psychology 2e》缩编／节录，CC BY-NC-SA 4.0"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04215a USE:U04215 -->
Memory trace decay and interference both affect short-term memory retention.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04215a -->
记忆痕迹衰退和干扰都会影响短时记忆保持。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04215a -->
<!-- USE:U04216 -->
## retention /rɪˈtenʃən/ n.
`医学上尿液或其他分泌物未能排出的潴留状态。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04216a USE:U04216 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"急性与慢性尿潴留","sourceUrls":["https://www.niddk.nih.gov/health-information/urologic-diseases/urinary-retention/definition-facts"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04216a USE:U04216 -->
Urinary retention may develop suddenly or gradually: some people cannot urinate at all, while others cannot empty their bladder completely.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04216a -->
尿潴留可能突然发生，也可能逐渐发展：有的人完全无法排尿，另一些人则无法完全排空膀胱。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04216a -->
<!-- USE:U04217 -->
## retentive /rɪˈtentɪv/ adj.
`倾向于留住水分等物质的，常接 of 说明所留住的东西。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04217a USE:U04217 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"粉土的园艺特性","sourceUrls":["https://www.rhs.org.uk/soil-composts-mulches/soil-types"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04217a USE:U04217 -->
Silt soils are fertile and light, but moisture-retentive and easily compacted.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04217a -->
粉土肥沃、质轻，却容易保留水分，也容易被压实。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04217a -->
<!-- USE:U04218 -->
## retentive /rɪˈtentɪv/ adj.
`人或记忆善于记住、不会轻易遗忘的，常说 a retentive memory。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04218a USE:U04218 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04218a USE:U04218 -->
Luke had an amazingly retentive memory.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04218a -->
卢克的记忆力好得惊人。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04218a -->
<!-- USE:U04219 -->
## retentive /rɪˈtentɪv/ adj.
`外科中用来把某物保持在位的，如固定性的绷带、结扎物等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04219a USE:U04219 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04219a USE:U04219 -->
The surgeon applied a retentive bandage to keep the dressing in place.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04219a -->
外科医生用固位绷带把敷料固定在位。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04219a -->
<!-- USE:U04220 -->
## retentively /rɪˈtentɪvli/ adv.
`以能够保留、记住或固定的方式，具体意义取决于对象。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04220a USE:U04220 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04220a USE:U04220 -->
She read retentively, remembering even minor details.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04220a -->
她阅读时很能记住内容，连细节都记住了。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04220a -->
<!-- USE:U04221 -->
## retentiveness /rɪˈtentɪvnəs/ n.
`保水、记忆保持或固定能力的性质，具体意义取决于所谈对象。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04221a USE:U04221 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04221a USE:U04221 -->
The test compares the retentiveness of the two soils.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04221a -->
这个试验比较两种土壤的保水能力。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04221a -->

<!-- WORD:W043 -->
# reverse

**词源**：经法语来自拉丁语表示“转回”的形式。由方向转回，联想到逆序、相反情况与物体背面；reversal 表示逆转，reversible 表示能够逆转或两面使用。

<!-- USE:U04301 -->
## reverse /rɪˈvɝs/ v.
`把决定、政策、趋势、性质或效果改向相反方向，及物。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04301a USE:U04301 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04301a USE:U04301 -->
They have made it clear they will not reverse the decision to increase prices.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04301a -->
他们已经明确表示，不会改变涨价的决定。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04301a -->
<!-- USE:U04302 -->
## reverse /rɪˈvɝs/ v.
`撤销或废止法令、裁决或行为的效力，及物，与改变排列方向区分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04302a USE:U04302 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"判决的撤销与例外","sourceUrls":["https://www.law.cornell.edu/supremecourt/text/349/294"],"displayContext":"1955年Brown II判决中的裁判结果，经短幅缩编。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04302a USE:U04302 -->
In Brown II (1955), the U.S. Supreme Court reversed the lower-court judgments, except for the Delaware judgment, which it upheld.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04302a -->
在1955年的布朗案第二次判决中，美国最高法院撤销了下级法院的判决，但特拉华州案件的判决除外，该判决得到维持。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04302a -->
<!-- USE:U04303 -->
## reverse /rɪˈvɝs/ v.
`把一组事物按相反顺序安排，使开头变成结尾，及物。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04303a USE:U04303 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04303a USE:U04303 -->
In the pair "The boy opened the door" and "The door was opened by the boy", the order of agent and patient is reversed.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04303a -->
在“The boy opened the door”和“The door was opened by the boy”这一组句子中，施事和受事的先后顺序被倒置。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04303a -->
<!-- USE:U04304 -->
## reverse /rɪˈvɝs/ v.
`让两者交换位置或职能，各取得原来属于对方的位置或作用，及物。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04304a USE:U04304 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04304a USE:U04304 -->
He reversed the position of the two stamps.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04304a -->
他把两枚邮票的位置对调了。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04304a -->
<!-- USE:U04305 -->
## reverse /rɪˈvɝs/ v.
`把某物转成另一朝向、上下翻转或里外翻转，及物。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04305a USE:U04305 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04305a USE:U04305 -->
She reversed the cloth to show its other side.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04305a -->
她把布翻转过来，展示另一面。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04305a -->
<!-- USE:U04306 -->
## reverse /rɪˈvɝs/ v.
`车辆向后行驶，或把车辆向后开，可及物或不及物，主要为英式用法；美式英语通常说 back up。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04306a USE:U04306 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04306a USE:U04306 -->
Another car reversed out of the drive.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04306a -->
另一辆车从车道里倒了出来。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04306a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04306b USE:U04306 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04306b USE:U04306 -->
He reversed his car straight at the policeman.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04306b -->
他倒车，径直朝那名警员撞去。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04306b -->
<!-- USE:U04307 -->
## reverse /rɪˈvɝs/ v.
`使发动机等沿相反方向工作，及物。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04307a USE:U04307 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04307a USE:U04307 -->
The operator reversed the engine.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04307a -->
操作员让发动机反向运转。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04307a -->
<!-- USE:U04308 -->
## reverse /rɪˈvɝs/ v.
`舞者沿原来相反的方向旋转，不及物，尤可用于华尔兹。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04308a USE:U04308 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04308a USE:U04308 -->
The dancers reversed and began turning the other way.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04308a -->
舞者反向，开始向另一方向旋转。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04308a -->
<!-- USE:U04309 -->
## reverse /rɪˈvɝs/ n.
`车辆用于后退的档位、装置或后退运动状态，常用 in reverse、into reverse。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04309a USE:U04309 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04309a USE:U04309 -->
He lurched the car in reverse along the ruts to the access road.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04309a -->
他挂着倒档，让汽车沿着车辙颠簸着退到入口道路上。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04309a -->
<!-- USE:U04310 -->
## reverse /rɪˈvɝs/ adj.
`被摆成或转成相反方向、位置的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04310a USE:U04310 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04310a USE:U04310 -->
The two panels face in reverse directions.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04310a -->
这两块面板朝向相反的方向。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04310a -->
<!-- USE:U04311 -->
## reverse /rɪˈvɝs/ adj.
`性质、效果或排列次序相反的，如 reverse effect、reverse order。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04311a USE:U04311 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04311a USE:U04311 -->
The wrong attitude will have exactly the reverse effect.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04311a -->
错误的态度会产生完全相反的效果。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04311a -->
<!-- USE:U04312 -->
## reverse /rɪˈvɝs/ n.
`与所述情况完全相反的事物或状态，常说 the reverse of、quite the reverse。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04312a USE:U04312 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04312a USE:U04312 -->
That statement is the reverse of the truth.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04312a -->
那种说法与事实恰好相反。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04312a -->
<!-- USE:U04313 -->
## reverse /rɪˈvɝs/ n.
`与惯常做法或方式相反的情况。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04313a USE:U04313 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04313a USE:U04313 -->
Instead of following the usual procedure, they did the reverse.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04313a -->
他们没有遵循惯常程序，而是采取了反向做法。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04313a -->
<!-- USE:U04314 -->
## reverse /rɪˈvɝs/ n.
`一次严重失利、厄运或挫折，尤可指战败，可数，属于正式用语。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04314a USE:U04314 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04314a USE:U04314 -->
It's clear that the party of the former Prime Minister has suffered a major reverse.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04314a -->
显然，这位前首相所属的政党遭受了重大挫败。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04314a -->
<!-- USE:U04315 -->
## reverse /rɪˈvɝs/ n.
`扁平双面物体的另一面或较次要的一面，常说 the reverse of、the reverse side。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04315a USE:U04315 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04315a USE:U04315 -->
Write your address on the reverse of the cheque.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04315a -->
请把地址写在支票背面。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04315a -->
<!-- USE:U04316 -->
## reverse /rɪˈvɝs/ n.
`钱币或奖章上带次要图案的一面，也可指该面图案，与带主要设计的正面相对。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04316a USE:U04316 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"一枚德纳里乌斯银币的背面","sourceUrls":["https://www.royalmintmuseum.org.uk/collection/coins/roman-denarius/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04316a USE:U04316 -->
The reverse design of a Roman denarius was once thought to show coin-making tools; some scholars now suggest a connection with Vulcan instead.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04316a -->
一枚罗马德纳里乌斯银币的背面图案，曾被认为画的是铸币工具；如今一些学者提出，它可能与伏尔甘有关。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04316a -->
<!-- USE:U04317 -->
## reverse /rɪˈvɝs/ n.
`一张书叶的背面，打开书时通常位于左侧的页。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04317a USE:U04317 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04317a USE:U04317 -->
The note is written on the reverse of the leaf.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04317a -->
这条批注写在书叶背面。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04317a -->
<!-- USE:U04318 -->
## reverse /rɪˈvɝs/ n.
`在 in reverse、into reverse 中表示以相反的方向、顺序或进程运行，可用于倒序叙事、过程逆转等，不限于倒车。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04318a USE:U04318 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04318a USE:U04318 -->
Amis tells the story in reverse, from the moment the man dies.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04318a -->
埃米斯从那个男人死亡的时刻开始，倒着讲述这个故事。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04318a -->
<!-- USE:U04319 -->
## reverse the charges /rɪˈvɝs ðə ˈtʃɑrdʒɪz/ v.
`打电话时让接听方支付通话费用，属于英式用法；美式英语说 call collect。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04319a USE:U04319 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04319a USE:U04319 -->
The caller asked to reverse the charges.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04319a -->
来电者要求由接听方付通话费。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04319a -->
<!-- USE:U04320 -->
## reverse arms /rɪˈvɝs ɑrmz/ v.
`在致哀仪式中把步枪枪托向上、枪口向下持握，也可用作要求采取这一姿势的口令。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04320a USE:U04320 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"致哀的倒持枪姿势","sourceUrls":["https://amosa.org.au/stories-and-events/militaria/catafalque-party"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04320a USE:U04320 -->
A catafalque guard stands with heads bowed and arms reversed, facing outward as a sign of respect for the fallen.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04320a -->
灵柩仪仗卫队垂首倒持武器、面朝外肃立，以表示对逝者的敬意。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04320a -->
<!-- USE:U04321 -->
## reversal /rɪˈvɝsəl/ n.
`过程、政策或趋势完全改变方向的逆转，可数，常接 of。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04321a USE:U04321 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04321a USE:U04321 -->
The Financial Times says the move represents a complete reversal of previous U.S. policy.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04321a -->
《金融时报》称，这一举措意味着美国先前政策的彻底逆转。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04321a -->
<!-- USE:U04322 -->
## reversal /rɪˈvɝsəl/ n.
`两人或群体交换原有位置、职能，可数，常说 a role reversal、a reversal of roles。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04322a USE:U04322 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04322a USE:U04322 -->
When children end up taking care of their parents, it is a strange role reversal indeed.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04322a -->
当孩子最终反过来照顾父母时，这的确是一种奇妙的角色互换。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04322a -->
<!-- USE:U04323 -->
## reversal /rɪˈvɝsəl/ n.
`一次失败或失利，通常涉及金钱损失，属于正式用语，常用复数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04323a USE:U04323 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04323a USE:U04323 -->
They teach managers to accept reversals as a fact of business life.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04323a -->
他们教管理者接受挫折是商业生活中的一部分。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04323a -->
<!-- USE:U04324 -->
## reversible /rɪˈvɝsəbəl/ adj.
`作用能够被逆转、从而恢复原来状态的。是否可逆取决于具体对象和条件，个别研究中的可逆情况不意味着所有病变或过程都可逆。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04324a USE:U04324 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04324a USE:U04324 -->
The attachment is reversible: the parts can be separated again without damage.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04324a -->
这种连接是可逆的：零件可以再次分离，而且不会损坏。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04324a -->
<!-- USE:U04325 -->
## reversible /rɪˈvɝsəbəl/ adj.
`衣物或材料两面都能作为外面穿着、展示的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04325a USE:U04325 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04325a USE:U04325 -->
He wore a reversible waistcoat.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04325a -->
他穿了一件双面可穿的马甲。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04325a -->
<!-- USE:U04326 -->
## reversibility /rɪˌvɝsəˈbɪləti/ n.
`能够逆转或两面使用的性质。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04326a USE:U04326 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04326a USE:U04326 -->
The experiment tests the reversibility of the change.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04326a -->
这个实验测试这种变化的可逆性。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04326a -->
<!-- USE:U04327 -->
## reversibly /rɪˈvɝsəbli/ adv.
`以能够逆转、恢复原状的方式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04327a USE:U04327 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04327a USE:U04327 -->
The molecule binds reversibly to the surface.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04327a -->
这种分子以可逆方式与表面结合。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04327a -->
<!-- USE:U04328 -->
## reversely /rɪˈvɝsli/ adv.
`以相反的方向、顺序或方式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04328a USE:U04328 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04328a USE:U04328 -->
The second diagram is reversely arranged.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04328a -->
第二幅图按相反方向排列。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04328a -->
<!-- USE:U04329 -->
## reverser /rɪˈvɝsər/ n.
`执行翻转、反向运转等动作的人或装置，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04329a USE:U04329 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04329a USE:U04329 -->
The mechanism includes a reverser for changing the drive direction.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04329a -->
这个机构包含一个用于改变传动方向的反转装置。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04329a -->

<!-- WORD:W044 -->
# sediment

**词源**：来自拉丁语 sedimentum“沉下、沉降”，联系到 sedere“坐下、落定”。可借“颗粒落定”理解液体沉渣与地质沉积，再联系到思想长期积淀的比喻。

<!-- USE:U04401 -->
## sediment /ˈsedəmənt/ n.
`沉到液体底部的固体物质、渣滓，可数或不可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04401a USE:U04401 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04401a USE:U04401 -->
A fine sediment settled at the bottom of the glass.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04401a -->
一层细小沉渣落在杯底。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04401a -->
<!-- USE:U04402 -->
## sediment /ˈsedəmənt/ n.
`地质上由水、风或冰等搬运并留下的泥沙、岩屑等物质，可在地表、海底等处沉积，长期也可能固结成岩石。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04402a USE:U04402 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"拆坝后的泥沙搬运","sourceUrls":["https://www.usgs.gov/news/state-news-release/moving-mountains-elwha-river-still-changing-five-years-after-worlds-largest"],"displayContext":"2018年报道，回顾2011年起拆坝后五年的变化。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04402a USE:U04402 -->
After two dams were removed from the Elwha River, released sediment moved downstream, reshaping the river and enlarging its delta.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04402a -->
埃尔瓦河上的两座水坝拆除后，释放出的沉积物向下游移动，重塑了河道，并扩大了三角洲。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04402a -->
<!-- USE:U04403 -->
## sedimented /ˈsedəmentɪd/ adj.
`地质上作为沉积物沉降、留下来的，是 sediment 的分词形式作形容词。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04403a USE:U04403 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04403a USE:U04403 -->
The core contains layers of sedimented material.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04403a -->
这根岩芯包含多层沉积下来的物质。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04403a -->
<!-- USE:U04404 -->
## sedimented /ˈsedəmentɪd/ adj.
`根深蒂固、经过长时间形成并确立的，是沉积的比喻用法，可描述思想、态度等。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04404a USE:U04404 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04404a USE:U04404 -->
The essay examines sedimented radicalism.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04404a -->
这篇文章探讨根深蒂固的激进主义。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04404a -->
<!-- USE:U04405 -->
## sedimented /ˈsedəmentɪd/ adj.
`啤酒中带有沉淀物的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04405a USE:U04405 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04405a USE:U04405 -->
The sedimented beer must be poured carefully.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04405a -->
这种带沉淀的啤酒需要小心倒出。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04405a -->
<!-- USE:U04406 -->
## sedimentary /ˌsedəˈmentəri/ adj.
`由沉积物形成或与沉积有关的，属于地质用语；sedimentary rocks 指沉积岩。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04406a USE:U04406 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04406a USE:U04406 -->
The cliff exposes sedimentary rock.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04406a -->
悬崖露出了沉积岩。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04406a -->
<!-- USE:U04407 -->
## sedimentation /ˌsedəmənˈteɪʃən/ n.
`固体颗粒沉降、沉积物逐渐形成的过程。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04407a USE:U04407 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04407a USE:U04407 -->
The tank allows sedimentation before the water is filtered.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04407a -->
水箱让水在过滤前先进行沉淀。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04407a -->

<!-- WORD:W045 -->
# shift

**词源**：来自古英语表示分配、安排的词，后来有交换、转移、改变的意思。另一个历史线索是“设法应付、谋划”，由此理解 shiftless 的缺乏进取与 shifty 的躲闪诡诈；现代词义要按对象和语域分别掌握。

<!-- USE:U04501 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ v.
`使某物或身体移位，或自身改变位置，常指轻微移动。可及物或不及物，常接方向、位置说明。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04501a USE:U04501 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"弹奏时移动手位","sourceUrls":["https://hub.yamaha.com/keyboards/k-how-to/how-to-use-keyboard-pedals/"],"displayContext":"这里是该钢琴练习中的衔接指导。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04501a USE:U04501 -->
When you need to shift your hand position, use the sustain pedal to connect the notes smoothly.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04501a -->
需要移动手位时，可以用延音踏板把音符平顺地衔接起来。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04501a -->
<!-- USE:U04502 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ v.
`意见、情势、政策或重点发生改变，或使其改变，可及物或不及物。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04502a USE:U04502 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04502a USE:U04502 -->
Attitudes to mental illness have shifted in recent years.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04502a -->
近年来，人们对精神疾病的态度发生了变化。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04502a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04502b USE:U04502 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04502b USE:U04502 -->
The emphasis should be shifted more towards Parliament.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04502b -->
重点应当更多转向议会。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04502b -->
<!-- USE:U04503 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ n.
`一次方向、政策或情况的改变，可数，常说 a shift in something。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04503a USE:U04503 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"美国观察者对万隆会议的看法","sourceUrls":["https://history.state.gov/milestones/1953-1960/bandung-conf"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04503a USE:U04503 -->
U.S. observers feared that the Bandung Conference signalled a leftward shift in the outlook of newly independent African and Asian nations.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04503a -->
美国观察者担心，万隆会议意味着亚非新独立国家的思想倾向正在向左转变。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04503a -->
<!-- USE:U04504 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ v.
`把本属于自己的责任、过错不公平地转给别人，含批评意味；shift responsibility/blame to/onto somebody 表示把责任或过错推到某人身上。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04504a USE:U04504 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04504a USE:U04504 -->
It was a vain attempt to shift the responsibility for the murder to somebody else.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04504a -->
那是一次徒劳的尝试，想把谋杀责任推给别人。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04504a -->
<!-- USE:U04505 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ v.
`卖出商品，尤指迅速、大量卖出或清掉难卖的库存。及物，英式口语用法，某些语境也可含不正当销售的意味。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04505a USE:U04505 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04505a USE:U04505 -->
Some suppliers were selling at a loss to shift stock.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04505a -->
一些供货商亏本出售，以清掉库存。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04505a -->
<!-- USE:U04506 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ v.
`把车辆调到另一档位，属于北美用法；可及物说 shift gears，也可不及物，英式英语通常说 change gear。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04506a USE:U04506 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"手动变速器的换档","sourceUrls":["https://www.toyota.com/car-tips/types-of-transmissions/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04506a USE:U04506 -->
To shift gears in a manual transmission, depress the clutch pedal, move the gear stick to the required gear, and then release the clutch gradually.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04506a -->
手动变速器换档时，踩下离合器踏板，把变速杆移到所需档位，再逐渐松开离合器。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04506a -->
<!-- USE:U04507 -->
## shift down /ʃɪft daʊn/ v.
`把车辆换到较低档位，美式短语动词；英式英语说 change down。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04507a USE:U04507 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04507a USE:U04507 -->
She shifted down before the steep climb.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04507a -->
陡坡开始前，她降了档。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04507a -->
<!-- USE:U04508 -->
## shift up /ʃɪft ʌp/ v.
`把车辆换到较高档位，美式短语动词；英式英语说 change up。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04508a USE:U04508 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04508a USE:U04508 -->
He shifted up once the engine speed increased.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04508a -->
发动机转速升高后，他升了档。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04508a -->
<!-- USE:U04509 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ n.
`一组工作人员工作、然后由下一组接替的时段，可数；an eight-hour shift 指八小时的班次，work shifts 指轮班工作。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04509a USE:U04509 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04509a USE:U04509 -->
She completed an eight-hour shift.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04509a -->
她完成了一个八小时班次。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04509a -->
<!-- USE:U04510 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ n.
`在同一轮值时段一起工作的那组人，可数，如 the night shift 也可指夜班人员。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04510a USE:U04510 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04510a USE:U04510 -->
The night shift took over at ten.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04510a -->
夜班人员在十点接班。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04510a -->
<!-- USE:U04511 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ v.
`用力除去、弄掉某物，及物，属于英式用法，如去掉污渍。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04511a USE:U04511 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04511a USE:U04511 -->
Washing won't shift the stains.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04511a -->
清洗也去不掉这些污渍。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04511a -->
<!-- USE:U04512 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ v.
`赶紧行动、加快速度，不及物，属于英式俚语。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04512a USE:U04512 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04512a USE:U04512 -->
We'll have to shift!
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04512a -->
我们得赶快了！
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04512a -->
<!-- USE:U04513 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ v.
`快速或大量吃下食物、喝下饮料，及物，属于英式俚语。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04513a USE:U04513 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04513a USE:U04513 -->
The hungry workers shifted a huge meal.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04513a -->
饥饿的工人们狼吞虎咽地吃了一大顿。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04513a -->
<!-- USE:U04514 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ v.
`尽可能筹划、设法把事情做下去，不及物。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04514a USE:U04514 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04514a USE:U04514 -->
They learned to shift as best they could.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04514a -->
他们学会了尽可能设法应付。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04514a -->
<!-- USE:U04515 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ v.
`装载的货物因摇晃而离开原位，不及物。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04515a USE:U04515 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04515a USE:U04515 -->
The cargo shifted during the storm.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04515a -->
货物在风暴中移了位。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04515a -->
<!-- USE:U04516 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ v.
`说话或行动间接、逃避明确表态，不及物，属于古义。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04516a USE:U04516 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04516a USE:U04516 -->
In the old account, the witness shifted instead of answering directly.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04516a -->
在这份旧记述中，证人推托躲闪，没有直接回答。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04516a -->
<!-- USE:U04517 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ n.
`一次位置的转移、移位，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04517a USE:U04517 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04517a USE:U04517 -->
A slight shift moved the frame out of alignment.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04517a -->
一次轻微移位使框架错了位。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04517a -->
<!-- USE:U04518 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ n.
`以一物替代另一物，或各方轮流更替，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04518a USE:U04518 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04518a USE:U04518 -->
The schedule allows a regular shift of duties between the teams.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04518a -->
这张日程表安排各队定期轮换职责。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04518a -->
<!-- USE:U04519 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ n.
`为应付情势想出的手段、策略或临时办法，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04519a USE:U04519 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04519a USE:U04519 -->
They tried every shift to keep the workshop open.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04519a -->
他们想尽各种权宜办法，让工场继续开下去。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04519a -->
<!-- USE:U04520 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ n.
`用来躲避、欺骗或推卸的手段，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04520a USE:U04520 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04520a USE:U04520 -->
His shifts failed to conceal the missing money.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04520a -->
他的推托伎俩没能掩盖钱款短缺。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04520a -->
<!-- USE:U04521 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ n.
`不收腰的女式直筒连衣裙，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04521a USE:U04521 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"玛丽·奎恩特与直筒裙","sourceUrls":["https://www.vam.ac.uk/articles/introducing-mary-quant"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04521a USE:U04521 -->
Short skirts and shift dresses became Mary Quant’s trademark and were popularised by the model Twiggy.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04521a -->
短裙和直筒连衣裙成为玛丽·奎恩特的标志性设计，并由模特崔姬推广开来。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04521a -->
<!-- USE:U04522 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ n.
`宽松的贴身内衣，可数，属于古义。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04522a USE:U04522 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04522a USE:U04522 -->
The inventory lists a linen shift worn beneath the dress.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04522a -->
清单列有一件穿在裙子里面的亚麻旧式内衣。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04522a -->
<!-- USE:U04523 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ n.
`光谱线相对于原来位置的偏移，用于物理、天文学等语境。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04523a USE:U04523 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"吸收线的位置改变","sourceUrls":["https://science.nasa.gov/mission/hubble/science/science-behind-the-discoveries/hubble-cosmological-redshift/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04523a USE:U04523 -->
The dark lines in an absorption spectrum show a shift from their normal positions as the universe expands.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04523a -->
随着宇宙膨胀，吸收光谱中的暗线相对于正常位置出现位移。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04523a -->
<!-- USE:U04524 -->
## sound shift /ˈsaʊnd ʃɪft/ n.
`语言发展中发音发生的系统性变化，属于语言学用法，也可简称 shift。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04524a USE:U04524 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"英语元音大推移","sourceUrls":["https://chaucer.fas.harvard.edu/pages/great-vowel-shift"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04524a USE:U04524 -->
The Great Vowel Shift changed long, stressed vowels; unstressed endings such as -ly were not affected in the same way.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04524a -->
英语元音大推移改变了重读长元音；-ly等非重读词尾没有以同样方式受到影响。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04524a -->
<!-- USE:U04525 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ n.
`键盘上用来切换大小写或其他输入状态的键，可数，键名常写 Shift。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04525a USE:U04525 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04525a USE:U04525 -->
Hold the Shift key while typing the letter.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04525a -->
输入字母时按住Shift键。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04525a -->
<!-- USE:U04526 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ n.
`桥牌叫牌时改叫另一花色，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04526a USE:U04526 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04526a USE:U04526 -->
The shift to hearts changed the direction of the bidding.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04526a -->
改叫红心改变了叫牌的走向。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04526a -->
<!-- USE:U04527 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ n.
`桥牌出牌时改出另一花色，可数，属于美式用法。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04527a USE:U04527 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04527a USE:U04527 -->
The defender's shift to diamonds forced a new response.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04527a -->
防守者改出方块，迫使对方作出新的应对。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04527a -->
<!-- USE:U04528 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ n.
`砌砖时让相邻砖层错开排列，使竖向端缝不重合，即错缝。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04528a USE:U04528 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04528a USE:U04528 -->
The shift between brick courses keeps the vertical joints from lining up.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04528a -->
相邻砖层的错缝排列，使竖向接缝不在同一条线上。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04528a -->
<!-- USE:U04529 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ n.
`机动车中操纵档位的杆，可数，属于北美用法。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04529a USE:U04529 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"变速杆上的标记","sourceUrls":["https://www.toyota.com/car-tips/types-of-transmissions/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04529a USE:U04529 -->
The gear shift has an R for reverse and numbers corresponding to the forward gears.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04529a -->
变速杆上有表示倒档的R，以及对应各前进档的数字。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04529a -->
<!-- USE:U04530 -->
## shift /ʃɪft/ n.
`执行车辆换档的装置或机构，可数，属于北美用法。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04530a USE:U04530 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04530a USE:U04530 -->
The mechanic checked the automatic shift.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04530a -->
机械师检查了自动换档机构。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04530a -->
<!-- USE:U04531 -->
## make shift /meɪk ʃɪft/ v.
`想办法勉强应付、把事情做下去，可接 without something，说明缺少某物也设法对付。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04531a USE:U04531 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04531a USE:U04531 -->
They made shift without it.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04531a -->
没有它，他们也设法应付了过去。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04531a -->
<!-- USE:U04532 -->
## shift for oneself /ʃɪft fər wʌnˈself/ v.
`依靠自己的努力谋生或应付处境。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04532a USE:U04532 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04532a USE:U04532 -->
He had to shift for himself after leaving home.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04532a -->
离家后，他不得不靠自己谋生。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04532a -->
<!-- USE:U04533 -->
## shift one's ground /ʃɪft wʌnz ɡraʊnd/ v.
`在争论等过程中改换立场。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04533a USE:U04533 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04533a USE:U04533 -->
She shifted her ground when the evidence changed.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04533a -->
证据改变后，她改换了争论立场。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04533a -->
<!-- USE:U04534 -->
## shift off /ʃɪft ɔf/ v.
`把责任等推给另一人，自己摆脱它，是短语动词。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04534a USE:U04534 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04534a USE:U04534 -->
He tried to shift off the responsibility onto his assistant.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04534a -->
他试图把责任推给助手。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04534a -->
<!-- USE:U04535 -->
## shiftable /ˈʃɪftəbəl/ adj.
`能够被移动、转移或改变的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04535a USE:U04535 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04535a USE:U04535 -->
The partitions are shiftable.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04535a -->
这些隔断可以移动。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04535a -->
<!-- USE:U04536 -->
## shifter /ˈʃɪftər/ n.
`执行移动、转移或换档的人或装置，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04536a USE:U04536 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"自动变速器的操纵装置","sourceUrls":["https://www.toyota.com/car-tips/types-of-transmissions/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04536a USE:U04536 -->
The shifter for an automatic transmission is simpler than a manual gear stick.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04536a -->
自动变速器的换档操纵装置比手动变速杆更简单。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04536a -->
<!-- USE:U04537 -->
## shifting /ˈʃɪftɪŋ/ adj.
`组成部分不断移动、彼此位置不断改变的，通常放在名词前。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04537a USE:U04537 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04537a USE:U04537 -->
The Croatian town of Ilok is a classic case of shifting populations.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04537a -->
克罗地亚的伊洛克镇是人口不断变动的一个典型例子。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04537a -->
<!-- USE:U04538 -->
## shiftless /ˈʃɪftləs/ adj.
`懒散、缺少进取心或应付事务能力的，含批评意味，也可指出效率低。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04538a USE:U04538 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04538a USE:U04538 -->
The novel follows the lives of two shiftless, violent ex-convicts in 1950s America.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04538a -->
这部小说讲述二十世纪五十年代美国两个懒散、暴力的出狱者的生活。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04538a -->
<!-- USE:U04539 -->
## shiftlessly /ˈʃɪftləsli/ adv.
`懒散、缺乏应对能力地。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04539a USE:U04539 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04539a USE:U04539 -->
He drifted shiftlessly from one unfinished task to another.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04539a -->
他懒散地从一项没做完的任务游荡到另一项。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04539a -->
<!-- USE:U04540 -->
## shiftlessness /ˈʃɪftləsnəs/ n.
`懒散、缺乏进取或办事能力的性质。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04540a USE:U04540 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04540a USE:U04540 -->
The account attributes the failure to shiftlessness.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04540a -->
这段记述把失败归因于懒散无能。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04540a -->
<!-- USE:U04541 -->
## shifty /ˈʃɪfti/ adj.
`显得不诚实、说话行动躲躲闪闪的，属于非正式口语用法，含批评意味；比较级为 shiftier，最高级为 shiftiest。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04541a USE:U04541 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04541a USE:U04541 -->
He had a shifty face and previous convictions.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04541a -->
他面相显得躲闪不诚实，而且有犯罪前科。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04541a -->
<!-- USE:U04542 -->
## shiftily /ˈʃɪftəli/ adv.
`以躲闪、不坦率的方式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04542a USE:U04542 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04542a USE:U04542 -->
He looked away shiftily when asked about the receipt.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04542a -->
被问到收据时，他躲躲闪闪地移开了目光。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04542a -->
<!-- USE:U04543 -->
## shiftiness /ˈʃɪftinəs/ n.
`不坦率、躲闪诡诈的表现或性质。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04543a USE:U04543 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04543a USE:U04543 -->
Her questions exposed his shiftiness.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04543a -->
她的追问暴露了他的不坦率。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04543a -->
<!-- USE:U04544 -->
## red shift /ˈred ʃɪft/ n.
`光谱向更长波长方向的偏移，属于天文学用语；远离观测者的星体所发的光可出现红移，“红”不表示所有光都变成肉眼可见的红色。现代科普中也常连写为 redshift，两种拼写不改变其长波方向含义。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04544a USE:U04544 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"宇宙膨胀造成的红移","sourceUrls":["https://science.nasa.gov/mission/hubble/science/science-behind-the-discoveries/hubble-cosmological-redshift/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04544a USE:U04544 -->
As light travels through expanding space, it stretches to longer wavelengths, producing cosmological redshift.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04544a -->
光在不断膨胀的空间中传播时，波长被拉长，产生宇宙学红移。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04544a -->

<!-- WORD:W046 -->
# sustain

**词源**：经法语来自拉丁语 sustinere“从下面托住、支持、维持”，后半联系到 tenere“握住”。由托住、使不倒下，联系到维持、供养、承受和延长声音；sustenance 也由这一历史线索发展。

<!-- USE:U04601 -->
## sustain /səˈsteɪn/ v.
`让某种状态、活动或努力持续一段时间，及物；也可用 sustained 作分词形容词描述持续的状态。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04601a USE:U04601 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"海上补给维持行动","sourceUrls":["https://www.royalnavy.mod.uk/news/2024/october/22/20241022-tidesurge-supports-uk-and-nato-ops-in-north-atlantic"],"displayContext":"2024年10月报道所回顾的补给任务。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04601a USE:U04601 -->
Tidesurge delivered fuel so that Allied warships could sustain their operations in the North Atlantic.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04601a -->
“潮涌”号运送燃料，使盟军舰艇能够维持在北大西洋的行动。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04601a -->
<!-- USE:U04602 -->
## sustain /səˈsteɪn/ v.
`经历损伤、失败、损失等不利事情，及物，属于正式用语，如 sustain damage、sustain injuries、sustain a defeat。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04602a USE:U04602 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04602a USE:U04602 -->
Every aircraft in there has sustained some damage.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04602a -->
那里的每架飞机都受到了一些损坏。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04602a -->
<!-- USE:U04603 -->
## sustain /səˈsteɪn/ v.
`通过帮助、力量或鼓励支持某人继续下去，及物，属于正式用语，也可涉及经济支持。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04603a USE:U04603 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04603a USE:U04603 -->
The cash dividends they get from the cash crop would sustain them during the lean season.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04603a -->
他们从经济作物中得到的现金收益，可以支撑他们度过青黄不接的季节。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04603a -->
<!-- USE:U04604 -->
## sustain /səˈsteɪn/ v.
`支撑某物的重量，尤其长时间支撑，及物。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04604a USE:U04604 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04604a USE:U04604 -->
The beams sustain the weight of the roof.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04604a -->
这些梁支撑屋顶的重量。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04604a -->
<!-- USE:U04605 -->
## sustain /səˈsteɪn/ v.
`食物提供营养以维持人或其他生物的生命，及物。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04605a USE:U04605 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04605a USE:U04605 -->
The stored grain sustained the family through winter.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04605a -->
储存的粮食使这家人维持生命，度过冬天。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04605a -->
<!-- USE:U04606 -->
## sustain /səˈsteɪn/ v.
`忍受、经受并抵挡某种压力或处境，及物。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04606a USE:U04606 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04606a USE:U04606 -->
The structure sustained the pressure without collapsing.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04606a -->
这个结构承受住了压力，没有倒塌。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04606a -->
<!-- USE:U04607 -->
## sustain /səˈsteɪn/ v.
`法院等支持、认可某项异议或主张，及物，属于法律用法，如 sustain an objection。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04607a USE:U04607 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04607a USE:U04607 -->
The judge sustained the objection.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04607a -->
法官支持了这项异议。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04607a -->
<!-- USE:U04608 -->
## sustain /səˈsteɪn/ v.
`以证据支撑、确认某项陈述或指控，及物。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04608a USE:U04608 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04608a USE:U04608 -->
The documents sustain the allegation.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04608a -->
这些文件支持并证实了该项指控。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04608a -->
<!-- USE:U04609 -->
## sustain /səˈsteɪn/ v.
`持续而充分地扮演、表现某个角色，及物，用于表演等语境。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04609a USE:U04609 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04609a USE:U04609 -->
She sustained the character convincingly through the final act.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04609a -->
直到最后一幕，她都令人信服地维持着这个角色的表现。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04609a -->
<!-- USE:U04610 -->
## sustain /səˈsteɪn/ v.
`让声音持续不断，及物，常见于音乐语境。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04610a USE:U04610 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"松开琴键后延续声音","sourceUrls":["https://hub.yamaha.com/keyboards/k-how-to/how-to-use-keyboard-pedals/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04610a USE:U04610 -->
The sustain pedal lets you sustain notes after lifting your fingers from the keys.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04610a -->
延音踏板能让你在手指离开琴键后仍延续音符。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04610a -->
<!-- USE:U04611 -->
## sustain /səˈsteɪn/ n.
`使音符持续所形成的延音效果，尤其可由电子手段产生，属于音乐用法。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04611a USE:U04611 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04611a USE:U04611 -->
The pedal increases the sustain of the note.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04611a -->
这个踏板增强了音符的延音效果。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04611a -->
<!-- USE:U04612 -->
## sustainedly /səˈsteɪnɪdli/ adv.
`持续不断地。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04612a USE:U04612 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04612a USE:U04612 -->
The engine operated sustainedly throughout the test.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04612a -->
发动机在整个测试中持续运转。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04612a -->
<!-- USE:U04613 -->
## sustainer /səˈsteɪnər/ n.
`提供支撑、使某事物持续的人或事物，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04613a USE:U04613 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04613a USE:U04613 -->
The foundation became a sustainer of the research programme.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04613a -->
这个基金会成了支持研究项目持续开展的资助方。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04613a -->
<!-- USE:U04614 -->
## sustainment /səˈsteɪnmənt/ n.
`维持、支持某事物继续的行为或状态。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04614a USE:U04614 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04614a USE:U04614 -->
The plan provides for the sustainment of the equipment.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04614a -->
这份计划为设备的持续维持作了安排。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04614a -->
<!-- USE:U04615 -->
## sustainable /səˈsteɪnəbəl/ adj.
`使用自然资源时能够维持生态平衡、避免资源耗竭或环境损害的，属于生态用法，可描述资源来源或发展方式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04615a USE:U04615 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"温室修缮的环境目标","sourceUrls":["https://www.kew.org/kew-gardens/palm-house-renovation"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04615a USE:U04615 -->
The Palm House renovation aims to create a more sustainable glasshouse by replacing gas boilers with an electrified heat-pump system.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04615a -->
棕榈温室修缮计划通过用电气化热泵系统替代燃气锅炉，打造更可持续的温室。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04615a -->
<!-- USE:U04616 -->
## sustainable /səˈsteɪnəbəl/ adj.
`计划、方法或系统能按一定速度、水平继续的，也可表示能够承受、支撑的，不限于环境问题。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04616a USE:U04616 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"抗微生物药物耐药性的资金安排","sourceUrls":["https://www.who.int/news-room/fact-sheets/detail/antimicrobial-resistance"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04616a USE:U04616 -->
The WHO action plan calls for sustainable financing and coordinated action across sectors against antimicrobial resistance.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04616a -->
世卫组织行动计划呼吁为应对抗微生物药物耐药性提供可持续的资金，并开展跨部门协调行动。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04616a -->
<!-- USE:U04617 -->
## sustainably /səˈsteɪnəbli/ adv.
`以维持生态平衡、避免耗竭自然资源的方式，属于生态用法。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04617a USE:U04617 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04617a USE:U04617 -->
The forest is managed sustainably.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04617a -->
这片森林以生态可持续的方式管理。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04617a -->
<!-- USE:U04618 -->
## sustainability /səˌsteɪnəˈbɪləti/ n.
`在不耗竭资源、不破坏生态平衡的条件下继续的能力，属于生态用法。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04618a USE:U04618 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04618a USE:U04618 -->
The article examines the growing concern about environmental sustainability.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04618a -->
这篇文章考察人们对环境可持续性日益增长的关注。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04618a -->
<!-- USE:U04619 -->
## sustainability /səˌsteɪnəˈbɪləti/ n.
`活动、增长或安排能够继续维持的性质，不限于生态问题。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04619a USE:U04619 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04619a USE:U04619 -->
The review raises doubts about the sustainability of the current economic expansion.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04619a -->
这份评论质疑当前经济扩张能否持续。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04619a -->
<!-- USE:U04620 -->
## unsustainable /ˌʌnsəˈsteɪnəbəl/ adj.
`不能继续维持、支撑或承受的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04620a USE:U04620 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04620a USE:U04620 -->
The workload became unsustainable.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04620a -->
工作量变得无法继续承受。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04620a -->
<!-- USE:U04621 -->
## unsustainable /ˌʌnsəˈsteɪnəbəl/ adj.
`没有足够依据、不能继续支持或证实的，可描述论点或主张。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04621a USE:U04621 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04621a USE:U04621 -->
Without supporting evidence, the accusation was unsustainable.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04621a -->
没有支持证据，这项指控便无法成立。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04621a -->
<!-- USE:U04622 -->
## unsustainably /ˌʌnsəˈsteɪnəbli/ adv.
`以无法继续维持、承受或支持的方式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04622a USE:U04622 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04622a USE:U04622 -->
The project was consuming funds unsustainably.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04622a -->
这个项目正在以无法持续的速度消耗资金。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04622a -->
<!-- USE:U04623 -->
## sustenance /ˈsʌstənəns/ n.
`人、动物或植物维持生命和健康所需的食物、饮料或营养，通常不可数，属于正式用语。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04623a USE:U04623 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04623a USE:U04623 -->
The state provided a basic quantity of food for daily sustenance, but little else.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04623a -->
国家提供了维持日常生存的基本食物，但除此之外几乎没有别的。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04623a -->
<!-- USE:U04624 -->
## sustenance /ˈsʌstənəns/ n.
`给予营养、使生命得到维持的滋养过程。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04624a USE:U04624 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04624a USE:U04624 -->
The roots are essential to the sustenance of the plant.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04624a -->
根对植物获得滋养的过程至关重要。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04624a -->
<!-- USE:U04625 -->
## sustenance /ˈsʌstənəns/ n.
`支持生活、维持生存的手段，即生计。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04625a USE:U04625 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04625a USE:U04625 -->
Fishing provided their sustenance.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04625a -->
捕鱼为他们提供了生计。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04625a -->

<!-- WORD:W047 -->
# tangible

**词源**：经法语及拉丁语联系到 tangere“触摸”，字面线索是“能够碰到的”。由实体可触，发展出结果、证据明确可感的意义；in- 构成相应否定形式 intangible。

<!-- USE:U04701 -->
## tangible /ˈtændʒəbəl/ adj.
`可以通过触觉感知、具有实体的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04701a USE:U04701 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"有形设施相等也不足以平等","sourceUrls":["https://www.archives.gov/milestone-documents/brown-v-board-of-education"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04701a USE:U04701 -->
In Brown v. Board of Education (1954), the U.S. Supreme Court concluded that racially segregated public schools denied equal educational opportunity even with equal physical facilities and other tangible factors.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04701a -->
在1954年的布朗诉教育委员会案中，美国最高法院认定：即使实体设施和其他有形条件相等，实行种族隔离的公立学校仍会剥夺平等的教育机会。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04701a -->
<!-- USE:U04702 -->
## tangible /ˈtændʒəbəl/ adj.
`足够具体、明确，能够被观察或确实感受到的，可描述 evidence、proof、benefits 等，不要求能够用手触摸。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04702a USE:U04702 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04702a USE:U04702 -->
There should be some tangible evidence that the economy is starting to recover.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04702a -->
应当有一些明确可见的证据，表明经济开始复苏。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04702a -->
<!-- USE:U04703 -->
## tangibles /ˈtændʒəbəlz/ n.
`有实体的东西，尤其有形资产，通常用复数 tangibles。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04703a USE:U04703 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04703a USE:U04703 -->
The inventory lists tangibles such as equipment and furniture.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04703a -->
清单列出了设备、家具等有形资产。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04703a -->
<!-- USE:U04704 -->
## tangibility /ˌtændʒəˈbɪləti/ n.; tangibleness /ˈtændʒəbəlnəs/ n.
`有形可触或具体明确的性质。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04704a USE:U04704 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04704a USE:U04704 -->
The tangibility of the object matters to the sculptor.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04704a -->
这个物体可触可感的性质对雕塑家很重要。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04704a -->
<!-- USE:U04705 -->
## tangibly /ˈtændʒəbli/ adv.
`以具体、确实可感知的方式，可说明某种结果显现得明确。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04705a USE:U04705 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04705a USE:U04705 -->
This tangibly demonstrated that the world situation could be improved.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04705a -->
这具体证明了世界局势能够得到改善。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04705a -->
<!-- USE:U04706 -->
## intangible /ɪnˈtændʒəbəl/ adj.
`不能被触摸、不具有实体的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04706a USE:U04706 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04706a USE:U04706 -->
A legal right is intangible even when it has economic value.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04706a -->
法律权利即使具有经济价值，也不具有可触摸的实体。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04706a -->
<!-- USE:U04707 -->
## intangible /ɪnˈtændʒəbəl/ adj.
`抽象、难以明确界定、理解或衡量的，不表示没有价值。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04707a USE:U04707 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"判决考虑了难以量化的因素","sourceUrls":["https://www.archives.gov/milestone-documents/brown-v-board-of-education"],"displayContext":"1954年判决回顾先前有关大学教育待遇的案件。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04707a USE:U04707 -->
In Brown v. Board of Education (1954), the U.S. Supreme Court recalled McLaurin's university case and its intangible considerations, including his ability to discuss ideas and exchange views with other students.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04707a -->
在1954年的布朗诉教育委员会案中，美国最高法院回顾麦克劳林的大学教育案件及其中难以量化的考量，包括他与其他学生讨论思想、交换观点的能力。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04707a -->
<!-- USE:U04708 -->
## intangible /ɪnˈtændʒəbəl/ n.; intangibles /ɪnˈtændʒəbəlz/ n.
`难以精确测量、评估的东西或利益，常用复数 intangibles。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04708a USE:U04708 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04708a USE:U04708 -->
Women workers place more importance on intangibles such as a sense of achievement.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04708a -->
女性员工更重视成就感等难以量化的因素。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04708a -->
<!-- USE:U04709 -->
## intangibility /ɪnˌtændʒəˈbɪləti/ n.
`无形或难以明确把握的性质。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04709a USE:U04709 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04709a USE:U04709 -->
The intangibility of the experience makes it difficult to describe.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04709a -->
这种体验难以具体把握，因此很难描述。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04709a -->
<!-- USE:U04710 -->
## intangibly /ɪnˈtændʒəbli/ adv.
`以无形、难以具体把握的方式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04710a USE:U04710 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04710a USE:U04710 -->
The melody intangibly links the scene to an earlier memory.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04710a -->
这段旋律以一种难以言明的方式，把这个场景与先前的记忆联系起来。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04710a -->

<!-- WORD:W048 -->
# textile

**词源**：来自拉丁语 textilis“织成的”，联系到 texere“编织”。text 与 textile 历史上有共同的“织合”线索，词源关系可辅助记忆，但现代意义仍按布料、原料与产业区分。

<!-- USE:U04801 -->
## textile /ˈtekstaɪl/ n.
`织成或黏合而成的布料、织物，可数，常用复数 textiles。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04801a USE:U04801 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"蓝色防染织物","sourceUrls":["https://www.metmuseum.org/art/collection/search/13795"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04801a USE:U04801 -->
Blue-resist textiles show indigo-blue designs on a loosely woven white cotton ground.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04801a -->
蓝色防染织物在疏织的白色棉布底上呈现靛蓝图案。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04801a -->
<!-- USE:U04802 -->
## textiles /ˈtekstaɪlz/ n.
`制造布料的纺织产业，用复数形式 textiles，常不带限定词。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04802a USE:U04802 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04802a USE:U04802 -->
Another 75,000 jobs will be lost in textiles and clothing.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04802a -->
纺织和服装业还将失去七万五千个岗位。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04802a -->
<!-- USE:U04803 -->
## textile /ˈtekstaɪl/ n.
`用于织布的纤维、长丝或纱线，可数，指纺织原料，不一定是已织成的成品。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04803a USE:U04803 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04803a USE:U04803 -->
The catalogue lists silk yarn as a textile.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04803a -->
目录把丝纱列为一种纺织原料。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04803a -->
<!-- USE:U04804 -->
## textile /ˈtekstaɪl/ adj.
`与织物、编织或纺织业有关的，如 textile industry。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04804a USE:U04804 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"织物来源的研究","sourceUrls":["https://www.metmuseum.org/art/collection/search/13795"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04804a USE:U04804 -->
Determining where the blue-resist fabric was made frustrated several generations of American textile historians.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04804a -->
确定这种蓝色防染布的制作地点，曾令几代美国纺织史研究者感到棘手。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04804a -->
<!-- USE:U04805 -->
## textile /ˈtekstaɪl/ adj.
`经过编织形成的，如 textile fabrics。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04805a USE:U04805 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04805a USE:U04805 -->
The collection includes textile fabrics made from cotton.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04805a -->
这批收藏包括用棉制成的织物。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04805a -->
<!-- USE:U04806 -->
## textile /ˈtekstaɪl/ adj.
`适合用于织布的，如 textile materials。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04806a USE:U04806 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04806a USE:U04806 -->
These flexible fibres can serve as textile materials, unlike the brittle fibres we rejected.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04806a -->
这些柔韧的纤维可以用作纺织原料，而被我们剔除的脆性纤维不行。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04806a -->

<!-- WORD:W049 -->
# transmit

**词源**：来自拉丁语 transmittere“送到另一边”，trans- 表示跨越，mittere 表示送。记住“由一处传到另一处”，再看传的是数据、疾病、思想、能量还是机械动力；transmitted、transmitting 双写 t。

<!-- USE:U04901 -->
## transmit /trænzˈmɪt/ v.
`用电线、电波、卫星等把数据或电子信息送到另一处，可及物；对象明确时也可省略宾语，用 to 说明接收对象。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04901a USE:U04901 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"同轴电缆的用途","sourceUrls":["https://openstax.org/books/university-physics-volume-2/pages/8-1-capacitors-and-capacitance"],"displayContext":"OpenStax《University Physics Volume 2》缩编／节录，CC BY-NC-SA 4.0"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04901a USE:U04901 -->
A coaxial cable is commonly used to transmit time-varying electrical signals.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04901a -->
同轴电缆常用于传送随时间变化的电信号。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04901a -->
<!-- USE:U04902 -->
## transmit /trænzˈmɪt/ v.
`播送广播、电视节目，及物；be transmitted live 表示直播。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04902a USE:U04902 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04902a USE:U04902 -->
The game was transmitted live in Spain and Italy.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04902a -->
这场比赛在西班牙和意大利现场直播。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04902a -->
<!-- USE:U04903 -->
## transmit /trænzˈmɪt/ v.
`使疾病或感染由一个人、动物等传播给另一个个体。及物，属于较正式的用语，常说 transmit a disease to somebody；传播风险取决于具体感染与过程，某一情境的判断不能推广为普遍零风险。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04903a USE:U04903 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04903a USE:U04903 -->
The report describes mosquitoes that transmit disease to humans.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04903a -->
这份报告描述了把疾病传给人类的蚊子。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04903a -->
<!-- USE:U04904 -->
## transmit /trænzˈmɪt/ v.
`使他人理解、感受到某种想法或情绪，属于文学用法；也可由承载思想、情感的信息或作品本身作主语。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04904a USE:U04904 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04904a USE:U04904 -->
He transmitted his keen enjoyment of singing to the audience.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04904a -->
他把自己对歌唱的强烈喜爱传递给了听众。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04904a -->
<!-- USE:U04905 -->
## transmit /trænzˈmɪt/ v.
`让热、光、声、电等通过，充当介质。及物，主语常是材料或物体。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04905a USE:U04905 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"玻璃作品与透光","sourceUrls":["https://www.getty.edu/news/artist-dialogues-getty-conservation-lita-albuquerque-malibu-line/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04905a USE:U04905 -->
By depositing a thin metallic layer on plate glass, artists altered how it absorbed, reflected, and transmitted light.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04905a -->
艺术家通过在平板玻璃上沉积一层薄金属，改变了玻璃吸收、反射和透过光线的方式。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04905a -->
<!-- USE:U04906 -->
## transmit /trænzˈmɪt/ v.
`把某物交送、传递给另一方，及物，不限定电子方式。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04906a USE:U04906 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04906a USE:U04906 -->
They transmitted the message.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04906a -->
他们传递了这条消息。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04906a -->
<!-- USE:U04907 -->
## transmissible /trænzˈmɪsəbəl/ adj.; transmittable /trænzˈmɪtəbəl/ adj.
`能够被传送、传播或传递的，具体对象和传播方式由语境确定。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04907a USE:U04907 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04907a USE:U04907 -->
The data are transmittable over the existing link.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04907a -->
这些数据可以通过现有链路传送。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04907a -->
<!-- USE:U04908 -->
## transmissive /trænzˈmɪsɪv/ adj.
`能够让光、热、声等透过或传递的，说明材料、系统的传输特性。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04908a USE:U04908 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04908a USE:U04908 -->
The layer is highly transmissive to visible light.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04908a -->
这一层材料对可见光具有很高的透过性。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04908a -->
<!-- USE:U04909 -->
## transmittal /trænzˈmɪtəl/ n.
`把某物传送、交递出去的行为。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04909a USE:U04909 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"髓鞘受损与信息传递","sourceUrls":["https://openstax.org/books/psychology-2e/pages/3-2-cells-of-the-nervous-system"],"displayContext":"OpenStax《Psychology 2e》缩编／节录，CC BY-NC-SA 4.0"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04909a USE:U04909 -->
Loss of the insulating myelin sheath interferes with electrical signalling, preventing the quick transmittal of information by neurons.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04909a -->
绝缘性髓鞘的丧失会干扰电信号，使神经元无法快速传递信息。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04909a -->
<!-- USE:U04910 -->
## transmission /trænzˈmɪʃən/ n.
`信息、疾病、知识等从一方传到另一方的过程或实例，可数或不可数，常接 of。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04910a USE:U04910 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"激光与光纤通信","sourceUrls":["https://www.nist.gov/history/nists-role-laser-measurements-and-applications/what-laser"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04910a USE:U04910 -->
Lasers enable the global transmission of telephone conversations and data through optical fibres.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04910a -->
激光使电话通话与数据能够通过光纤在全球传输。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04910a -->
<!-- USE:U04911 -->
## transmission /trænzˈmɪʃən/ n.
`播送广播、电视节目的行为，通常不可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04911a USE:U04911 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04911a USE:U04911 -->
Transmission of the programme began at noon.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04911a -->
节目的播出于中午开始。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04911a -->
<!-- USE:U04912 -->
## transmission /trænzˈmɪʃən/ n.
`一次播出的节目，可数，与播送行为区分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04912a USE:U04912 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04912a USE:U04912 -->
The archive preserves a recording of the original transmission.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04912a -->
档案保存了原始广播节目的录音。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04912a -->
<!-- USE:U04913 -->
## transmission /trænzˈmɪʃən/ n.
`把发动机动力传到车轴、车轮的齿轮和传动轴等系统，可数或不可数；automatic transmission、manual transmission 分别指自动、手动变速传动系统。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04913a USE:U04913 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"手动与自动传动系统","sourceUrls":["https://www.toyota.com/car-tips/types-of-transmissions/"]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04913a USE:U04913 -->
In an automatic transmission, an on-board computer controls gear selection and clutch operation instead of the driver.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04913a -->
在自动传动系统中，车载电脑代替驾驶者控制档位选择和离合器操作。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04913a -->
<!-- USE:U04914 -->
## transmitter /trænzˈmɪtər/ n.
`传递某物的人或事物，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04914a USE:U04914 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04914a USE:U04914 -->
A teacher can be a transmitter of knowledge.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04914a -->
教师可以是知识的传递者。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04914a -->
<!-- USE:U04915 -->
## transmitter /trænzˈmɪtər/ n.
`产生并发出承载信息的电磁波的设备，可数，尤指广播、电视发射机或发射台。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04915a USE:U04915 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04915a USE:U04915 -->
The transmitter sends a radio signal to the receiver.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04915a -->
发射机向接收器发送无线电信号。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04915a -->
<!-- USE:U04916 -->
## transmitter /trænzˈmɪtər/ n.; neurotransmitter /ˌnʊroʊtrænzˈmɪtər/ n.
`由神经纤维释放、使神经信号传到另一神经或肌肉的化学物质，即神经递质。属于生物化学用法，此处 transmitter 与 neurotransmitter 同义。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU04916a USE:U04916 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"神经递质跨越突触间隙","sourceUrls":["https://openstax.org/books/psychology-2e/pages/3-2-cells-of-the-nervous-system"],"displayContext":"OpenStax《Psychology 2e》缩编／节录，CC BY-NC-SA 4.0"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU04916a USE:U04916 -->
Released into the synaptic cleft, neurotransmitters cross the gap and bind to matching receptors on a neighbouring neuron.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU04916a -->
神经递质被释放到突触间隙后，会跨过间隙，与相邻神经元上相匹配的受体结合。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU04916a -->

<!-- WORD:W050 -->
# yield

**词源**：来自古英语表示支付、回报、补偿的词。由“交出、给出”可联想到让步、交出控制与产出回报，但具体句法和对象仍需区分。

<!-- USE:U05001 -->
## yield /jild/ v.
`停止抵抗、服从压力，或接受劝说而改变行动。属于正式用语，不及物，常接 to，也可单用 yield。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU05001a USE:U05001 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU05001a USE:U05001 -->
Will she yield to growing pressure for her to retire?
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU05001a -->
要求她退休的压力越来越大，她会屈服吗？
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU05001a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU05001b USE:U05001 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU05001b USE:U05001 -->
She yielded to persuasion.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU05001b -->
她听从了劝告。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU05001b -->
<!-- USE:U05002 -->
## yield /jild/ v.
`把自己控制、负责的事物交给别人，或投降、放弃。属于正式用语，及物，如 yield control、yield a fortress；yield oneself a prisoner 表示束手就擒。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU05002a USE:U05002 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU05002a USE:U05002 -->
He may yield control.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU05002a -->
他可能会交出控制权。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU05002a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU05002b USE:U05002 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU05002b USE:U05002 -->
They yielded themselves prisoners.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU05002b -->
他们束手就擒。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU05002b -->
<!-- USE:U05003 -->
## yield /jild/ v.
`减速或停下，让有优先权的人或车辆先通过。不及物，美式英语常说 yield to somebody，英式通常说 give way。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU05003a USE:U05003 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"专用右转车道仍须让行人","sourceUrls":["https://www.dmv.ca.gov/portal/handbook/california-driver-handbook/navigating-the-roads/"],"displayContext":"加州驾驶手册中的专用右转车道情境。"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU05003a USE:U05003 -->
Even in a dedicated right-turn lane, always yield to pedestrians in a crosswalk when turning.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU05003a -->
即使在专用右转车道，转弯时也始终要让人行横道上的行人先行。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU05003a -->
<!-- USE:U05004 -->
## yield /jild/ v.
`物体受压力后弯曲、移位或让开，不及物，如门被推开、地面变软下陷。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU05004a USE:U05004 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU05004a USE:U05004 -->
The door yielded easily when he pushed it.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU05004a -->
他一推，门就轻易让开了。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU05004a -->
<!-- USE:U05005 -->
## yield /jild/ v.
`土地、动物等产出一定数量的作物或食物，及物；也可不带宾语来说明产出状况。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU05005a USE:U05005 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU05005a USE:U05005 -->
The land yields crops.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU05005a -->
这块土地产出农作物。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU05005a -->

<!-- CONTEXT:CU05005b USE:U05005 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU05005b USE:U05005 -->
The land yields poorly.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU05005b -->
这块土地产量很低。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU05005b -->
<!-- USE:U05006 -->
## yield /jild/ n.
`土地、动物等所产出的食物数量，可数，如 crop yield、grain yield、milk yield；good/poor yield 表示产量好或差，boost/increase yields 表示提高产量。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU05006a USE:U05006 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU05006a USE:U05006 -->
Polluted water lessens crop yields.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU05006a -->
受污染的水会降低作物产量。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU05006a -->
<!-- USE:U05007 -->
## yield /jild/ v.
`税收、投资等带来一定金额的收入或利润，及物，属于商业用法。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU05007a USE:U05007 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU05007a USE:U05007 -->
In this hypothetical calculation, the investment yields 15%.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU05007a -->
在这一假设计算中，这项投资的收益率为15%。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU05007a -->
<!-- USE:U05008 -->
## yield /jild/ n.
`投资产生的收入、利润或相应收益水平，可数，属于商业用语，常接 on。可谈 bond/dividend/gilt yield；attractive、high、low 描述收益是否有吸引力或高低，average、gross、net、prospective 分别说明平均、总额、净额或预期收益。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU05008a USE:U05008 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU05008a USE:U05008 -->
The high yields available on the dividend shares made them attractive to private investors.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU05008a -->
这些派息股票可带来的高收益，使它们受到私人投资者青睐。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU05008a -->
<!-- USE:U05009 -->
## yield /jild/ v.
`提供结果、信息或成果，及物，不限于金钱或农产品。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU05009a USE:U05009 KIND:abridgment -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"矩形近似得到的面积","sourceUrls":["https://openstax.org/books/calculus-volume-1/pages/5-1-approximating-areas"],"displayContext":"该书以f(x)=(x−1)³+4、区间[0,2]演示的具体近似；不是一般公式。 OpenStax《Calculus Volume 1》缩编／节录，CC BY-NC-SA 4.0"} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU05009a USE:U05009 -->
For f(x) = (x − 1)³ + 4 on [0, 2], a right-endpoint approximation using four equal-width rectangles yields an area estimate of 8.5.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU05009a -->
对于区间[0, 2]上的函数f(x) = (x − 1)³ + 4，用四个等宽矩形作右端点近似，会得到8.5的面积估计值。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU05009a -->
<!-- USE:U05010 -->
## yield /jild/ v.
`承认自己在某方面不如别人，不及物，常接 to；I yield to none in... 表示在某方面不比任何人差。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU05010a USE:U05010 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU05010a USE:U05010 -->
I yield to none in understanding the problem.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU05010a -->
在理解这个问题方面，我不比任何人差。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU05010a -->
<!-- USE:U05011 -->
## yield /jild/ v.
`在辩论等场合允许另一人接着发言，即让出发言权，不及物，属于美式用法。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU05011a USE:U05011 KIND:excerpt -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"把一分钟交给下一位议员","sourceUrls":["https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/CREC-2025-06-12/html/CREC-2025-06-12-pt1-PgH2796-3.htm"],"displayContext":"2025年6月12日美国众议院记录，相邻两轮；省去两轮开头的Mr. Speaker。","speakers":{"SCU05011a":"Mrs. McClain","SCU05011a_1":"Mr. Bean of Florida"}} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU05011a USE:U05011 -->
I yield 1 minute to the gentleman from Florida (Mr. Bean).
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU05011a_1 -->
I thank Chair McClain for the time.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU05011a -->
麦克莱恩：“我把一分钟发言时间让给佛罗里达州的比恩先生。”比恩：“感谢麦克莱恩主席给我发言时间。”
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU05011a -->
<!-- USE:U05012 -->
## yielding /ˈjildɪŋ/ adj.
`受到压力会移动、弯曲而非僵硬不动的，可描述表面、物体或材料。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU05012a USE:U05012 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU05012a USE:U05012 -->
Their boots sank into the yielding ground.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU05012a -->
他们的靴子陷进了松软的地面。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU05012a -->
<!-- USE:U05013 -->
## yielding /ˈjildɪŋ/ adj.
`愿意服从、让步的。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU05013a USE:U05013 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU05013a USE:U05013 -->
He was patient and yielding in the discussion.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU05013a -->
他在讨论中很耐心，也愿意让步。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU05013a -->
<!-- USE:U05014 -->
## yieldingly /ˈjildɪŋli/ adv.
`以顺从、让步的方式，用于人的应答和行为。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU05014a USE:U05014 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU05014a USE:U05014 -->
She answered yieldingly and accepted the change.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU05014a -->
她顺从地回答，接受了这一改变。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU05014a -->
<!-- USE:U05014M -->
## yieldingly /ˈjildɪŋli/ adv.
`受压力时以柔顺、容易弯曲的方式，用于物体或材料。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU05014Ma USE:U05014M KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU05014Ma USE:U05014M -->
The soft cushion compressed yieldingly beneath her weight.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU05014Ma -->
柔软的坐垫在她的重量下柔顺地压缩了。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU05014Ma -->
<!-- USE:U05015 -->
## yieldingness /ˈjildɪŋnəs/ n.
`材料柔软可弯、受压力会让开的性质。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU05015a USE:U05015 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU05015a USE:U05015 -->
The material's yieldingness makes it suitable for a cushion.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU05015a -->
这种材料柔软可弯的性质，使它适合做坐垫。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU05015a -->
<!-- USE:U05015P -->
## yieldingness /ˈjildɪŋnəs/ n.
`人顺从、愿意让步的性质，与材料柔软可弯的意义区分。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU05015Pa USE:U05015P KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU05015Pa USE:U05015P -->
He mistook her patience for yieldingness, but she would not change her decision.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU05015Pa -->
他把她的耐心误当成顺从，但她并不愿改变决定。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU05015Pa -->
<!-- USE:U05016 -->
## yielder /ˈjildər/ n.
`产出农产品、成果等的人或事物，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU05016a USE:U05016 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU05016a USE:U05016 -->
This variety is a reliable yielder.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU05016a -->
这个品种产出稳定。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU05016a -->
<!-- USE:U05017 -->
## yielder /ˈjildər/ n.
`能产生投资收益或回报的事物，可数，属于财务用法，不是领取收益的人。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU05017a USE:U05017 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU05017a USE:U05017 -->
This bond is a modest yielder.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU05017a -->
这只债券能产生不高的收益。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU05017a -->
<!-- USE:U05018 -->
## yielder /ˈjildər/ n.
`停止抵抗、服从或作出让步的人，可数。`

<!-- CONTEXT:CU05018a USE:U05018 KIND:example -->
<!-- CONTEXT_META:{"title":"","sourceUrls":[]} -->
<!-- SENTENCE:SCU05018a USE:U05018 -->
In the negotiation he became the first yielder.
<!-- TRANSLATION:CU05018a -->
在这次谈判中，他成了第一个让步的人。
<!-- END_CONTEXT:CU05018a -->
